*Ioanna Gemou,Matteo Gamba,Randall Balestriero,Ritambhara Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，即使预测性能相似，不同模型的反事实解释行为也存在显著差异，这种差异主要源于决策边界位置与数据支持区域的相互作用。仅改变分类器头部就能在保持准确率不变的情况下改变反事实行为，表明反事实特性是模型评估的一个独立维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实解释在机器学习系统的解释和审计中广泛应用。然而，在预训练编码器和下游分类器组成的现代多模态系统中，具有相似预测性能的模型在反事实解释的可行性和距离上可能存在显著差异。现有研究缺乏对这一变化的系统分析，因此需要理解这种差异的来源及其对模型选择和可靠性的影响。

Method: 采用标准化的局部搜索探测方法，跨多个预训练编码器和线性分类器头部进行实验。通过固定表示空间、仅改变分类器头部的方式，隔离分析分类器对反事实行为的影响。同时分析决策边界邻近度与局部数据支持区域的相互作用，以解释反事实行为差异的成因。

Result: 实验表明，尽管预测性能相似，不同模型的反事实行为差异显著。在固定表示的情况下，仅改变分类器头部就能改变反事实结果，而预测性能基本保持不变。这种差异可以通过决策边界邻近度和局部数据支持区域的相互作用来解释——两者共同决定预测变化是否可行且位于数据支持区域内。这一发现还可用于改进固定模型内的反事实搜索。

Conclusion: 反事实行为是独立于预测性能之外的一个独特维度，可以在不改变准确率的情况下进行调整。这一发现对模型选择、鲁棒性评估以及反事实方法的可靠性具有重要意义，建议在模型评估中应将反事实特性作为独立的考量因素。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations seek small, semantically meaningful changes to an input that alter a model's prediction, and are widely used to interpret and audit machine learning systems. In modern vision, language, and multimodal systems, pretrained encoders map inputs to representation spaces, and downstream classifier heads impose decision boundaries within those spaces. As a result, the feasibility and distance of nearby counterfactuals depend on boundary placement relative to the data. Yet models with similar predictive performance can differ substantially in whether such changes are achievable and how far representations must move. This work examines this variation using a standardized local search probe across several pretrained encoders and linear classifier heads. Results show that despite similar predictive performance, models differ substantially in their counterfactual behavior. Under fixed representations, varying only the classifier head alters counterfactual outcomes while leaving predictive performance largely unchanged. This variation is explained by the interaction of decision-boundary proximity and local data support, which jointly determine whether prediction changes are both feasible and lie in regions supported by the data, and can also improve counterfactual search within fixed models. Together, these findings identify counterfactual behavior as a distinct dimension beyond predictive performance and show that it can be altered without changing accuracy, with implications for model selection, robustness, and the reliability of counterfactual methods.

</details>


### [38] [Edge of Stability Selectively Shapes Learning Across the Data Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04212)
*Shauna Kwag,Anakha Ganesh,Tomaso Poggio,Pierfrancesco Beneventano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了边缘稳定性的选择性机制：它通过稳定性约束重新分配学习资源，优先提升梯度方向与顶层Hessian特征向量对齐且梯度幅度不消失的数据组，从而在训练分布上实现有选择性的学习进度分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将边缘稳定性视为优化的全局属性，本文旨在揭示其选择性特征，即稳定性约束如何在训练分布的不同子集之间重新分配学习进度，从而理解边缘稳定性作为学习分配机制的深层作用。

Method: 通过分支干预实验（从相同训练状态进入或退出边缘稳定区域）进行因果验证；设计受控扰动实验（保持距离但随机化方向）分离梯度对齐机制；分析交叉熵损失下的梯度饱和现象，研究梯度幅度持续性的影响。

Result: 因果证明了边缘稳定性的选择性学习权衡；发现数据组受益的两个必要条件：1）聚合梯度与顶层Hessian特征向量对齐；2）持续保持非零梯度幅度；交叉熵损失下，梯度饱和使高置信度分类组解耦，优势转向梯度持续的输出异常值组。

Conclusion: 边缘稳定性不仅是稳定性边界，更是调控数据分布上学习资源分配的关键机制，为理解神经网络训练动态和优化行为提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Existing analyses of the edge of stability (EoS) treat it as a global property of optimization. We show that it is also selective: the stability constraint redistributes learning across subsets of the training distribution, amplifying progress on some groups while suppressing progress on others. Using a branching intervention that enters or exits the EoS regime from the same training state, we causally demonstrate this trade-off and identify two necessary conditions for a group to benefit. First, its aggregate gradient must align with the top Hessian eigenvector. We isolate this mechanism with a controlled perturbation that preserves distance but randomizes direction, destroying alignment and eliminating the advantage. Second, the group must sustain non-vanishing gradient magnitude over time. Under cross-entropy loss, gradient saturation decouples confidently classified groups, shifting the advantage to output-outliers, whose gradients persist. Together, these results show that EoS functions not only as a stability boundary, but as a mechanism governing the allocation of learning across the data distribution.

</details>


### [39] [Recover-LoRA for Aggressive Quantization: Reclaiming Accuracy in 2-Bit Language Models via Low-Rank Adaptation with Knowledge Distillation on Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04238)
*Devleena Das,Rajeev Patwari,Elliott Delaye,Ashish Sirasao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了针对大语言模型2-bit超低比特量化的混合精度策略与恢复方法。通过仅对MLP的gate和up投影层进行2-bit量化，结合Recover-LoRA方法进行精度恢复，在保持7.5-23.3%吞吐量提升的同时，实现了80-95%的精度恢复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的2-bit激进权重量化虽然能显著提升推理吞吐量和内存效率，特别适用于边缘和端侧部署场景，但通常会导致严重的精度下降。现有方法在超低比特量化场景下缺乏有效的精度恢复手段，因此需要开发一种轻量级、无需真实数据的恢复方法来解决这一问题。

Method: 1. **选择性混合精度策略**：提出W4/W2-GateUp配置，仅对MLP的gate和up投影层进行2-bit量化，其他线性层保持较高精度（4-bit）。
2. **Recover-LoRA扩展应用**：将原本用于模型权重损坏恢复的Recover-LoRA方法扩展到超低比特量化场景。
3. **基于合成数据的低秩适配训练**：通过logit蒸馏在量化层上训练低秩适配器，使用合成数据进行训练，无需标注数据。

Result: 1. **性能提升**：在三个模型家族（4B-20B）和两个硬件平台上，W4/W2-GateUp部署相比均匀W4量化实现了7.5-23.3%的TPS提升。
2. **精度恢复**：在Qwen3-4B案例研究中，Recover-LoRA在12个基准测试中的9个上实现了80-95%的精度恢复，仅使用10k合成训练样本。
3. **数据有效性**：合成数据在基于蒸馏的恢复中表现与精选标注数据相当，且恢复能力可泛化到分布外评估任务。

Conclusion: Recover-LoRA被证明是一种实用的后量化精度恢复工具，适用于部署场景中的激进权重压缩。该方法通过选择性混合精度策略和基于合成数据的低秩适配训练，在显著提升推理效率的同时有效恢复了因2-bit量化损失的精度，为大语言模型的边缘部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Aggressive weight quantization to 2-bit precision offers substantial throughput and memory gains for large language model (LLM) inference, but typically incurs severe accuracy degradation. These gains are particularly relevant for edge and on-device deployment, where memory capacity and bandwidth are primary constraints. In this work, we extend Recover-LoRA -- a lightweight, data-free accuracy recovery method originally developed for general model weight corruption -- to the setting of ultra-low-bit quantization. We propose a selective mixed-precision strategy in which only gate and up projection layers of the MLP are quantized to 2-bit (W2), while all other linear layers remain at higher precision, yielding a mixed-precision GateUp configuration. We demonstrate via roofline analysis across three model families (4B--20B) and two hardware platforms that a W4/W2-GateUp deployment (4-bit base with 2-bit gate/up) delivers 7.5--23.3\% TPS improvement over uniform W4 depending on model and context length, while confining quantization error to a predictable subset of layers. We then apply Recover-LoRA -- training low-rank adapters on the quantized layers via logit distillation with synthetic data -- to recover accuracy lost from 2-bit quantization of the gate and up layers. In a case study on Qwen3-4B, Recover-LoRA achieves 80--95\% accuracy recovery on 9 of 12 benchmarks, using only 10k synthetic training samples and no labeled data. We further demonstrate that synthetic data performs comparably to curated labeled data for distillation-based recovery, and that recovery generalizes to out-of-distribution evaluation tasks. Our results present Recover-LoRA as a practical post-quantization accuracy recovery tool for aggressive weight compression in deployment settings.

</details>


### [40] [RL Excursions during Pre-Training: Re-examining Policy Optimization for LLM training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04272)
*Rachit Bansal,Clara Mohri,Tian Qin,David Alvarez-Melis,Sham Kakade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现强化学习（RL）在预训练早期即可有效应用，且通过数据组成优化比模型规模更能提升RL效果。RL直接应用于基础模型可扩展分布，而SFT会削弱通用能力。并行平均融合RL和SFT目标的方法在各项指标上表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM训练流程将强化学习（RL）仅用于预训练和监督微调（SFT）之后。作者质疑这一常规做法，探索在预训练中间检查点直接应用RL、SFT及SFT后RL的效果，以期优化训练流程。

Method: 从零开始训练LLM，在预训练中间检查点直接应用RL、SFT及SFT→RL流程。通过调整预训练数据组成、对比模型规模影响，分析RL效果。采用并行平均方法融合RL和SFT目标，评估模型推理准确率、分布特性和通用能力。

Result: RL在预训练早期即有效，效果可匹配完整SFT→RL流程。目标预训练数据组成对RL效果影响显著，优于模型规模。RL直接应用于基础模型可扩展分布，SFT后RL则会产生锐化效应。RL基本不改变模型通用能力，而SFT会导致能力退化。并行平均融合方法在所有指标上均优于其他训练方法，且保留了通用能力。

Conclusion: 研究表明LLM训练可扩大RL的应用范围，早期引入RL并通过数据组成优化及目标融合方法，能提升推理能力并保持通用能力，为训练流程优化提供了新方向。

Abstract: The standard LLM training pipeline applies reinforcement learning (RL) only after pre-training and supervised fine-tuning (SFT). We question this status quo by training a LLM from scratch and applying RL, SFT, and SFT followed by RL directly to intermediate pre-training checkpoints. We find that RL is effective very early, and often matches the full SFT$\to$RL pipeline early as well. Through experiments on harder problems, we find that targeted pre-training data composition is a strong lever for RL effectiveness, even more so than model scale. Beyond reasoning accuracy, applying RL directly to base checkpoints expands the model's distribution; the sharpening effect reported in recent work arises only when RL follows SFT. The general capabilities of the model remain essentially unchanged by RL, while they degrade following SFT. Finally, we merge RL and SFT objectives by parallel averaging, which outperforms across all other training methods discussed, across metrics, while preserving general capabilities. Together, these results suggest that LLM training might benefit from an expanded use of RL.

</details>


### [41] [From Ticks to Flows: Dynamics of Neural Reinforcement Learning in Continuous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04275)
*Saket Tiwari,Tejas Kotwal,George Konidaris*

Main category: cs.LG

Result: 首次在连续强化学习中推导出了描述每一步梯度更新时状态分布无穷小变化的方程，刻画了时间依赖的随机变量（环境状态和累积折扣回报估计）在双层网络无限宽极限下沿梯度步长的演化规律，并通过玩具连续控制任务验证了理论结果。

Abstract: We present a novel theoretical framework for deep reinforcement learning (RL) in continuous environments by modeling the problem as a continuous-time stochastic process, drawing on insights from stochastic control. Building on previous work, we introduce a viable model of actor-critic algorithm that incorporates both exploration and stochastic transitions. For single-hidden-layer neural networks, we show that the state of the environment can be formulated as a two time scale process: the environment time and the gradient time. Within this formulation, we characterize how the time-dependent random variables that represent the environment's state and estimate of the cumulative discounted return evolve over gradient steps in the infinite width limit of two-layer networks. Using the theory of stochastic differential equations, we derive, for the first time in continuous RL, an equation describing the infinitesimal change in the state distribution at each gradient step, under a vanishingly small learning rate. Overall, our work provides a novel nonparametric formulation for studying overparametrized neural actor-critic algorithms. We empirically corroborate our theoretical result using a toy continuous control task.

</details>

Abstract: Machine-learned (ML) exchange-correlation (XC) functionals aim to replace human-designed density functional approximations by learning directly from reference data, but they still do not consistently outperform traditional $\mathcal{O}(N^4)$-scaling hybrid functionals. We study a hybrid-distillation setting in which $\mathcal{O}(N^3)$-scaling ML-XC functionals are trained to reproduce B3LYP/def2-SVP targets. We introduce Derivative Informed XC-Loss (DI-Loss), a loss that incorporates additional information from the reference hybrid functional by supervising first and second derivatives of the energy on the Grassmannian of admissible density matrices. Rather than only matching the self-consistent fixed point, DI-Loss aligns the local first- and second-order response of the learned functional with that of the target functional. Across four evaluated architectures, DI-Loss consistently improves the main energy metrics. Averaged uniformly across architectures, the total-energy MAE decreases by 66% relative to energy and density supervision alone. The density-sensitive mean-field energy metric $E_ρ$ improves from $1.2$ to $0.8$ mEh on average, while dipole and $\mathcal{L}_2$ density errors do not improve uniformly. We further show that densities from the distilled functionals reduce hybrid-functional SCF iterations by up to 50%. In downstream TDDFT calculations, Hessian supervision improves excited-state predictions, with XCdiff reducing the mean excitation-energy MAE by 19 - 35%.

Abstract: Contrastive learning has become a leading paradigm for self-supervised representation learning, yet the conditions under which it recovers meaningful latent geometry remain incompletely understood. We develop a measure-theoretic framework formalizing the diversity condition, a support requirement on positive-pair sampling that is necessary for isometric latent recovery. We show that the standard full-support von Mises-Fisher setting implies the satisfaction of the diversity condition and as a consequence global contrastive loss minimizers recover latent geometry up to orthogonal transformation, while restricted conditionals can make non-orthogonal maps attain strictly lower asymptotic contrastive loss. We introduce a support-corrected Information Noise Contrastive Estimation (InfoNCE) variant as a theoretical fix: this correction makes orthogonal latent space recovery achievable but does not uniquely select it. Experiments on synthetic benchmarks validate the identifiability predictions, and CIFAR-10 experiments are consistent with the qualitative prediction that architectural inductive bias becomes more important when sampling diversity is limited. Together, our results clarify how sampling mechanisms and encoder inductive bias interact in contrastive representation learning.

Abstract: Preference modeling plays a central role in reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF), enabling large language models (LLMs) to align with human values. However, most existing approaches assume a universal reward function, neglecting the diversity and heterogeneity of human preferences. To address this limitation without additional annotation costs, recent work has proposed learning multiple preference components from binary data and combining them to model individual preferences. Nevertheless, these components often fail to capture coherent and disentangled patterns, limiting their interpretability and effectiveness for personalization. In this work, we propose a sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) reward model that encourages sparse routing and expert diversity during training on binary preference data. Across controlled and real-world experiments, sparse MoE learns interpretable routing patterns and specialized experts. It also improves test-time personalization, and post-adaptation shifts in expert weights provide a qualitative lens for analyzing how the model adapts to personalized preferences.

</details>

Abstract: Generating realistic and diverse graphs is a key problem in machine learning, with applications in molecular discovery, circuit design, cybersecurity, and beyond. However, current graph generative models remain limited by scalability and novelty. Diffusion-based methods often require costly full-adjacency operations and long denoising chains, while many autoregressive and hybrid models have at least quadratic complexity. In addition, these models often imitate training graphs rather than generalize beyond them.
  We propose a lightweight autoregressive framework to address these issues. It uses a structure-guided topological ordering to serialize graphs into regular edge sequences, enabling near log-linear generation, and a two-phase training strategy that combines exploration-oriented augmentation with iterative refinement to reduce overfitting and promote controlled novelty.
  Experiments on molecular and non-molecular benchmarks show that our approach improves novelty while preserving high validity and uniqueness. The framework also supports both LSTM and Mamba-style causal sequence backbones, with large-memory accelerators enabling longer graph-sequence experiments beyond typical GPU limits.

</details>

Method: 提出物理编码模块化混合层（PE-MHL）框架，以基准物理模型为基础，通过增量添加新子模型进行逐步细化，每个新组件在保留已学习内容的同时增加复杂度，并采用最小二乘法初始化新子模型。

Conclusion: PE-MHL框架通过模块化增量构建方式有效解决了混合模型的复杂度控制问题，在保持物理一致性的同时实现了优异的性能表现，为控制应用中的混合建模提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Hybrid models that combine physics-based and data-driven components have shown strong potential for achieving accuracy and interpretability in control applications. While recent methods have made progress in incorporating physical consistency, challenges remain in scalability, robustness to noise, and control of model complexity. This paper proposes a Physics-Encoded Modular Hybrid Layer (PE-MHL) framework, in which a baseline physics-based model is incrementally refined through the addition of new sub-models, where each new component adds complexity while preserving what previous components have already learned. We establish a theoretical guarantee for this construction: with a least-squares initialization of each new sub-model, the training error is monotonically non-increasing in the number of sub-models and provably converges. Empirical evaluations on a nonlinear NARX benchmark and the Quanser Aero 2 platform demonstrate that PE-MHL outperforms equivalently sized monolithic networks in both accuracy and generalization, while also providing more stable training dynamics and better preservation of underlying data structures.

</details>

Method: 提出一种线性赌博机算法来平衡离线与在线的权衡：在早期轮次依赖离线数据，随着时间视界的增长逐步偏向探索。算法设计使得离线数据利用与在线探索动态调整。

Result: 建立了遗憾界，证明该方法同时对纯在线和纯离线解决方案具有竞争力。具体而言，相对于最优动作的遗憾关于在线交互次数呈次线性；相对于离线参考策略的遗憾随着离线样本数量的增加而递减。实验结果进一步验证了该方法在不同问题参数下的有效性。

Abstract: We study online learning with an additional offline dataset in the stochastic linear bandit setting. Although this problem arises frequently in practice, the offline-to-online tradeoff remains poorly understood in structured environments. We propose a linear bandit algorithm that balances this tradeoff: it relies on offline data during early rounds, and increasingly favors exploration as the horizon grows. We establish regret bounds showing that our method is simultaneously competitive with both purely online and purely offline solutions. In particular, it achieves sublinear regret relative to the optimal action in the number of online interactions, while its regret relative to an offline reference decreases as the number of offline samples grows. Empirical results further demonstrate its effectiveness across various problem parameters.

</details>

*Jun Hu*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有有限对称性的贝叶斯模型（如成分可交换的混合模型、具有紧密间隔模态的结构识别）会产生在标签置换群下不变的后验分布，造成冗余多模态性，这会降低MCMC收敛诊断的有效性和准确性。

Method: 引入Folded Transport MCMC (FolT-MCMC)，通过对称群的商空间进行推断：在对称群的基本域上构建独立采样器；通过对学习到的归一化流在群轨道上进行对称化来形成商提案；将LCNF振荡认证框架转移到商度量，并引入稳定子修正的球质量边界和改进的覆盖半径。

Result: 在维度d=2-20的高斯混合模型、多达24个等价模态的标签交换目标以及标准贝叶斯三成分混合后验上，量化核心认证改进比率范围为2倍到145倍；折叠证书在经验上几乎与维度无关；在台风山竹期间超高层建筑的真实加速度计数据上，FolT-MCMC产生了非平凡的量化核心证书，而未折叠的证书是平凡的。

Conclusion: FolT-MCMC成功解决了对称性导致的冗余多模态问题，通过商空间推断显著改善了MCMC的收敛诊断性能，证明了量化核心认证框架在商度量下的有效性，为具有对称结构的贝叶斯推断问题提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Bayesian models with finite symmetry - mixture models with exchangeable components, structural identification with closely-spaced modes - define posteriors that are invariant under a group of label permutations, creating redundant multimodality that degrades MCMC convergence diagnostics. We introduce Folded Transport MCMC (FolT-MCMC), which performs inference directly on the quotient posterior by constructing an independence sampler on the fundamental domain of the symmetry group. The quotient proposal is formed by symmetrising a learned normalising flow over the group orbits. We prove that the LCNF oscillation-based certification framework transfers to the quotient metric with a stabiliser-corrected ball-mass bound and improved covering radius, and that the quantile-core certified lower bound improves whenever the unfolded flow exhibits cross-mode proposal deficiency. On Gaussian mixtures (d = 2 - 20), label-switching targets (up to 24 equivalent modes), and a standard Bayesian three-component mixture posterior, the quantile-core certified improvement ratio ranges from 2x to 145x, with the folded certificate empirically nearly dimension-free. On real accelerometer data from a supertall building during Typhoon Mangkhut, FolT-MCMC yields a non-vacuous quantile-core certificate where the unfolded certificate is vacuous.

</details>


### [49] [Latent Anchor-Driven Test Generation for Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04310)
*Bin Duan,Matthew B. Dwyer,Guowei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Latte，一种基于潜在空间的黑盒DNN测试框架，通过VQ-VAE编码和锚点引导的潜在变异，在保持语义相似性的前提下生成更多样化的故障揭示测试用例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DNN测试方法在潜在空间生成时面临探索可控性、失败多样性和种子相对语义漂移之间的权衡问题，需要一种能够同时优化这些方面的测试方法。

Method: Latte使用预训练VQ-VAE对输入种子进行编码，执行以种子为中心的单步潜在变异，变异方向由从替代类别采样的锚点定义，随后进行量化解码回输入空间，从而在学习的潜在流形内探索种子的局部邻域。

Result: 在5个数据集和10个DNN模型上的评估表明，Latte在单模型和多模型测试场景下，在相同测试预算内提高了故障暴露和行为多样性。在单模型设置下，还保持了对源种子的低种子相对语义漂移。

Conclusion: Latte成功克服了现有方法的局限性，能够在保持语义邻近性的同时生成更多样化的故障揭示测试用例，有效平衡了探索可控性、失败多样性和语义漂移的权衡问题。

Abstract: Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) are increasingly being deployed in security-critical and safety-sensitive applications, which makes rigorous testing essential to identify and mitigate model weaknesses. Existing DNN testing approaches explore either the input space or a learned latent space. While latent-space generation can better maintain plausibility than direct input-space mutation, current methods still face a trade-off among exploration controllability, failure diversity, and seed-relative semantic drift. To overcome these limitations, we propose Latte, a black-box testing framework that generates semantically proximate, diverse, and fault-revealing test cases by leveraging the latent space. Specifically, Latte encodes each input seed with a pre-trained VQ-VAE and performs a seed-centered, one-step latent mutation along directions defined by anchors sampled from alternative classes, followed by quantization and decoding back to the input space. This explores local neighborhoods around each seed within the learned latent manifold, resulting in a larger number and broader diversity of oracle-triggering prediction discrepancies under the same budget. We evaluated Latte on 5 datasets and 10 DNN models in single-model and multi-model testing scenarios. Across the evaluated datasets and models, Latte improves fault exposure and behavioral diversity under matched testing budgets. Under the single-model setting, it also maintains low seed-relative semantic drift with respect to the source seeds.

</details>


### [50] [Testing Neural Networks via Bayesian-Guided Exploration of Decision Landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04314)
*Bin Duan,Meiru Che,Guowei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了BayesWarp框架，通过结合可解释性显著技术和不确定性感知的贝叶斯优化策略，实现了在保持数据分布和语义接近性的同时高效发现多样化的神经网络失效案例，提升了测试效果和模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络测试方法（黑盒或白盒）主要采用全局变异或覆盖率引导策略，在保持原始数据分布和语义接近性的前提下，难以高效发现多样化的模型失效案例。

Method: 提出BayesWarp测试框架，利用可解释性显著技术识别决策关键输入区域进行变异，并采用不确定性感知的贝叶斯优化策略自适应引导测试过程。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10和ImageNet数据集上对六个神经网络模型的评估表明，BayesWarp在固定变异预算下提升了失效发现能力、失效多样性、测试用例质量和关键神经元覆盖率。此外，使用生成的失效案例进行微调可以改善模型性能。

Conclusion: BayesWarp通过结合显著性引导的区域变异和贝叶斯优化，有效解决了现有测试方法在效率、多样性和数据接近性方面的平衡问题，提升了神经网络测试的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: As neural networks are increasingly deployed in safety-critical domains, testing is essential to evaluate and improve their reliability. Existing testing methods, whether black-box or white-box, primarily use global mutation or coverage-guided strategies, both of which struggle to efficiently uncover diverse model failures while remaining proximate to the original data distribution and semantics. We propose BayesWarp, a testing framework that addresses this limitation by mutating decision-critical input regions identified via interpretable saliency techniques and adaptively guiding the testing process using an uncertainty-aware Bayesian Optimization strategy, enabling the discovery of diverse failures while preserving distributional and semantic proximity to the original data. Evaluation on MNIST, CIFAR-10, and ImageNet across six neural network models shows that BayesWarp improves failure discovery, failure diversity, test case quality, and critical neuron coverage under a fixed mutation budget. These results demonstrate that BayesWarp improves testing effectiveness. Moreover, fine-tuning with the generated failure cases leads to improvements in model performance.

</details>


### [51] [OpenRFM: Dissecting Relational In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04320)
*Zhikai Chen,Junyu Yin,Jialiang Gu,Siheng Xiong,Xiaoze Liu,Ruowang Zhang,Keren Zhou,Kai Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenRFM，通过双阶段ICL架构和同质性感知的预训练策略，将关系基础模型(RFM)的平均任务性能提升约30%，超越商业模型KumoRFMv1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源关系基础模型(RFM)与商业 counterparts之间存在显著性能差距，且这一差距的根源尚未被系统性地理解。现有框架如关系Transformer(RT)存在模型和数据两方面的局限性，需要深入分析并解决。

Method: 从模型和数据两个视角剖析RT框架：(1)模型侧：提出双阶段ICL架构，将关系骨干网络与从预训练表格基础模型提取的批次级ICL层结合；(2)数据侧：设计同质性感知的合成数据与持续真实数据混合预训练策略，并引入基于原型的正则化。核心发现是需要支持可识别的关系潜在变量来弥补标签生成过程中的不足。

Result: OpenRFM相比RT骨干网络平均任务性能提升约30%，并在大规模评估任务集上超越商业模型KumoRFMv1。通过核回归视角揭示了当稀疏标签单元格覆盖导致欠定回归时RT失效的机制，并发现仅合成预训练和分布内预训练会导致相同架构进入懒惰学习与特征学习两种不同状态。

Conclusion: 研究系统性地识别了开源RFM性能差距的根源：关系级ICL在标签稀缺时失效以及预训练数据策略不当。通过双阶段架构设计和同质性感知的预训练混合策略，OpenRFM成功弥合了开源与商业RFM之间的性能鸿沟，为关系数据库基础模型的发展提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Relational Foundation Models (RFMs) promise a single pre-trained predictor that, given any relational database, returns predictions in one forward pass via relational in-context learning (ICL). Yet a substantial gap separates open RFMs from their commercial counterparts, and the origin of this gap has not been systematically understood. We dissect a representative framework, the Relational Transformer (RT), from two perspectives. Model side: we show that RT performs relation-level ICL, and a kernel regression view shows it fails when sparse label-cell coverage yields an underdetermined regression. Data side: we ablate RT's pre-training source and find that existing synthetic-only pre-training and in-distribution pre-training drive the same architecture into different regimes, lazy vs. feature-learning. Probing this gap reveals that the missing ingredient is a support-identifiable relational latent in the label-generation process. These two diagnoses translate into (1) a dual-stage ICL architecture that combines the relational backbone with a batch-level ICL layer lifted from a pre-trained tabular foundation model to overcome relation-level label scarcity, and (2) a homophily-aware synthetic plus continual real-data pre-training mixture, augmented with a prototype-based regularization. These choices define OpenRFM, a simple yet effective RFM that improves average task performance by approximately 30% over the RT backbone and surpasses the commercial model KumoRFMv1 on a large set of evaluation tasks.

</details>


### [52] [Neural Galerkin Normalizing Flows for Bayesian Inference of Diffusions with Inaccessible Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04324)
*Riccardo Saporiti,Fabio Nobile*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于归一化流的扩散模型过渡密度学习新架构，解决了贝叶斯推断中似然函数不可解析的难题，实现了高效的参数后验采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型参数贝叶斯推断面临核心挑战：连续观测时刻之间的转移密度函数缺乏解析表达式，导致无法推导似然函数。现有方法要么需要实时求解Fokker-Planck方程计算成本高，要么依赖似然自由方法需要重复模拟扩散桥。

Method: 在神经Galerkin框架下求解以Dirac质量为初始条件的Fokker-Planck方程，使用新型归一化流架构学习扩散过程在两个观测时间之间的转移密度函数。训练覆盖初始数据和扩散系数的指定分布，特别关注扩散矩阵在不可达边界区域消失的过程（如满足Feller条件的随机波动率模型）。

Result: 训练完成后获得转移密度函数，沿观测轨迹评估这些密度的乘积近似似然函数，能够通过MCMC进行低成本的后验采样。离线训练后，推断过程避免了实时求解FP方程或重复模拟扩散桥的需求。

Conclusion: 该方法显著提升了贝叶斯推断效率，为扩散模型参数估计提供了一种在训练后可快速进行似然评估和后验采样的有效解决方案。

Abstract: One of the primary challenges in Bayesian inference on the parameters of a diffusion model from discrete observations is the unavailability of an analytical expression for the transition density function between consecutive observation times, which is needed to derive the likelihood function.
  Extending previous studies that solve Fokker-Planck (FP) type partial differential equations with Normalizing Flows, we propose a new Normalizing Flow architecture to learn the transition density function of the diffusion process between two observation times. We do so by solving in a Neural Galerkin framework the associated FP equation with a Dirac mass as initial condition, over a specified training distribution of the initial datum and the coefficients of the diffusion. We specifically focus on processes whose diffusion matrix vanishes in certain inaccessible boundary regions, such as Stochastic Volatility models that satisfy a Feller condition.
  The product of the obtained transition densities evaluated along the observed trajectory approximates the likelihood function, thereby enabling cheap posterior sampling via Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC). After the offline training phase, inference becomes significantly more efficient, as it avoids the need to solve the FP equation in real time for each parameter proposed by the MCMC sampler or to rely on other likelihood-free methods for Bayesian inference that involve repeated simulation of diffusion bridges.

</details>


### [53] [Measuring What Matters: Synthetic Benchmarks for Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04326)
*Julian Skirzynski,Harry Cheon,Shreyas Kadekodi,Meredith Stewart,Berk Ustun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文开发了用于概念瓶颈模型的合成基准测试，通过控制数据模态、概念选择、标注质量和完整性等性能影响因素，支持决策支持和自动化两大应用场景的系统性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念瓶颈模型依赖高层概念进行预测以实现可解释性，但现有数据集极少包含概念标签，导致研究者无法确定哪些问题适合此类模型、难以隔离影响性能或导致失败的因素、无法评估不同算法的优劣。

Method: 开发了针对概念瓶颈模型的合成基准测试框架，聚焦决策支持（模型辅助人类决策）和自动化（模型独立处理常规任务）两种主要用例，该基准可以生成带标签的数据集，并控制影响性能的关键属性：数据模态、概念选择、标注质量和完整性。

Result: 通过代表性概念瓶颈模型类别的评估演示，展示了基准测试如何诊断模型失败模式，并指导后续测试工作，验证了基准测试框架在性能影响因素分析方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的合成基准测试为概念瓶颈模型的研究提供了系统性评估工具，能够帮助研究者识别模型适用场景、分析性能驱动因素和失败原因，为算法选择和改进提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Concept bottleneck models predict outcomes from high-level concepts detected in inputs. Although concepts provide a simple way to reap benefits from interpretability, very few datasets include concept labels. This limits researchers' ability to determine which problems are suitable for these models, isolate the factors that drive their performance or lead to failures, or uncover which algorithms perform well. In this paper, we develop synthetic benchmarks for concept-bottleneck models, focusing on their two main use cases: decision support, in which models assist humans in making better decisions, and automation, in which models handle routine tasks without supervision. Our benchmarks can generate labeled datasets while controlling for properties that affect performance, including data modality, concept choice, annotation quality, and completeness. We demonstrate how the benchmarks can be used to evaluate representative classes of concept bottleneck models. Our demonstrations show how the benchmarks can diagnose failure modes and guide follow-up testing.

</details>


### [54] [A Geometric Characterization of the Stationary Plateau for Two-Layer Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04327)
*Tian Ding,Dawei Li,Ruoyu Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究两层神经网络损失景观中由“神经元分裂”引起的平稳高原的几何结构，提出了“内Hessian矩阵”概念来分类这些平稳点，发现分裂局部最小值可产生最小值和鞍点的混合或全鞍点高原，而分裂鞍点总产生鞍点高原。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解神经网络宽度扩展时损失景观的几何变化，特别是神经元分裂现象导致的平稳高原结构，为模型扩展和重参数化提供理论基础。

Method: 引入“内Hessian矩阵”这一逐神经元曲率对象，分析其定定性和分裂系数如何共同决定高原的局部几何性质，对所有平稳点进行系统性分类。

Result: 证明了分裂局部最小值可产生局部最小值与鞍点的混合或全鞍点高原，并在温和假设下识别出确定的鞍点区域；分裂鞍点总是产生鞍点高原。内Hessian的定定性是决定性因素。

Conclusion: 统一并扩展了先前的景观分析，阐明了模型扩展何时以及如何保持或改变平稳点性质，为神经网络宽度扩展和重参数化的影响提供了新的几何洞察。

Abstract: We investigate the geometric structure of stationary plateaus that arise in the loss landscape of two-layer neural networks with smooth activation functions. We focus on the phenomenon of "neuron splitting" where duplicating a hidden neuron yields an affine set of stationary points in a wider network. We provide a comprehensive classification of all stationary points on these plateaus, determining under what conditions they constitute local minima or saddle points. Our characterization hinges on a per-neuron curvature object we term the "inner Hessian" matrix. Our analysis reveals that the definiteness of the inner Hessian and the choice of splitting coefficients jointly dictate the local geometry of the plateau. We show that "splitting" a local minimum can yield either a mixture of local minima and saddles or an all-saddle plateau, with a concrete sure-saddle region identified under mild assumptions. In contrast, splitting a saddle point always produces a plateau of saddle points. Our results unify and extend prior landscape analyses, elucidating when and how model expansion preserves or alters the nature of stationary points. These findings offer new geometric insights into the effects of width expansion and reparameterization in neural networks.

</details>


### [55] [Policy Gradient for Continuous-Time Robust Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04335)
*Tanya Veeravalli,David M. Bossens,Atsushi Nitanda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续时间鲁棒马尔可夫决策过程（RMDP）框架下的策略梯度算法，提出了双环优化器和平均场优化器，并证明了其收敛性和样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的RMDP研究集中在离散时间动态，而现实世界的许多系统本质上具有连续时间特性。为了在连续时间设置下设计具有鲁棒性保证的强化学习智能体，需要研究连续时间RMDP框架下的策略梯度算法，填补该领域的研究空白。

Method: 利用随机微分方程和常微分方程的路径公式和伴随公式推导策略梯度和对抗梯度。提出两种优化器：（1）双环优化器，通过内外层迭代优化；（2）平均场优化器，作为分布优化器并采用N粒子近似。理论分析基于oracle设置和样本设置。

Result: 双环优化器在oracle设置下达到线性收敛率，在样本设置下达到$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\frac{1}{ε^2})$的样本复杂度；平均场优化器在oracle设置下达到$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\frac{1}{K})$收敛率，在N粒子近似下达到$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\frac{N^2}ε)$的样本复杂度。同时为无折扣总成本MDP框架推导了新的理论工具。

Conclusion: 在具有神经常微分方程动态的连续时间RMDP上的实验验证了所提出的连续时间策略梯度算法的有效性，证明了双环优化器和平均场优化器的实用性，为连续时间鲁棒强化学习提供了有效的理论框架和算法工具。

Abstract: The framework of robust Markov decision processes (RMDPs) allows the design of reinforcement learning agents that satisfy performance guarantees under worst-case transition dynamics. Traditional RMDPs consider discrete-time dynamics and recently, sample-efficient policy gradient algorithms have been considered in this context. This paper investigates policy gradient algorithms within a continuous-time RMDP framework. Policy gradients and adversarial gradients are derived using pathwise and adjoint-based formulas for stochastic and ordinary differential equations. We propose double-loop optimisers to obtain linear convergence in the oracle-based setting and an $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\frac{1}{ε^2})$ sample complexity in the sample-based setting in an analysis which also derives novel tools for the framework of undiscounted total cost MDPs. Additionally, we propose mean-field optimisers as distributional optimisers with an $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\frac{1}{K})$ oracle-based convergence rate and an $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\frac{N^2}ε)$ sample complexity under $N$-particle approximation. The effectiveness of continuous-time policy gradient algorithms is confirmed for both optimisers on continuous-time RMDPs with neural ordinary differential equation dynamics.

</details>


### [56] [Federated Learning for Multi-Center Sepsis Early Prediction with Privacy-Preserving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04338)
*Xixi Tian,Di Wu,Xiang Liu,Yiziting Zhu,Yujie Li,Xin Shu,Bin Yi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究系统验证了联邦学习在多中心脓毒症早期预测中的可行性与安全性，实验表明其预测性能与集中式建模相当，且能有效防止隐私泄露和数据重构攻击，为隐私保护的多中心医疗协作提供了可靠解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多中心医疗数据具有隐私敏感性和分布式特征，这使得集中式建模进行准确的脓毒症早期预测面临严重障碍。虽然联邦学习作为协作模型开发的框架备受关注，但其实际性能、鲁棒性和隐私保护优势在真实世界临床数据集上的评估仍然不足。

Method: 研究构建了一个包含来自中国三家三甲医院、经过严格纳入排除标准筛选的648个临床样本的数据集。首先建立集中式训练范式作为性能基准，然后实现水平联邦学习框架进行分布式协作建模，并进行广泛的实验验证和隐私安全分析。

Result: 基于联邦学习的模型实现了与集中式模型高度可比的预测准确性，同时从根本上避免了隐私泄露。进一步的隐私安全分析证实，恶意攻击者无法从传输的模型参数中重构原始患者数据，表明对数据重构攻击具有强抵抗力。

Conclusion: 本研究不仅验证了联邦学习在临床脓毒症预测中的实用性和安全性，而且为隐私保护的多中心医疗协作提供了可靠可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Privacy-sensitive and distributed characteristics of multi-center medical data bring severe obstacles to centralized modeling for accurate early prediction of sepsis. Federated learning (FL) has attracted growing attention as a promising framework for collaborative model development, as it allows multiple institutions to jointly train predictive models without directly sharing or centralizing raw data. Nevertheless, its practical performance, robustness, and privacy-preserving benefits remain insufficiently evaluated using real-world clinical datasets. To bridge this gap, this study systematically examines the application of federated learning to multi-center sepsis prediction. The experimental dataset consists of 648 clinically screened samples collected from three tertiary hospitals in China, with rigorous inclusion and exclusion criteria. We establish a centralized training paradigm as the performance baseline, and then implement a horizontal federated learning framework for distributed collaborative modeling. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that the federated learning-based model achieves highly comparable prediction accuracy to the centralized counterpart, while fundamentally avoiding privacy leakage. Further privacy security analysis verifies that malicious attackers cannot reconstruct the original patient data from the transmitted model parameters, indicating strong resistance against data reconstruction attacks. This work not only validates the practicality and security of federated learning in clinical sepsis prediction, but also provides a reliable and feasible solution for privacy-preserving multi-center medical collaboration.

</details>


### [57] [Literature-Guided Minimax Optimization of Virtual Epilepsy Neurostimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04339)
*Cathy Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个文献引导的最小最大化流程，用于鲁棒的神经调控设计。在内在模型控制实验中，最坏情况奖励提升了39.8%，但临床式外部刺激搜索仅带来1.7%的最坏情况改善，且虚拟队列未显示总体获益，证明了概念可行性但临床效果有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的癫痫计算模型优化工作流通常寻找平均表现良好的参数，这在神经调控中是一个薄弱目标。一个能改善平均响应的协议仍可能在那些对刺激耐受性最低的患者身上失效，因此需要开发针对最坏情况进行优化的方法。

Method: 提出了一个文献引导的最小最大化流程，结合了PubMed规模的假设提取、The Virtual Brain (TVB) Epileptor仿真和大型语言模型引导的黑盒优化。优化器提出内在模型控制参数或临床可解释的外部刺激协议；TVB在每个采样的虚拟患者中评估每个提议；目标函数最大化最坏情况奖励（定义为模拟癫痫活动的负方差）。

Result: 在内在模型控制实验中，最佳存档参数集将最坏情况奖励从-0.5285提高到-0.3182，相比基线提升了39.8%。临床式外部刺激搜索产生的最坏情况改善小得多（1.7%），20名患者的虚拟队列未显示总体获益（p=0.9019），尽管有55%的响应率和阳性颞叶亚组信号。

Conclusion: 这项研究应被视为鲁棒、文献感知的神经调控设计的计算机概念验证，而非临床证据。证明了该方法的可行性，但临床式外部刺激的实际效果仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Computational models of epilepsy promise patient-specific treatment design, but most optimization workflows still search for parameters that perform well on average. In neuromodulation, this is a weak target: a protocol that improves the mean response can still fail in the patient whose network is least tolerant to stimulation. We present a literature-guided minimax pipeline that couples PubMed-scale hypothesis extraction, The Virtual Brain (TVB) Epileptor simulations, and large-language-model-guided black-box optimization. The optimizer proposes either intrinsic model-control parameters or clinically interpretable external-stimulation protocols; TVB evaluates each proposal across sampled virtual patients; and the objective maximizes worst-case reward, defined as the negative variance of simulated seizure activity. In the intrinsic model-control experiment, the best archived parameter set improved worst-case reward from -0.5285 to -0.3182, a 39.8% gain over baseline. The clinical-style external-stimulation search produced a much smaller worst-case improvement (1.7%), and a 20-patient virtual cohort showed no aggregate benefit (p=0.9019), despite a 55% responder rate and a positive temporal-lobe subgroup signal. The study should be read as an in silico proof of concept for robust, literature-aware neurostimulation design, not as clinical evidence.

</details>


### [58] [Expectations vs. Realities: The Cost of MSE-Optimal Forecasting Under Conditional Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04342)
*Riku Green,Zahraa S. Abdallah,Telmo M Silva Filho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了多步时间序列预测中仅使用MSE评估的局限性，证明了在条件不确定性下存在点精度与边际真实性的根本权衡，发现在MSE上做小幅让步（≤5%）可换来边际真实性的大幅提升（中位数17.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MSF评估普遍使用MSE等点误差指标，隐含假设条件均值是充分目标，但在条件不确定性较高时（尤其是长预测视界），条件期望可能无法代表典型实现值，导致评估结果误导。

Method: 1) 理论上形式化条件不确定性间隙，证明当该间隙非零时，不存在确定性预测器能同时最小化MSE并匹配实现值的边际分布；2) 使用受控随机动力系统和9个真实预测基准，经验刻画精度-真实性前沿；3) 分析不同预测策略在前沿上的分布特征。

Result: 证明了点精度与边际真实性之间存在根本的模型无关权衡；随着预测视界增加，条件不确定性上升，形成明显的帕累托前沿；在所有基准上，MSE的小幅放松（≤5%）能解锁边际真实性的不成比例提升，中位数改善17.3%，部分数据集超过30%；直接多输出预测器聚集在精度最优端，而递归策略和基于样本的推断偏向边际真实性。

Conclusion: MSE单一指标评估在长视界预测中存在结构性失效模式，模型选择需要导航不可避免的精度-真实性权衡，小幅牺牲点精度可换取显著的分布真实性改善，这重新定义了预测策略和推断方法的选择框架。

Abstract: Multi-step time series forecasting (MSF) is commonly evaluated using point-wise error metrics such as mean squared error (MSE), implicitly treating the conditional mean as a sufficient target. We show that this can be misleading under conditional uncertainty, where the conditional expectation becomes unrepresentative of typical realized values at longer horizons. We formalize this effect through a conditional uncertainty gap and prove that whenever this gap is nonzero, no deterministic predictor can simultaneously minimize MSE and match the marginal distribution of realized futures. This establishes a fundamental, model-agnostic trade-off between point accuracy and marginal realism in MSF evaluation. Using controlled stochastic dynamical systems and nine real-world forecasting benchmarks, we empirically characterize the resulting accuracy--realism frontier and \textbf{quantify the practical cost of MSE-only model selection}. As conditional uncertainty increases with forecast horizon, the attainable set expands into a pronounced Pareto front, separating MSE-optimal but under-dispersed predictors from methods that trade accuracy for realistic marginal variability. \textbf{Across benchmarks, we find that small relaxations in MSE ($\boldsymbol{\le 5\%}$) frequently unlock disproportionate gains in marginal realism, with median improvements of $\mathbf{17.3\%}$ and gains exceeding $\mathbf{30\%}$ in some datasets.} We further show that common forecasting strategies systematically occupy different regions of this frontier: direct multi-output predictors concentrate near the accuracy-optimal extreme, while recursive strategies and sample-based inference favors marginal realism. Together, these results expose a structural failure mode of MSE-based evaluation in long-horizon forecasting and recast strategy and inference selection as navigation of an unavoidable accuracy--realism trade-off.

</details>


### [59] [MeshTok: Efficient Multi-Scale Tokenization for Scalable PDE Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04366)
*Yanshun Zhao,Xiaoyu Peng,Jiamin Jiang,Congcong Zhu,Jingrun Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MeshTok框架，借鉴自适应网格细化(AMR)思想，通过选择性细化具有陡峭梯度或瞬态特征的空间区域生成多尺度token，在统一Transformer序列中处理，实现粗粒度全局上下文和细粒度局部细节的同时捕获，在多种PDE任务上提升了效率-精度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统patchified Transformer在统一空间分区上均匀分配计算资源，无法根据局部特征灵活调整，这种僵化的tokenization方案在表示和处理复杂PDE解时效率低下，难以有效处理具有多尺度结构、陡峭梯度或瞬态特征的物理场。

Method: 提出AMR启发的自适应tokenization方法，识别并选择性细化具有陡峭梯度、瞬态特征或多尺度结构的空间区域，在固定模拟网格上生成异构多尺度token集合；将这些token在统一的Transformer序列中处理，无需专门架构组件即可同时捕获粗粒度全局上下文和细粒度局部细节。

Result: 在多个PDE族和基准数据集上的实验表明，相比统一网格基线方法，MeshTok持续改善效率-精度权衡；自适应细化虽然适度增加token数量，但实现了对物理信息丰富区域更有针对性的计算资源分配。

Conclusion: 自适应多尺度tokenization可作为神经PDE建模的可扩展、可泛化的设计原则；该方法的成功表明将物理先验（如计算资源向关键区域集中）作为实用归纳偏置是有效的，为未来物理驱动的深度学习架构设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Conventional patchified Transformers operate on uniform spatial partitions, distributing computational effort evenly across the domain irrespective of local features. This inflexible tokenization scheme is inherently limited in its ability to efficiently represent and process solutions to complex PDEs. To address this, we propose MeshTok, an adaptive mesh refinement (AMR)-inspired tokenization and sequence modeling framework. This method selectively refines spatial regions exhibiting sharp gradients, transient features, or multiscale structures, generating a heterogeneous set of multiscale tokens defined on a fixed simulation grid. These tokens are processed within a unified Transformer sequence, enabling the model to simultaneously capture coarse-grained global context and fine-grained local details without requiring specialized architectural components. Although adaptive refinement moderately increases token count, it promotes a more targeted allocation of computational resources to physically informative regions, which we view as a practical inductive bias rather than a formal optimality guarantee. Experimental evaluations across multiple PDE families and benchmark datasets demonstrate that MeshTok consistently improves the efficiency-accuracy trade-off compared to uniform-grid baselines. This suggests adaptive multiscale tokenization as a scalable and generalizable design principle for neural PDE modeling. Code is available at https://github.com/SCAILab-USTC/MeshTok.

</details>


### [60] [When Do Fewer Coordinates Suffice in DP-SGD?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04375)
*Huiqi Zhang,Fang Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TP-TopK方法，通过两阶段训练解决DP-SGD中噪声随参数维度d缩放的问题，使噪声仅随活跃维度k缩放，在活跃维度较小时效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DP-SGD在每个更新的坐标上注入噪声，导致注入的噪声能量随环境参数维度d增加而增加。作者探索是否可以更新更少的坐标而不丢失优化所需的信号，从而降低噪声影响。

Method: 提出TP-TopK（Two-Phase TopK DP-SGD）两阶段坐标稀疏私有训练方法，无需公共数据。第一阶段为私有预热阶段，识别坐标支撑集；第二阶段为主要训练阶段，在识别出的坐标支撑上进行训练。该方法包含一个判断坐标限制是否有益的准则，以及在非凸平稳性界下证明噪声项随活跃维度k而非全参数维度d缩放的分析。

Result: 在MNIST、FMNIST和CIFAR-10上的实验表明：学习到的坐标支撑集比同等大小的随机支撑集能保留更多的梯度能量。当活跃维度较小且预热分数具有信息性时，增益最大。给出了基于预热的坐标排名可靠性的下界。

Conclusion: 在满足坐标限制有益准则的条件下，TP-TopK方法能够有效降低差分隐私训练中的噪声影响，使噪声项随活跃维度k而非全参数维度d缩放。该方法在活跃维度较小且预热阶段能有效识别重要坐标时效果最佳，为高维参数空间下的差分隐私训练提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Differentially private stochastic gradient descent (DP-SGD) injects noise into every updated coordinate, making the injected noise energy scale with the ambient parameter dimension \(d\). We ask when private training can update fewer coordinates without losing the signal needed for optimization. We propose \textsc{TP-TopK} (Two-Phase TopK DP-SGD), a two-phase method for coordinate-sparse private training without public data, in which a private warm-up phase identifies a coordinate support used to guide the main training phase. We give a criterion characterizing when coordinate restriction can be beneficial, show via a nonconvex stationarity bound that under this condition the relevant noise term scales with the active dimension \(k\) rather than the full parameter dimension \(d\), and provide a lower bound on the reliability of warm-up-based coordinate ranking. Experiments on MNIST, FMNIST, and CIFAR-10 show that learned coordinate supports can retain more gradient energy than size-matched random supports, with the largest gains when the active dimension is small and warm-up scores are informative.

</details>


### [61] [From Symbolic to Geometric: Enabling Spatial Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04381)
*Chen Chu,Bita Azarijoo,Li Xiong,Khurram Shafique,Cyrus Shahabi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出首个将位置信息作为一等模态的多模态大语言模型SLM，通过几何空间表示而非符号匹配实现真正的空间推理能力，在新基准SpatialEval上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型的空间推理能力本质上是符号性的，源于对空间语言的pattern matching而非真正的几何推理；由于LLM基于离散token运作，缺乏对连续空间表示、显式几何计算和结构化空间算子的原生支持。

Method: 提出Spatial Language Model (SLM)，将位置信息视为一等模态，使模型能在推理过程中进行几何空间推理；构建Spatial Instruction Dataset来对齐空间表示、原子几何操作和自然语言指令；直接操作学习的空间表示而非空间关系的文本描述。

Result: 在新提出的SpatialEval基准上（涵盖属性、距离、拓扑和相对位置任务），SLM显著优于依赖提示工程或文本抽象进行符号推理的现有LLM方法，证明了集成几何空间表示对鲁棒空间推理的价值。

Conclusion: 成功证明了将几何空间表示集成到大语言模型中的有效性，SLM作为首个原生支持连续空间表示和几何计算的多模态LLM，为空间推理任务提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent large language models (LLMs) often appear to exhibit spatial reasoning ability; however, this capability is largely \emph{symbolic}, arising from pattern matching over spatial language rather than true \emph{geometric} reasoning over space. Because LLMs operate on discrete tokens, they lack native support for continuous spatial representations, explicit geometric computation, and structured spatial operators. To address this limitation, we introduce the \emph{Spatial Language Model (SLM)}, the first multimodal LLM that treats location information as a first-class modality and enables geometric spatial reasoning within the model's inference process. SLM directly operates on learned spatial representations rather than textual descriptions of spatial relations. To support effective training, we construct a \emph{Spatial Instruction Dataset} that aligns spatial representations, atomic geometric operations, and natural language instructions. We further propose a new benchmark named \emph{SpatialEval}, which is designed to evaluate spatial reasoning across attributes, distance, topology, and relative-position tasks. Extensive experiments show that SLM significantly outperforms existing LLM-based approaches that rely on symbolic reasoning via prompt engineering or textual abstraction, demonstrating the benefits of integrating geometric spatial representations for robust spatial reasoning.
  Our instruction dataset, evaluation benchmark, model training codes, and models' checkpoints can be found at:
  \hyperlink{https://github.com/chuchen2017/SLM}{https://github.com/chuchen2017/SLM}.

</details>


### [62] [Revisiting Privacy Amplification by Subsampling in Selective Release DPSGD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04384)
*Xiaobo Huang,Fang Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DPSR-CG算法，通过重新审视选择性释放机制的隐私分析，解决了现有DPSUR算法中因忽略采样概率变化而导致的隐私保证不严谨问题，在保持严格隐私保证的同时实现了优异的模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习对敏感数据的依赖需要差分隐私保护技术（如DPSGD），但DPSGD因梯度裁剪和噪声注入存在效用显著下降和收敛缓慢的问题。现有的DPSUR算法虽实现了较好的模型效用，但其隐私会计忽略了选择性释放机制引入的采样概率变化，损害了隐私保证的严谨性。

Method: 重新评估选择性释放机制的隐私分析，提出基于裁剪梯度的差分隐私选择性释放算法（DPSR-CG），推导出新的严格隐私分析方法，确保选择性释放过程中的隐私保证严谨性。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10、IMDB和FMNIST等多个数据集上的大量实验表明，DPSR-CG机制在维持严格隐私保证的同时，实现了卓越的模型性能。

Conclusion: DPSR-CG算法成功解决了现有选择性释放机制中隐私分析的缺陷，为差分隐私机器学习提供了一个既保证严格隐私又具有高模型效用的解决方案。

Abstract: Machine learning's reliance on sensitive data necessitates privacy-preserving techniques like Differentially Private Stochastic Gradient Descent (DPSGD). However, DPSGD suffers from substantial utility degradation and slow convergence due to gradient clipping and noise injection. Prior works have attempted to improve DPSGD from various perspectives; notably, the Differentially Private Selective Update and Release (DPSUR) algorithm has achieved remarkable model utility. However, the privacy accounting in DPSUR overlooks the variation in sampling probability introduced by the selective release mechanism, which compromises the rigor of its privacy guarantees. To address these limitations, we re-evaluate the privacy analysis of the selective release mechanism and propose a novel algorithm: Differentially Private Selective Release based on Clipped Gradients (DPSR-CG). Through a rigorous, newly derived privacy analysis and extensive experiments on multiple datasets (MNIST, CIFAR-10, IMDB, and FMNIST), we demonstrate that our DPSR-CG mechanism maintains strict privacy guarantees while achieving exceptional model performance.

</details>


### [63] [Shortcomings and capacities of real-constrained neural networks in complex spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04390)
*Andrew Gracyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过HCIZ公式推导了复假设类中实预激活与复预激活存储容量的渐近比率，提供了一种更稳健的近似方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在相同复假设类中，强制使用实预激活而非复预激活时，存储容量的渐近比率关系，以理解不同激活类型对存储能力的影响。

Method: 基于临界容量下的Gardner体积比较方法，创新性地应用Harish-Chandra-Itzykson-Zuber (HCIZ)公式，结合Weyl积分公式和Haar测度在酉和正交紧流形上进行积分运算。

Result: 获得了复假设类中实预激活与复预激活存储容量的渐近比率，并通过HCIZ公式实现了更稳健的最终渐近比率近似。

Conclusion: 所提出的HCIZ公式应用方法在文献中非标准但有效，为研究紧流形上的积分问题提供了新思路，该方法特别适用于涉及酉和正交紧流形积分的相关工作。

Abstract: We find the asymptotic ratio between the storage capacities when enforcing real pre-activations in a complex hypothesis class as opposed to complex ones in the same class. Our methods depend on Gardner volume comparisons at critical capacity. Our proof relies on an application of the Harish-Chandra-Itzykson-Zuber (HCIZ) formula, nonstandard in literature. With the HCIZ formula, we may obtain a more robust approximation for the final asymptotic ratio. This strategy is applicable to our work specifically since we integrate over the unitary and orthogonal compact manifolds, facilitated via the Weyl integration formula and the Haar measure.

</details>


### [64] [Physics-Informed Neural Network Modeling of Biodegradable Contaminant Transport through GCL/SL Composite Liners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04392)
*Dong Li,Yapeng Cao,Haiping Zhao,Shutong Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究开发了双域物理信息神经网络框架用于模拟GCL/SL复合衬垫系统的污染物传输，硬约束PINN(H-PINN)相比标准PINN显著提高了预测精度，并成功应用于反演建模识别降解参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对GCL/SL复合衬垫系统中污染物传输的复杂物理过程，传统数值方法存在计算成本高、参数识别困难等问题，需要开发一种高效准确的计算框架来模拟薄层GCL和下层土壤衬垫的污染物传输行为。

Method: 提出双域物理信息神经网络框架：薄GCL层采用稳态对流-弥散-生物降解公式，下层土壤衬垫作为瞬态传输域建模。评估了两种方法：标准PINN（Std-PINN）使用软约束，硬约束PINN（H-PINN）将边界和初始条件直接嵌入试函数中，采用tanh激活函数并优化网络结构。

Result: H-PINN显著优于Std-PINN：MAE从0.058-0.067降至0.011-0.023，MRE从9.10%-19.16%降至2.08%-3.14%。Std-PINN在早期传输阶段和高渗透压头条件下误差较大，H-PINN减轻了基于惩罚的约束执行的优化负担，提供更准确稳定的浓度预测。反演建模能从有限浓度观测中可靠识别SL降解半衰期，在低至中度观测噪声下表现稳健。

Conclusion: 硬约束PINN框架在模拟复合衬垫系统污染物传输方面具有优越性能，为填埋场衬垫系统设计和污染物传输预测提供了可靠的计算工具，同时具备参数反演能力，可用于实际工程应用。

Abstract: This study develops a two-domain physics-informed neural network framework for contaminant transport through a GCL/SL composite liner system, in which the thin GCL layer is treated using a steady-state advection-dispersion-biodegradation formulation and the underlying soil liner is modeled as a transient transport domain. Two formulations are evaluated against analytical and finite-element reference solutions under different leachate-head conditions: a standard PINN with soft constraint enforcement (Std-PINN) and a hard-constrained PINN (H-PINN), in which selected boundary and initial conditions are embedded directly into the trial solutions. The Std-PINN captures the overall breakthrough behavior but shows larger errors during the early transport stage, particularly under higher leachate heads where advective transport becomes more pronounced. The H-PINN reduces the optimization burden associated with penalty-based constraint enforcement and provides more accurate and stable concentration predictions, lowering the MAE from approximately 0.058-0.067 for the Std-PINN to about 0.011-0.023 for the H-PINN, while reducing the MRE from approximately 9.10%-19.16% to about 2.08%-3.14%. Parametric analyses confirm that the H-PINN with the tanh activation function and an optimized network structure provides the best predictive accuracy. The H-PINN is further extended to inverse modeling for identifying the SL degradation half-life from limited concentration observations, showing reliable convergence toward prescribed values and acceptable robustness under low-to-moderate observation noise.

</details>


### [65] [DPDL: Towards Differential Privacy Preservation in Decentralized Stochastic Learning on Non-IID Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04399)
*Yunsheng Yuan,Xue Xiao,Lina Wang,Feng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向非独立同分布（non-IID）数据的隐私保护去中心化学习算法DPDL，通过在交叉梯度聚合中应用差分隐私与基于相似度的校准技术，实现了在保护隐私的同时保持模型训练的线性加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化学习中，多智能体协作训练全局模型需要频繁交换梯度信息，存在严重的隐私泄露风险；同时，现实场景中各智能体的数据通常呈非独立同分布，进一步增加了隐私保护去中心化学习的挑战。

Method: 提出DPDL算法：在每个训练轮次，每个智能体使用高斯噪声机制对其交叉梯度（即邻居模型在本地私有数据上的导数）进行扰动后共享；接收到扰动交叉梯度后，采用余弦相似度进行校准，并以类似动量的方式聚合校准后的交叉梯度以更新本地模型。

Result: 理论分析给出了达到特定隐私保护水平所需的最小噪声水平，并证明该算法在非独立同分布数据下仍能实现训练的线性加速；在真实数据集上的大量实验验证了算法在抵御隐私攻击和训练高精度模型方面的有效性。

Conclusion: DPDL算法有效解决了去中心化学习在非独立同分布数据下的隐私保护问题，在保证隐私的同时实现了良好的模型训练性能，为实际应用提供了一种可行的隐私保护去中心化学习方案。

Abstract: In the paradigm of decentralized learning, a group of agents collaborate to train a global model using distributed datasets without a central server. Although the power of collaboration has been verified by many state-of-the-art studies, it entails extensive gradient information exchanging among the agents and thus induces high risk of privacy leakage for the individual agents. Moreover, in real-world applications, the training data are usually non-identically and independently distributed across the agents, inducing more challenges to enable privacy-preserved decentralized learning. To address these issues, we propose a privacy-preserved decentralized learning algorithm with non-IID data, DPDL, which leverages the notion of Differential Privacy (DP) in cross-gradient aggregation through a similarity-based calibration technique. Specifically, in each round, each agent perturbs the cross-gradients (i.e., the derivatives of its neighbors' local model in its private local data) by Gaussian noise mechanism before sharing them with its neighbors; it then adopt cosine similarity to calibrate the received perturbed cross-gradients such that the aggregation of the calibrated cross-gradients can be utilized to effectively update local model in a momentum-like manner. Our rigorous theoretical analysis not only reveals the minimum noise level required to achieve a specific level of privacy preservation, but also illustrates that our algorithm still achieves a linear speedup in training with non-IID data. We finally conduct extensive experiments on real-world dataset to validate the effectiveness of our algorithm in defending privacy attacks and in training accurate models.

</details>


### [66] [TANDEM: Bi-Level Data Mixture Optimization with Twin Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04401)
*Jiaxing Wang,Deping Xiang,Jin Xu,Mingyang Yi,Guoqiang Gong,Zicheng Zhang,Haoran Li,Pengzhang Liu,Zhen Chen,Ke Zhang,Ju Fan,Qixiang Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TANDEM方法，通过双网络结构简化双层优化问题，实现了大语言模型训练数据领域混合比例的优化，在多种场景下显著提升了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的能力严重依赖于来自不同领域的训练数据，如何优化领域特定的数据混合比例是一个关键问题。现有的方法在处理这一问题时存在局限性，需要一种更具理论保证和广泛适用性的解决方案。

Method: 将领域特定混合比例优化建模为双层优化问题，并将其简化为单层惩罚形式。采用双网络架构：一个是在主要数据上训练的代理模型，另一个是用额外数据动态更新的参考模型。通过测量两个模型之间的差异来评估数据有效性，并对从额外数据中获益更多的领域进行加权。

Result: TANDEM方法在理论上有保证，比现有方法具有更广泛的适用性。在数据受限场景和监督微调等新设置下，优化后的混合比例显著提升了性能。大量实验验证了TANDEM在所有场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: TANDEM成功解决了大语言模型训练数据领域混合比例优化问题，提供了新的双层优化视角，拓展了领域重加权的研究范围，为数据受限场景和监督微调等实际应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: The capabilities of large language models (LLMs) significantly depend on training data drawn from various domains. Optimizing domain-specific mixture ratios can be modeled as a bi-level optimization problem, which we simplify into a single-level penalized form and solve with twin networks: a proxy model trained on primary data and a dynamically updated reference model trained with additional data. Our proposed method, Twin Networks for bi-level DatA mixturE optiMization (TANDEM), measures the data efficacy through the difference between the twin models and up-weights domains that benefit more from the additional data. TANDEM provides theoretical guarantees and wider applicability, compared to prior approaches. Furthermore, our bi-level perspective suggests new settings to study domain reweighting such as data-restricted scenarios and supervised fine-tuning, where optimized mixture ratios significantly improve the performance. Extensive experiments validate TANDEM's effectiveness in all scenarios.

</details>


### [67] [Low-Rank Decay for Grokking in Scale-Invariant Transformers: A Spectral-Geometric View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04405)
*Mingyu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出低秩衰减(LRD)正则化方法，通过核范数谱正则化解决缩放不变性下L2权重衰减失效的问题，在模运算任务中显著加速了泛化延迟(grokking)现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代Transformer架构中广泛使用RMSNorm和Query-Key Normalization等归一化机制，使模型部分组件对权重幅度近似具有缩放不变性。在这种设置下，标准的Frobenius范数权重衰减仅沿权重空间的径向方向作用，无法直接简化归一化层所表示的函数，导致模型在记忆训练集后难以重塑权重谱结构。

Method: 提出低秩衰减(LRD)方法，一种类核范数的谱正则化器。其子梯度包含极因子UV⊤，即使在缩放不变性设置下仍保持切向分量。这使得在任务梯度消失后，LRD能以ℓ1类方式继续压缩奇异值，从而有效降低权重矩阵的有效秩。作者还通过核范数次微分在低秩流形附近的"针到扇"扩展提供了谱几何解释。

Result: 在模运算任务上，LRD诱导Query/Key矩阵的快速有效秩坍缩，显著扩展了延迟泛化(grokking)发生的数据比例边界。相比L2衰减在模型记忆训练集后无法重塑权重谱，LRD能够持续压缩奇异值，促进模型的泛化能力。

Conclusion: LRD通过引入具有切向分量的谱正则化机制，有效克服了缩放不变性设置下传统权重衰减的局限性。该方法不仅提供了理论上的谱几何解释，还在实践中加速了grokking现象，为理解深度学习中的泛化延迟问题提供了新视角。

Abstract: Modern Transformer architectures frequently employ normalization mechanisms such as RMSNorm and Query-Key Normalization, making parts of the model approximately scale-invariant with respect to weight magnitudes. In this regime, standard Frobenius-norm weight decay acts purely along the radial direction of the weight space and cannot directly simplify the function represented by the normalized layer. We study grokking in small algorithmic tasks through this lens and propose \emph{Low-Rank Decay} (LRD), a nuclear-norm-like spectral regularizer whose subgradient -- the polar factor $UV^\top$ -- retains a tangential component even in the scale-invariant setting. This distinction has a concrete dynamical consequence: after the model memorizes the training set and task gradients vanish, L2 decay can no longer reshape the weight spectrum, whereas LRD continues to compress singular values in an $\ell_1$-like fashion. On modular arithmetic tasks, we find that LRD induces rapid effective-rank collapse in Query/Key matrices and expands the data-fraction boundary at which delayed generalization (grokking) occurs. We further provide a spectral-geometric interpretation through the ``needle-to-fan'' expansion of the nuclear-norm subdifferential near low-rank strata.

</details>


### [68] [An Ensembled Latent Factor Model via Differential Evolution and Gradient Descent Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04408)
*Rui Zhang,Jinhang Liu,Wenbo Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ELFM-DEGDO模型，通过集成差分进化与梯度下降两种优化方法，有效解决高维不完整数据中潜在因子模型的表示不足和偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在因子模型大多仅依赖梯度下降进行优化，在处理异构高维不完整数据时容易产生不足和有偏的表示。因此需要一种能够融合不同优化范式优势的方法来提升表示学习的质量。

Method: 提出基于差分进化与梯度下降优化的集成潜在因子模型(ELFM-DEGDO)，包含两个核心设计：1)分别通过差分进化和梯度下降优化独立建模两个不同的潜在因子模型；2)通过定制的自适应加权机制组合这两个模型，有效融合两者的优势。

Result: 在三个高维不完整数据集上的实验表明，ELFM-DEGDO始终优于多个相关潜在因子模型，证明了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过利用差分进化和梯度下降两种优化范式的互补优势，ELFM-DEGDO能够为高维不完整数据生成更全面、偏差更小的表示，为该领域提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: High-dimensional and incomplete (HDI) data are prevalent in many real-world big data scenarios. Latent factor models serve as a common representation learning approach, capable of uncovering informative latent factors from such data. Nevertheless, most existing latent factor models rely solely on gradient descent for optimization, which may lead to insufficient and biased representations, particularly when dealing with heterogeneous HDI data. Thus, this study proposes an Ensembled Latent Factor Model via Differential Evolution and Gradient Descent Optimization (ELFM-DEGDO) with two-fold designed: 1) two diverse latent factor models are independently modeled via differential evolution and gradient descent optimization, respectively, and 2) the two diverse latent factor models are combined via a customized self-adaptive weighting mechanism to effectively fuse their strengths. By leveraging the complementary advantages of both optimization paradigms, ELFM-DEGDO is able to produce more comprehensive and less biased representations for HDI data. Three HDI datasets are tested to show that ELFM-DEGDO consistently performs better than related several latent factor models.

</details>


### [69] [(Mis)generalization of Helpful-only Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04413)
*Mohammad Omar Khursheed,Baram Sosis,Fabien Roger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了现有仅帮助（helpful-only）模型的缺陷，包括新兴错位、残留拒绝行为、可控性差、迎合性和角色不一致等问题，并提出了通过合成文档微调和在SFT与RL中加入角色相关问题来缓解这些问题的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅帮助模型（始终遵循用户意图的模型）对危险能力评估和AI研发具有重要价值，但对其泛化特性了解不足。现有研究表明仅帮助模型比无害模型拒绝更少，但缺乏对其对齐其他维度的研究。

Method: 1. 系统研究现有仅帮助模型的缺陷
2. 分析简单反拒绝训练导致的问题
3. 提出缓解方法：合成文档微调（synthetic document fine-tuning）
4. 在监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）中添加角色相关问题

Result: 发现仅帮助模型存在以下问题：
1. 新兴错位（emergent misalignment）
2. 残留拒绝行为
3. 可控性差
4. 迎合性（sycophancy）
5. 角色不一致

证明这些问题并非仅帮助训练的必然结果，所提方法可有效缓解这些问题。

Conclusion: 仅帮助训练本身不会导致上述问题，通过合理的训练策略（合成文档微调和角色相关问题增强）可以构建性能良好的仅帮助模型，为危险能力评估等应用提供可靠工具。

Abstract: Helpful-only models, that is, models that are trained to always follow user intent, are valuable for dangerous capability evaluations and other areas of AI R&D where refusals would be an obstacle. Little is known about the generalization properties of helpful-only training: helpful-only models refuse less than their harmless counterparts, but previous work has not studied other dimensions of their alignment. We study the shortcomings of existing helpful-only models. We find that some show emergent misalignment, others have residual refusal behaviors, and most show poor steerability, sycophancy, and incoherent character. We show that simple anti-refusal training can cause many of these issues. None of these problems are necessary consequences of helpful-only training, though: we show that synthetic document fine-tuning and adding character-related questions to SFT and RL can mitigate them.

</details>


### [70] [Loss-Conditional PINNs for Parametric PDE Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04420)
*Anna Lazareva,Alexander Tarakanov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了LC-PINN方法，将条件向量作为网络输入，探索整个权重空间而非调优单一权重向量，使单次训练即可得到权重参数化的解族，性能媲美甚至超越逐权重重新训练的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PINN的性能严重依赖于损失权重的选择，不当的权重配置会导致模型陷入次优解（满足某些物理约束而忽略其他）。现有方法仅选择或适应单一良好的权重组合，本文希望探索整个权重空间而非调优单一权重向量。

Method: 将Dosovitskiy和Djolonga(2020)的条件损失训练扩展到PDE残差设置：将条件向量（损失权重或物理系数）作为网络输入，在每次优化步骤中从简单先验中采样，从而学习以该向量为索引的连续解族。采用拼接方式处理损失权重，使用FiLM层处理系数参数，并提出固定求积L-BFGS收尾协议使参数系数 regime 可训练。

Result: 在参数化Helmholtz、薛定谔、粘性Burgers和Buckley-Leverett方程上的实验表明，单个LC-PINN模型能够匹配或超越重新训练的逐权重PINN基线，同时在单个模型中参数化整个解族。总体训练成本相比逐实例重新训练具有显著的摊销优势。

Conclusion: LC-PINN成功地将损失权重和参数系数两种regime统一到一个架构下，在保持完全物理信息的同时，将训练成本摊销到参数化族上，为PINN的权重选择问题提供了新的解决方案，介于经典PINN和算子学习之间。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) approximate solutions of ODEs and PDEs by minimising a weighted combination of residual, boundary, initial, and data losses. Their performance is often dominated by the choice of loss weights: a poor weighting can drive training to a degenerate solution in which one physical constraint is satisfied while another is ignored. Existing methods select or adapt a single good set of weights. We take a different view: instead of tuning one weight vector, we explore the entire weight space during training.
  We introduce LC-PINN, which adapts the loss-conditional training of Dosovitskiy and Djolonga (2020) to the PDE-residual setting: the conditioning vector (either the loss weights or a scalar physical coefficient) is treated as a network input and sampled from a simple prior at every optimisation step. This turns PINN training into learning a continuous family of solutions indexed by that vector, with no solver-generated paired data. LC-PINN thus lies between classical PINNs and operator learning: it stays fully physics-informed but amortises training over a parametric family. Our contribution is not the loss-conditional construction itself, but its extension to PINNs, the unification of the loss-weight and parametric-coefficient regimes under one architecture (concatenation for loss weights, FiLM for coefficients), and a fixed-quadrature L-BFGS finishing protocol that makes the parametric-coefficient regime trainable.
  We give a lambda-invariance result for the conditional optimum and study LC-PINN on parametric Helmholtz, Schrodinger, viscous Burgers, and Buckley-Leverett equations. A single LC-PINN matches or improves retrained per-weight PINN baselines while parameterising the full family in one model, at a total cost that amortises favourably against per-instance retraining.

</details>


### [71] [The price of multi-group transductive learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04423)
*Noah Bergam,Samuel Deng,Daniel Hsu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在转导学习设置中，多群体学习器相比单群体设置可能面临线性增长误差惩罚，与统计设置中的对数惩罚形成鲜明对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多群体学习在转导设置与统计设置下的性能差异，揭示不同学习框架下群体数量对误差率惩罚的影响程度。

Method: 理论分析方法，通过构造和证明展示转导设置中多群体学习器相比单群体设置必然面临的误差率乘性惩罚，并分析惩罚与群体数量及样本规模的关系。

Result: 在转导设置中，多群体学习器在某些群体上的误差率惩罚可达群体数量的线性增长，最高约达样本规模的平方根；而在类似的群体可实现统计设置中，最优多群体学习器的惩罚仅为样本规模的对数级且独立于群体数量。

Conclusion: 转导设置与统计设置在多群体学习问题上存在根本性能差异，转导设置下的误差惩罚显著更严重，这一发现对多群体学习算法设计具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: We show every multi-group learner in the transductive setting may incur a multiplicative penalty in its error rate on some group relative to the error rate achievable in the single-group setting, and the penalty can increasing linearly with the number of groups, up to roughly the square-root of the sample size. This stands in stark contrast to optimal multi-group learners in an analogous (group-realizable) statistical setting, where the penalty is always at most logarithmic in the sample size and independent of the number of groups.

</details>


### [72] [LoopMoE: Unifying Iterative Computation with Mixture-of-Experts for Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04438)
*Wenkai Chen,Tianshu Li,Wenyong Huang,Yichun Yin,Lifeng Shang,Chengwei Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LoopMoE，一种结合稀疏路由和循环计算的MoE语言模型，在严格控制参数量、计算量和活跃参数比例的条件下，首次实现了循环MoE与普通MoE的公平对比，并在多个基准测试上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的循环架构主要基于稠密主干网络，导致参数量与每token计算量（FLOPs）相互耦合，无法在匹配预算条件下独立研究迭代计算的效果。因此需要一种能够解耦这两个因素的新型架构。

Method: 提出了LoopMoE模型，通过两个核心设计实现：(1) IterAdaLN机制，通过联合条件于迭代索引和每token隐藏状态的调制信号解决权重共享对称性问题；(2) 容量平衡策略，恢复注意力机制到FFN的活跃参数比例，使其与调优良好的非循环参考模型一致。这两项设计使得在完全相同的总参数量、每token FLOPs和活跃子层比例条件下进行严格控制的对比评估成为可能。

Result: 在3B规模下，LoopMoE在9个下游基准测试中有8个超越普通MoE，平均提升超过1个百分点；在9B规模下，LoopMoE继续超越匹配参数的普通MoE，表明架构优势在更大规模下仍然持续存在。

Conclusion: 本研究成功建立了稀疏性与循环性的控制性综合框架，证明了LoopMoE架构的有效性和可扩展性，为循环语言模型的发展指明了有前景的方向。该工作首次在严格控制的条件下验证了结合MoE和循环架构的优势。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) and looped architectures scale models along two orthogonal axes, namely parameter capacity and effective depth. However, mainstream looped architectures rely on dense backbones that couple parameter count with per-token FLOPs, which makes it impossible to isolate the effect of iterative computation under matched budgets. To this end, we present LoopMoE, a looped MoE language model that integrates sparse routing with iterative weight-shared computation through two designs. The first is IterAdaLN, which resolves weight-sharing symmetry via a modulation signal jointly conditioned on the iteration index and the per-token hidden state. The second is a capacity-balancing strategy that recovers the attention-to-FFN active parameter ratio of well-tuned non-looped references. Together, these designs enable the first strictly controlled, head-to-head evaluation of a looped MoE against a Vanilla MoE under identical total parameters, per-token FLOPs, and active sublayer ratios. At the 3B scale, LoopMoE outperforms the Vanilla MoE on 8 of 9 downstream benchmarks with an average improvement exceeding 1 point. At the 9B scale, LoopMoE continues to outperform the matched Vanilla MoE, indicating that the architectural gain persists at larger scale. Our work establishes a controlled synthesis of sparsity and recurrence, and suggests a promising direction for looped language models.

</details>


### [73] [RowNet: A Memory Transformer for Tabular Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04445)
*Askat Rakhymbekov,Gulshat Muhametjanova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RowNet，一种基于检索的神经网络架构，通过将查询房产与历史标记房产进行成对相似度比较来进行房地产每平米价格预测，解决了传统方法无法显式建模相似历史观测检索的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 房地产估值是一个结构化回归问题，价格受异构特征类型、稀疏区域效应、非线性交互和可比属性的实际逻辑支配。标准多层感知机将每行视为孤立向量，必须仅从监督中学习局部性、尺度敏感性和类别匹配；梯度提升决策树虽然提供强大的表格基准，但其以特征为中心的分裂机制不能显式建模相似历史观测的检索。

Method: RowNet通过成对相似性特征将查询属性表示为与标记属性的记忆库的对比。第一检索层仅从特征相似性估计粗略目标。第二层用目标一致性特征增强记忆比较，并使用多个学习的注意力头来检索互补的可比集合。最后的专家混合模块结合学习门控、残差校正、熵正则化和头部多样性正则化来产生预测。

Result: RowNet在房地产每平米价格预测任务上实现了基于检索的神经网络架构，通过双层检索机制和专家混合模块有效捕捉了可比属性的检索和组合。

Conclusion: RowNet成功地将检索机制融入神经网络架构中，为房地产估值问题提供了一种新的解决方案，该架构能够显式建模可比属性的检索过程，并通过多头注意力和专家混合模块增强了预测能力。

Abstract: Real estate valuation is a structured regression problem in which prices are governed by heterogeneous feature types, sparse regional effects, nonlinear interactions, and the practical logic of comparable properties. Standard multilayer perceptrons treat each row as an isolated vector and must learn locality, scale sensitivity, and categorical matching from supervision alone. Gradient-boosted decision trees provide strong tabular baselines, but their feature-centric splitting mechanism does not explicitly model the retrieval of similar historical observations.
  This paper presents RowNet, a retrieval-based neural architecture for real estate price-per-square-meter prediction. RowNet represents a query property through pairwise similarity features against a memory bank of labeled properties. A first retrieval layer estimates a coarse target from feature-only similarities. A second layer augments the memory comparison with target-consistency features and uses multiple learned attention heads to retrieve complementary comparable sets. A final mixture-of-experts module combines learned gating, residual correction, entropy regularization, and head-diversity regularization to produce the prediction.

</details>


### [74] [On Out-of-sample Embedding in UMAP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04451)
*Mohammad Tariqul Islam,Jason W. Fleischer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过优化原始k近邻图中的成对相互作用，解决了UMAP算法在添加样本外点时的“排斥效应”，并证明了参数化UMAP在复杂数据集上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UMAP算法在处理样本外点时存在“排斥效应”，即将新点错误地放置在聚类边缘而非其相关邻居所在的内部，这一问题限制了其在实际应用中的表现。

Method: 通过优化原始k近邻图中的成对相互作用来克服排斥效应，并采用参数化的UMAP方法，同时使用可信度、最近邻分类器以及分析嵌入中的吸引力和排斥力来表征不同的UMAP方法。

Result: 参数化UMAP比非参数算法获得更好的嵌入效果，特别是在复杂数据（如医学图像）上表现更优，并且在使用参数化UMAP嵌入数据时，排斥效应能够自然得到缓解。

Conclusion: 参数化UMAP不仅能有效解决样本外点添加时的排斥效应问题，还能在复杂数据集上提供更优的嵌入质量，为UMAP算法的实际应用提供了改进方案。

Abstract: Neighbor embedding algorithms reveal correlations in high-dimensional data by constructing an equivalent graph representation in a lower-dimensional space. An increasingly popular algorithm is Uniform Manifold Learning and Projection (UMAP), which uses algebraic topology to map distances between the two spaces. While it works well on many types of data sets, UMAP has trouble adding out-of-sample points to a pre-existing mapping. In particular, UMAP often places new points on the periphery of the found clusters, rather than in their interiors with their correlated neighbors. Here, we overcome this ``repulsion effect'' by optimizing pairwise interactions within the original k-nearest-neighbor graph. Moreover, we show that parameterizing UMAP obtains better embeddings than non-parametric algorithms, particularly as the data gets more complex (e.g., medical images). We also show that the repulsion effect is naturally mitigated when a parameterized UMAP is employed to embed the data. We characterize different UMAP approaches using trustworthiness, nearest neighbor classifiers, and by analyzing attractive and repulsive forces in the embeddings.

</details>


### [75] [ParetoPilot: Zero-Surrogate Offline Multi-Objective Optimization via Infer-Perturb-Guide Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04468)
*Ruiqing Sun,Sen Yang,Dawei Feng,Bo Ding,Yijie Wang,Huaimin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ParetoPilot，一种无需代理模型的离线多目标优化扩散框架。该方法通过引入IPG引擎，在无条件去噪步骤中隐式推断目标方向并构建正交化的扰动向量，在51个任务上超越14个基线方法，同时保护数据隐私并提升了超体积和Pareto前沿覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离线多目标优化生成方法主要依赖外部代理模型，这引入了显著的计算开销、易受欺骗性评估影响，且偏离了主流生成模型与条件联合训练的范式。为了消除这些瓶颈，本文旨在探索一种零代理（zero-surrogate）的解决方案。

Method: 提出ParetoPilot框架，核心是嵌入在无条件去噪步骤中的Infer-Perturb-Guide (IPG) 引擎。该方法：1) 通过匹配条件和无条件噪声预测隐式推断瞬时目标方向；2) 数学正交化平行引力场（保证收敛）和边缘感知排斥力（保证多样性），构建动态退火的扰动向量；3) 通过标准无分类器引导(CFG)无缝引导生成过程。

Result: 在51个任务上的广泛实验表明，ParetoPilot优于14个最先进的基于代理和逆向生成基线方法。通过消除辅助代理训练，该方法在保护数据隐私的同时，实现了超体积改进和稳健的Pareto前沿覆盖。

Conclusion: ParetoPilot成功实现了无需代理模型的离线多目标优化，充分利用了预训练扩散模型中固有的条件先验。该方法不仅解决了依赖代理模型带来的计算开销和欺骗性评估问题，还在多个任务上展现了优越性能，为离线多目标优化领域提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Offline multi-objective optimization (Offline MOO) aims to discover novel Pareto-optimal designs based on static datasets without expensive environment interactions. While recent generative methods have achieved notable success, they predominantly rely on external surrogate models. This dependency introduces significant computational overhead, suffers from deceptive evaluations, and deviates from the prevailing paradigm of jointly training mainstream generative models with conditions. To address these bottlenecks, we propose ParetoPilot, a novel zero-surrogate diffusion framework for offline MOO. ParetoPilot fully leverages the conditional priors inherently embedded within pre-trained diffusion models. At its core, the framework introduces the Infer-Perturb-Guide (IPG) engine, which is seamlessly interleaved within the unconditional denoising steps of the reverse generation process. First, it implicitly infers the instantaneous objective direction by matching conditional and unconditional noise predictions. Next, it mathematically orthogonalizes a parallel gravity field for strict convergence and an edgeness-aware repulsive force for mutual diversity, creating a dynamically annealed perturbation vector. Finally, this perturbed target seamlessly steers the generation process via standard Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG). Extensive experiments across 51 tasks demonstrate that ParetoPilot outperforms 14 state-of-the-art surrogate-based and inverse generative baselines. By eliminating auxiliary proxy training, our approach preserves data privacy while achieving hypervolume improvement and robust Pareto front coverage.

</details>


### [76] [ChessMimic: Per-Rating Transformer Models for Human Move, Clock, and Outcome Prediction in Online Blitz Chess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04473)
*Thomas Johnson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了ChessMimic系统，包含三个小型仅编码器Transformer模型，分别用于国际象棋的人类走法预测、思考时间预测和比赛结果预测，在人类走法预测准确率上超越了Maia-2，并在结果预测上达到0.78的AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有国际象棋AI模型难以精确模拟不同水平人类玩家的行为特征。论文旨在构建能够更准确预测人类走法选择、思考时间和比赛结果的系统，实现更细粒度的技能水平校准。

Method: 采用三个独立的仅编码器Transformer模型（走法预测、思考时间预测、结果预测），以棋局位置、近期走法历史、玩家等级分和时钟状态为条件输入。为每100分等级分段训练独立模型实例，以参数效率换取更精确的技能校准。

Result: 走法预测模型在所有等级分段上超越Maia-2，900万参数模型准确率介于Maia-3-5M和Maia-3-23M之间；结果预测模型AUC达到0.78，优于Maia-2和基于材料、等级分、时间的逻辑回归；思考时间模型达到Pearson r=0.41、Spearman rho=0.50、MAE=4.10秒的性能。

Conclusion: ChessMimic成功实现了多维度的人类国际象棋行为建模，提供了更精确的按技能水平校准的预测能力。系统已发布公开演示并开源代码、权重和数据处理流程，为研究和应用提供了可用的工具。

Abstract: We present ChessMimic, a system of three small encoder-only transformers - for move, thinking-time, and outcome prediction - conditioned on the position, recent move history, player rating, and clock state. We fit a separate instance of each model per 100-Elo rating band, trading parameter efficiency for sharper per-skill calibration. On a held-out month-wide slice of Lichess Rated Blitz games ChessMimic's human move prediction accuracy outperforms Maia-2 in every Elo band. Compared to Maia-3, our 9M parameter model's accuracy sits between Maia-3-5M and Maia-3-23M without the additional complexity of Geometric Attention Bias. In addition to the move matching model, we also train a game outcome model that conditions not only on the position, but also player ratings, time control, and remaining clock times. The outcome model achieves an AUC of 0.78 out of sample, beating Maia-2 as well as logistic regressions based on material, ratings, and clock time. Finally, we train a clock model that predicts human thinking times. The clock model provides a usable but non-SOTA per-ply think-time signal under ALLIE-style filters (Pearson r = 0.41, Spearman rho = 0.50, MAE 4.10 s, against ALLIE's reported r = 0.70), with the residual gap concentrated in per-position bucket sharpness rather than bucket-marginal calibration. A public demo is at 1e4.ai and we release code, per-band weights, and the C++ data-filter pipeline code in GitHub.

</details>
