We introduce Hamiltonian Causal Models (HCMs), a trajectory-level framework in which observed variables interact with local environments and interventions act as controls of Hamiltonian mechanisms. HCMs separate immutable equations of motion from intervenable mechanisms and define causal effects as discrepancies between interventional path laws. A key motivation for HCMs is their natural interface with non-equilibrium thermodynamics. Entropy production quantifies the irreversibility of a process and is a central causal observable: it is estimable from data and witnesses causal effects along the system's evolution that are invisible to endpoint and cumulative versions of the standard average treatment effect. As in physics, cause and effect are not primitives of the relation between two random variables but arise from the non-invertibility of the thermodynamic arrow. With this, our paper reconciles the language of statistical causal models and non-stationary thermodynamics, offering new tools to describe causality in a wide range of physical systems.

</details>


### [113] [Invariant Gradient Alignment for Robust Reasoning Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05025)
*Zehua Cheng,Wei Dai,Jiahao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出不变梯度对齐（IGA）框架，通过逻辑同构集和梯度掩码对齐跨域梯度，显著提升大语言模型的分布外泛化能力和逻辑一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型存在捷径学习问题，在语义表面与训练数据不同但逻辑结构相同的分布外（OOD）输入上系统性失效，这损害了将思维链推理迁移至小型模型的知识蒸馏流程。

Method: 提出不变梯度对齐（IGA）框架，包含三个核心创新：（i）构建跨不同语义领域但逻辑结构相同的逻辑同构集；（ii）设计可微的连续梯度冲突掩码，抑制高跨域梯度方差的参数维度，保留不变方向；（iii）通过截断SVD投影将掩码梯度映射回LoRA低秩流形，保持参数效率。

Result: IGA在四个基准测试上超越八个基线方法，相比ERM-SFT准确率提升高达14.3个百分点，逻辑一致性分数从0.142改善至0.031，表征不变性提升四倍。理论上证明IGA具有比经验风险最小化更紧的OOD泛化界，且在温和正则条件下以标准SGD速率收敛。

Conclusion: IGA有效解决了大语言模型的捷径学习问题，通过梯度对齐实现了更好的分布外泛化能力，为知识蒸馏和思维链推理迁移提供了更可靠的训练框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) suffer from shortcut learning: they systematically fail on out-of-distribution (OOD) inputs whose semantic surface differs from training data, even when the logical structure is identical. This undermines knowledge distillation pipelines that transfer chain-of-thought reasoning to smaller students. We introduce Invariant Gradient Alignment (IGA), a training framework that aligns gradient updates across semantically diverse but logically isomorphic examples via three innovations: (i) Logical Isomer Sets, groups of problems sharing identical logical structure across distinct semantic domains (mathematics, medicine, law, science); (ii) a differentiable \emph{Continuous Gradient Conflict Mask}, that suppresses parameter dimensions with high cross-domain gradient variance while preserving invariant directions; and (iii) a truncated SVD projection of the masked gradient back onto the LoRA low-rank manifold, maintaining parameter efficiency throughout. Theoretically, IGA yields tighter OOD generalization bounds than ERM, scaling with the number of isomer domains, and converges at the standard SGD rate under mild regularity. Empirically, IGA outperforms eight baselines across four benchmarks with accuracy gains up to 14.3 pp over ERM-SFT and a Logical Consistency Score of 0.031 versus 0.142 -- a fourfold improvement in representational invariance.

</details>


### [114] [Towards Efficient and Evidence-grounded Mobility Prediction with LLM-Driven Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05130)
*Linyao Chen,Qinlao Zhao,Zechen Li,Mingming Li,Likun Ni,Jinyu Chen,Yuhao Yao,Xuan Song,Noboru Koshizuka,Hiroki Kobayashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了AgentMob，一个无需训练的LLM智能体框架，通过自适应证据收集决策机制进行个体移动预测，在模糊预测场景中显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的监督序列模型需要特定任务训练且缺乏透明度；而基于LLM的方法虽然提升了可解释性，但依赖静态提示和单次推理，难以处理移动信号微弱或冲突的情况。

Method: 提出训练-free的LLM驱动智能体框架AgentMob，将下一位置预测建模为自适应证据控制决策过程。常规案例通过基于历史规律性的快速路径解决，模糊案例则触发迭代工具使用，整合最近轨迹、历史行为、停留-移动可能性和地理证据。

Result: 在三个移动数据集上，AgentMob在无训练LLM方法中表现最优。GPT-5.4在BW上Acc@1达71.42%，YJMob100K上33.14%，上海ISP上33.50%。在BW非快速路径案例中，LLM控制器将Acc@1从统计基线的30.65%提升至48.62%。

Conclusion: AgentMob的核心优势在于通过自适应证据收集解决模糊预测问题，证明了LLM智能体在移动预测任务中处理复杂、不确定场景的有效性。

Abstract: Individual-level mobility prediction is central to urban simulation, transportation planning, and policy analysis. Supervised sequence models achieve strong accuracy but require task-specific training and offer limited decision-level transparency. Recent LLM-based methods improve interpretability, yet mostly rely on static prompts and single-pass inference, limiting their ability to seek additional evidence when mobility signals are weak or conflicting. We propose \method{}, a training-free LLM-driven agent framework that formulates next-location prediction as adaptive evidence-controlled decision making. \method{} resolves routine cases through a fast path based on historical regularity, while ambiguous cases trigger iterative tool use over recent trajectories, historical behavior, stay-move likelihood, and geographical evidence. Across three mobility datasets, AgentMob achieves the strongest overall performance among training-free LLM-based methods, with GPT-5.4 reaching 71.42\% Acc@1 on BW, 33.14\% on YJMob100K, and 33.50\% on Shanghai ISP. On BW non-fast-path cases, the LLM controller improves Acc@1 from 30.65\% to 48.62\% over a same-tool statistical baseline, showing that its main benefit lies in resolving ambiguous predictions through adaptive evidence gathering. Our code is available at https://github.com/Unknown-zoo/AgentMob.

</details>


### [115] [Prediction Under Imperfect Compression: A Theory of Approximate MDL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04834)
*Qian Li,Xinyu Mao,Shang-Hua Teng,Guangxu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了近似MDL在加性近似误差下仍能保证可靠的序列预测，且这一理论刻画是紧的：当正则化参数λ<1时会出现过拟合，乘性近似下模型选择失效，因此加性近似既充分又必要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典MDL预测理论表明精确优化MDL目标能提供强压缩保证，但实际机器学习通常只能近似优化目标函数。为填补这一理论与实际的差距，本文旨在解决一个基本问题：在何种形式的近似和正则化下，近似MDL仍能保证可靠的序列预测？

Method: 作者考虑平衡MDL目标λ·L(model)+L(data|model)的加性近似（误差为C）。对于λ≥1的情况，采用亲和力-望远镜论证（affinity-telescoping argument）；对于边界情况λ=1，采用基于精确静态MDL边界的似然比停止论证（likelihood-ratio stopping argument）。同时通过构造反例分析λ<1和乘性近似的情况。

Result: 证明了对于任何具有加性松弛C的近似，当λ≥1时，累积期望平方预测误差是有限的；当0<λ<1时，在可估计测度的通用类中会出现过拟合，导致无限累积期望误差；在乘性近似下，任何正则化区间λ>0中的模型选择都可能失败。

Conclusion: 经典MDL正则化对任意固定加性优化误差保持鲁棒性。强形式的模型复杂度正则化（λ≥1）是必要的，加性近似对于可靠的模型选择既充分又必要，而乘性近似则不可行。该工作为近似MDL框架提供了原则性刻画。

Abstract: Minimum Description Length (MDL) formalizes the principle of Occam's razor by optimizing the total description length: $L(\mathrm{model})+L(\mathrm{data} \ | \ \mathrm{model})$. For sequential prediction, the MDL method repeatedly selects a model with a minimum objective score of the observed prefix for the next step prediction. Classical MDL prediction theory shows that exact optimization of the MDL objective indeed provides a strong compression guarantee that supports reliable prediction. However, practical machine learning usually can only find models by approximately optimizing the objective function. To bridge this gap, this paper addresses the following fundamental question: Under what forms of approximation and regularization does approximate MDL still guarantee reliable sequential prediction? This work offers a principled characterization. We prove that for any approximation with additive slack $C$ of the more general form of the balanced MDL objective: $λ\cdot L(\mathrm{model})+L(\mathrm{data} \ | \ \mathrm{model})$, the cumulative expected squared prediction error is finite for all $λ\ge1$. The case $λ>1$ is proved by an affinity-telescoping argument, while the boundary case $λ=1$ is proved by a likelihood-ratio stopping argument based on exact static MDL bounds. Our results establish that classical MDL regularization remains robust to any fixed additive optimization error. Furthermore, we establish that our characterization of the approximate MDL framework is sharp: When $0<λ<1$, overfits can happen to incur infinite cumulative expected error in the universal class of estimable measures, and hence a strong form of model-complexity regularization is necessary. In addition, model selection may fail in every regularized regime $λ>0$, under multiplicative approximation, and thus, additive approximation is both sufficient and essential.

</details>


### [116] [Failed Reasoning Traces Tell You What Is Fixable (But Not by Reading Them)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05145)
*Nizar Islah,Istabrak Abbes,Irina Rish,Sarath Chandar,Eilif B. Muller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用失败推理轨迹中的可恢复性结构，通过三个问题级轨迹特征对失败进行聚类和表征，并基于此设计了训练无关的路由规则来选择合适的测试时干预策略，从而有效提升推理问题的解决率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当后训练语言模型在推理问题上失败时，通用的测试时扩展策略是增加计算进行更多尝试，而失败的轨迹通常被丢弃。作者认为这种做法浪费了关键信号：某些失败源于不幸运的采样（可通过更多尝试解决），而另一些是结构性的（无法通过重采样解决）。失败轨迹中编码了可恢复性结构，即哪些测试时干预可以挽救特定失败的推理时签名。

Method: 提出从可用干预结构中导出的三个问题级轨迹特征，这些特征从失败轨迹的分布签名而非文本中恢复可恢复性结构。这些特征用于：1)将失败聚类为稳定的类别；2)表征不同后训练方法的失败拓扑结构；3)支持训练无关的路由规则，选择合适的干预策略。方法在两个跨家族探针上进行了验证。

Result: 实验表明：1)特征对失败类别的聚类表征准确率达84.3±4.3%，比多数类基线高20%；2)路由规则在Steerable-Hard子集（重试不足且有界干预可达的失败）上将挽救率提升+12.2%；3)特征和路由规则在跨家族探针上具有良好的迁移性。

Conclusion: 三个轨迹特征将失败轨迹从被丢弃的数据转变为诊断对象，支持测试时路由和后训练分析，且无需训练时或权重空间访问权限。这证明了失败轨迹中包含有价值的可恢复性结构信息，可用于指导测试时干预策略的选择。

Abstract: When post-trained language models fail on reasoning problems, the common test-time-scaling response is to spend more compute on additional attempts, and the failed traces play no further role. We argue this discards a crucial signal; some failures come from unlucky sampling, where more rollouts help, while others are structural and resist resampling regardless of budget. We propose that failed traces encode recoverability structure: the inference-time signature of which test-time interventions can rescue a given failure. Three problem-level trajectory features, derived from the structure of available interventions, recover this structure from the distributional signature of failed rollouts, not their text. They cluster failures into stable regimes, characterize the failure topography of different post-training methods ($84.3{\pm}4.3\%$ accuracy, $+20\%$ over a majority-class baseline), and support a training-free routing rule that lifts rescue by $+12.2\%$ on the deployment-relevant Steerable-Hard subset (failures where retry is insufficient and a bounded intervention is reachable). The features and the routing rule transfer across two cross-family probes. The same three features thus convert failed traces from discarded data into a diagnostic object, supporting test-time routing and post-training analysis without training-time or weight-space access.

</details>


### [117] [Reinforcement Learning from Rich Feedback with Distributional DAgger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05152)
*Rishabh Agrawal,Jacob Fein-Ashley,Paria Rashidinejad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DistIL方法，基于DAgger的分布变体使用前向交叉熵目标函数，有效利用丰富反馈（如执行轨迹、工具输出、专家修正等）改进推理模型，在科学推理、编程和数学问题等多个领域优于现有RLVR和自蒸馏基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流的RLVR方法仅使用单一比特的奖励信号（答案正确性），但许多场景提供了丰富的反馈信息（执行轨迹、工具输出、专家修正、模型自评估等）。现有基于反向KL或Jensen-Shannon散度的RL自蒸馏方法无法保证策略的单调改进，即使专家具有更高奖励，其更新也可能增加差动作的概率。

Method: 提出基于DAgger分布变体的方法，学习者在当前策略访问的状态上具有专家分布的本地访问权限。设计简单的前向交叉熵目标函数，支持黑盒专家，其序列级梯度通过将未来的专家-学生差异传播回早期决策来执行丰富的信用分配。该方法优化教师加权成功似然的下界。

Result: 理论上证明前向交叉熵目标函数支持单调策略改进并享有遗憾保证，而基于反向KL或Jensen-Shannon的方法无法保证单调策略改进。实验上，DistIL在科学推理、编程和解决复杂数学问题等多个领域优于RLVR和RL自蒸馏基线，改善了Pass@N性能。

Conclusion: DistIL提供了一种有效利用丰富反馈信号的推理模型训练框架，通过前向交叉熵目标函数实现单调策略改进和更好的理论保证，在多个实际应用领域中取得优异性能，为推理模型训练提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Reasoning models have advanced rapidly, but the dominant reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) recipe remains surprisingly narrow: sample many responses and reward each with a single bit indicating whether the final answer is correct. Yet many settings provide rich feedback, including execution traces, tool outputs, expert corrections, and model self-evaluations. We study how to use such feedback through a distributional variant of the classic imitation learning algorithm DAgger, where the learner has local access to an expert distribution on states visited by the current policy. This yields a simple forward cross-entropy objective that admits a blackbox expert and whose sequence-level gradient {conduct rich credit assignment by propagating} future expert-student disagreement back to earlier decisions. We show that prior RL with self-distillation objectives based on reverse KL or Jensen-Shannon fail to guarantee monotonic policy improvement: even when the expert has higher reward, their updates may increase probability on worse actions. In contrast, we show that forward cross-entropy admits monotonic policy improvement and enjoys guarantees on regret. We further show that our objective optimizes a lower bound on teacher-weighted likelihood of success, leading to improved Pass@N. Empirically, our approach, DistIL, improves over RLVR and RL with self-distillation baselines across a variety of domains: scientific reasoning, coding, and solving hard mathematical problems.

</details>


### [118] [Rethinking Incompleteness: Formalizing Protocol Divergence and Train-Once Learning for Robust IMVC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04857)
*Haolu Liu,Xiyue Wang,Xuanting Xie,Liangjian Wen,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了不完整多视图聚类(IMVC)评估中的一个根本性漏洞：相同缺失率的协议在完整样本比例上可能相差50倍，导致完全不同的学习效果。作者提出了CRAFT模型，通过架构设计而非损失函数来实现对缺失数据的鲁棒性，单次训练即可适应多种缺失模式，性能匹配或超越基线模型，同时减少8.8倍训练开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IMVC评估范式存在根本性缺陷：针对不同缺失数据配置重新训练独立模型，且仅用缺失率来刻画数据不完整性。本文发现相同名义缺失率的协议在完整样本比例上可相差高达50倍，形成截然不同的学习机制，这一现象被形式化为"不完整性发散"(incompleteness divergence)。理论证明，对于一类基于重构的目标函数，当完整样本比例低于临界阈值时，学习在结构上变得不适定，导致近乎随机的性能表现。

Method: 提出CRAFT（完整数据鲁棒注意力掩码融合Transformer）模型，将鲁棒性负担从损失函数转移至架构设计。两个核心特性：(i)样本独立性，消除对完整样本共现的依赖；(ii)掩码感知变长融合，通过注意力掩码仅聚合已观测视图。该设计允许单一模型在完整数据上训练一次，即可在推理时泛化至多种缺失模式，无需重新训练。

Result: 在七个基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，CRAFT匹配或优于针对每种配置单独训练的基线模型，同时将训练开销减少8.8倍。证明了缺失数据鲁棒性可作为架构的内在属性实现。作者还提供了imvc-audit工具包用于系统评估缺失数据协议。

Conclusion: 本研究从根本上挑战了IMVC领域的传统评估范式，揭示了缺失率作为单一指标不足以刻画数据不完整性。通过理论分析建立了重构目标函数的性能下界，并提供了实际的架构解决方案。CRAFT证明了架构层面的鲁棒性设计可以替代传统的重新训练策略，为IMVC研究提供了新的方法论基础和评估工具。

Abstract: Standard IMVC evaluation retrains separate models for different missing-data configurations. We show that this paradigm obscures a fundamental vulnerability: missing rate alone is insufficient to characterize data incompleteness. Specifically, we show that protocols with identical nominal missing rates can differ by up to $50\times$ in their proportion of fully observed samples, inducing drastically different learning regimes. We formalize this phenomenon as incompleteness divergence, providing measures that capture structural disparities across missing-data protocols. We further prove that for a broad class of reconstruction-based objectives, learning becomes structurally ill-posed when the proportion of complete samples falls below a critical threshold, leading to near-random performance. To bypass this theoretical bound, we propose CRAFT (Complete-data Robust Attention-masked Fusion Transformer). CRAFT shifts the burden of robustness from the loss function to the architecture via two key properties: (i) per-sample independence, which removes reliance on complete-sample co-occurrence, and (ii) mask-aware variable-length fusion, which aggregates only observed views through attention masking. This design allows a single model, trained once on complete data, to generalize to diverse missing patterns at inference time without retraining. Extensive experiments on seven benchmarks show that CRAFT matches or outperforms per-configuration baselines while reducing training overhead by $8.8\times$, demonstrating that robustness to missing data can be achieved as an inherent architectural property. Code (CRAFT) and our imvc-audit toolkit are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/CRAFT-BF80/ and https://anonymous.4open.science/r/imvc-audit-8263/.

</details>


### [119] [Provably Reduced Sample Cost in Prior-Guided Hyperparameter Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04866)
*Leona Hennig,Jasmin Brandt,Lukas Fehring,Barbara Hammer,Marius Lindauer,Marcel Wever*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为带先验的多保真度超参数优化（HPO）建立了分布相关的样本复杂度边界，证明了信息丰富的先验可以将所需评估次数减少高达90%，为计算高效的绿色AutoML提供了理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模超参数优化在自动化机器学习中消耗大量计算资源，引发了对可扩展性和能效的担忧。现有方法虽然启发式地利用先验信息加速优化，但缺乏对先验信息如何定量降低样本复杂度的理论刻画。

Method: 通过固定预算最佳臂识别的形式化视角，直接对臂均值（即配置性能）建模先验，推导出显式的、分布相关的误差边界，量化先验与评估预算之间的关系。

Result: 理论分析表明，信息丰富的先验（概率质量集中在近最优臂上）能减少所需评估次数，而无信息或误导性先验则恢复到基线性能。在合成基准测试和LCBench上的实验验证了理论结果，在保持解质量的同时实现了高达90%的预算减少。

Conclusion: 研究结果为先验引导和计算高效的绿色AutoML提供了有原则的理论基础，首次定量刻画了先验信息与样本复杂度之间的关系。

Abstract: Large-scale hyperparameter optimization (HPO) in automated machine learning (AutoML) consumes substantial computational resources, raising growing concerns about scalability and energy efficiency. Existing methods use prior information heuristically to accelerate both black-box and multi-fidelity settings, but they lack a characterization of how prior informativeness quantitatively reduces sample complexity. In this work, we provide the first distribution-dependent sample complexity bounds for multi-fidelity HPO with priors through the formal lens of fixed-budget best-arm identification. By modeling priors directly over arm means as configuration performance, we derive explicit, distribution-dependent error bounds that quantify the relationship between priors and evaluation budget. Our analysis shows that informative priors, which concentrate probability mass on near-optimal arms, yield reductions in the number of required evaluations, whereas baseline performance is recovered with uninformative or misleading priors. We conduct proof-of-concept experiments on a synthetic benchmark and on LCBench, a common multi-fidelity HPO benchmark for deep learning, to confirm our theoretical results, achieving up to 90% budget reduction while retaining solution quality. Together, our results provide a principled foundation for prior-guided and compute-efficient green AutoML.

</details>


### [120] [Towards Pretraining Text Encoders for TabPFN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04876)
*Mustafa Tajjar,Alexander Pfefferle,Lennart Purucker,Frank Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TabPFN文本适配器，通过将文本嵌入映射到TabPFN嵌入空间的短序列令牌，解决了标准PCA压缩造成的信息瓶颈问题，同时保持TabPFN的数值特征处理优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格基础模型（如TabPFN）无法原生处理高基数文本特征。标准流程使用语言模型嵌入文本并通过PCA压缩为少量标量特征，这造成了信息瓶颈：大部分嵌入维度被丢弃，压缩表示需由TabPFN特征编码器重新扩展。端到端方案可避免PCA，但需要大量包含文本单元的预训练数据且性能通常不如在大规模合成数据上预训练的表格基础模型。

Method: 受LLaVA（视觉到LLM令牌投影）和TableGPT类系统（表格到LLM令牌投影）等模态对齐方法启发，提出TabPFN文本适配器（文本到TFM令牌投影）。冻结句子编码器和TabPFN，仅训练一个轻量级适配器，将文本嵌入映射为TabPFN嵌入空间中的短序列令牌。

Result: 该设计消除了PCA瓶颈，保留了TabPFN在数值特征处理上的优势，比端到端文本-表格流程训练效率更高。

Conclusion: TabPFN文本适配器成功实现了文本模态与表格基础模型的有效对齐，在避免信息损失的同时保持了计算效率，为表格基础模型处理文本特征提供了一种轻量且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models, such as TabPFN, achieve strong performance on tabular datasets with numerical and categorical data, but do not natively handle high-cardinality text features. Standard pipelines, therefore, embed text with a language model and compress the resulting vectors with PCA into a small number of scalar features before inputting them into TabPFN. This creates an information bottleneck: most embedding dimensions are discarded, and the compressed representation must then be expanded again by TabPFN's feature encoder. End-to-end alternatives can avoid PCA, but they require large amounts of pretraining data containing text cells and usually perform subpar compared to tabular foundation models that were pretrained on large amounts of synthetic data. Inspired by modality-alignment approaches like LLaVA (vision-to-LLM token projection) and TableGPT-style systems (table-to-LLM token projection), we introduce the TabPFN Text Adapter (text-to-TFM token projection). We freeze both the sentence encoder and TabPFN, and train only a lightweight adapter that maps text embeddings into a short sequence of tokens in TabPFN's embedding space. This design removes the PCA bottleneck, preserves TabPFN's numerical strengths, and is more efficient to train than end-to-end text-tabular pipelines.

</details>


### [121] [Worker Utility as Hysteresis: A Preisach Model of Transaction Acceptance in Gig Labour Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04916)
*Piotr Frydrych*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将零工交易中的工人决策建模为Preisach磁滞模型，通过双输出神经网络估计接受/拒绝效用面，实现了成本降低21.3%同时填充率提升9.7个百分点的优化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零工经济中工人效用无法直接观测，仅能观察到接受或拒绝的二进制结果。这种结构特征促使研究者寻找合适的潜在偏好表示方法，以更好地理解和预测工人的决策行为。

Method: 采用Preisach磁滞算子建模聚合输出，设计双输出神经网络（共享层256->128，边际损失约束U_1≥U_0）估计接受效用U_1(X)和拒绝效用U_0(X)两个潜在效用面。分类时计算Preisach间隙U_1(X)-U_0(X)，结合价格-阈值编码输入XGBoost分类器。

Result: 在36,891笔零工交易上达到Jaccard=0.827和ROC AUC=0.799，价格-阈值编码相比原始效用特征提升AUC 11.0个百分点。模型验证了磁滞预测的方向不对称性：价格下降对完成率的负面影响大于等价上升的正面影响。应用模型建议后，总工资账单降低21.3%，预期填充率提升9.7个百分点。

Conclusion: Preisach模型成功捕捉了工人决策的磁滞特性，实现了成本节约与填充率提升的双重优化。对于74.2%的交易（接受概率>0.80），可通过降薪实现成本节约（中位31%）；对于剩余25.4%的交易，中位7%的加薪可恢复43个百分点的接受率。显式建模无差异区间是实现双向优化的关键。

Abstract: Worker utility is not observed -- only its consequence is. Each gig transaction produces a single bit: accepted or rejected. We argue this structure points directly to the Preisach hysteresis model as the natural representation of latent worker preferences. The Preisach operator models aggregate output as an integral over a population of binary threshold elements -- precisely the structure that emerges when heterogeneous workers each carry a private acceptance wage. We estimate two latent utility surfaces: acceptance utility U_1(X) and rejection utility U_0(X), via a dual-output neural network (shared layers 256->128, margin loss enforcing U_1 >= U_0). Classification reduces to the Preisach gap U_1(X) - U_0(X), passed into an XGBoost classifier alongside clip-stabilised price-to-threshold encodings. On 36,891 gig transactions, this pipeline achieves Jaccard = 0.827 and ROC AUC = 0.799. The price-to-threshold encoding accounts for +11.0 pp AUC over raw utility features. The model confirms the directional asymmetry hysteresis predicts: price decreases depress completion rates more than equivalent increases raise them. Applied to the full dataset, the model's recommendations simultaneously reduce the total wage bill by 21.3% and increase expected fill rate by 9.7 pp. For 74.2% of transactions, P(accept) already exceeds 0.80; reducing the wage keeps it above threshold (mean post-cut P = 0.972), releasing cost savings (median 31%). For the remaining 25.4%, a median 7% wage increase recovers +43 pp acceptance. A model without an explicit indifference zone cannot execute both moves simultaneously.

</details>


### [122] [Toward Multi-Domain and Long-Tailed Quantization via Feature Alignment and Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04920)
*Chin-Yuan Yeh,Ting-An Chen,De-Nian Yang,Ming-Syan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了EmaQ及其扩展EmaQ-LT方法，分别解决多领域对齐和长尾分布下的神经网络量化问题，在低比特量化中取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经网络量化方法主要针对单领域和类别平衡数据设计，而实际应用场景常面临领域偏移或严重的类别不平衡问题，这些具有挑战性的设置尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出高效多领域对齐量化(EmaQ)方法：通过基于累积分布函数(CDF)的投影对齐领域分布，并使用敏感性感知的权重聚合来稳定多领域量化。进一步扩展为EmaQ-LT方法：引入类别条件方差缩放和基于置信度的逻辑调整，以缓解长尾分布中多数类别的过度自信问题。

Result: 在标准数据集、多领域数据集(Office-31、Digits)和长尾分布数据集(SynDigits-LT、CIFAR-10-LT、CIFAR-100-LT)上的实验表明，EmaQ和EmaQ-LT在领域偏移和类别不平衡条件下均实现了强健的低比特量化性能。理论分析建立了收敛保证并验证了所提出的敏感性和缩放机制的有效性。

Conclusion: EmaQ和EmaQ-LT成功解决了多领域对齐和长尾分布场景下的神经网络量化挑战，为实际应用中的资源受限设备提供了有效的量化解决方案。

Abstract: Quantizing deep neural networks is essential for efficient inference on resource-constrained devices. However, most existing methods are designed for single-domain and class-balanced data, leaving practical settings with domain shifts or severe class imbalance underexplored. We address these challenges with Efficient Multi-Domain Alignment Quantization (EmaQ), which aligns domain distributions through a CDF-based projection and uses sensitivity-aware weight aggregation to stabilize multi-domain quantization. We further extend EmaQ to EmaQ-LT for long-tailed quantization by introducing class-conditioned variance scaling and confidence-based logit adjustment to mitigate majority-class overconfidence. Theoretical analyses establish convergence guarantees and motivate the proposed sensitivity and scaling mechanisms. Experiments on standard, multi-domain (Office-31, Digits), and long-tailed (SynDigits-LT, CIFAR-10-LT, CIFAR-100-LT) benchmarks show that EmaQ and EmaQ-LT achieve strong low-bit performance under domain shift and class imbalance.

</details>


### [123] [Data Attribution in Large Language Models via Bidirectional Gradient Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04928)
*Frédéric Berdoz,Luca A. Lanzendörfer,Kaan Bayraktar,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对大语言模型的训练数据归因（TDA）新方法，通过双向梯度优化扰动模型，有效识别影响特定输出的训练数据，提升了模型的可解释性和问责制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在各领域的广泛应用，治理、问责和数据溯源问题日益突出。如何理解哪些训练数据对模型的输出产生了主要影响，仍然是一个根本性的开放问题。

Method: 本文扩展了逆向表述：如果模型在训练过程中看到了生成的输出，训练数据会受到怎样的影响？通过对生成的文本样本使用双向梯度优化（梯度上升和下降）来扰动基础模型，并测量训练样本损失的相应变化，从而实现训练数据归因。该框架支持任意数据粒度的归因，能够进行事实和风格层面的归因分析。

Result: 在具有已知数据集的预训练模型上与基线方法进行评估，结果表明该方法在影响力指标方面优于先前的工作。

Conclusion: 该方法增强了模型的可解释性，这是构建负责任AI系统的关键要求，为解决大语言模型的治理和问责问题提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed across diverse applications, raising critical questions for governance, accountability, and data provenance. Understanding which training data most influenced a model's output remains a fundamental open problem. We address this challenge through training data attribution (TDA) for auto-regressive LLMs by expanding upon the inverse formulation: How would training data be affected if the model had seen the generated output during training? Our method perturbs the base model using bidirectional gradient optimization (gradient ascent and descent) on a generated text sample and measures the resulting change in loss across training samples. Our framework supports attribution at arbitrary data granularity, enabling both factual and stylistic attribution. We evaluate our method against baselines on pretrained models with known datasets, and show that it outperforms previous work on influence metrics, thereby enhancing model interpretability, an essential requirement for accountable AI systems.

</details>


### [124] [Sequential Data Poisoning in LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04929)
*Jack Sanderson,Yihan Wang,Xiaoqian Lu,Gautam Kamath,Yiwei Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出序列数据投毒威胁模型，揭示了多阶段后训练流程中存在的复合漏洞：多个攻击者分别攻击不同阶段时，单独评估看似威胁可忽略，但协同攻击会显著增强攻击效果，打破了单一攻击者假设下的安全评估假象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献假设数据投毒攻击发生在每个训练阶段，但忽略了多个攻击者的可能性。大语言模型后训练经历多个阶段（如SFT、RLHF/DPO），每个阶段从不同且可能不受信任的来源获取数据。现有研究未能全面评估整个后训练流程的可信度，特别是多阶段交互带来的复合漏洞。

Method: 提出序列数据投毒威胁模型，其中多个攻击者分别对SFT和偏好数据集进行投毒。在SFT→DPO管道中研究攻击贡献的加性效应，在SFT→PPO管道中研究攻击贡献的互补效应。通过实验验证固定投毒预算在不同阶段分配的效果优于单阶段集中使用。

Result: 发现了"单一攻击者错觉"现象：每个攻击者单独评估时威胁可忽略，但跨阶段协作时真实漏洞被暴露。在SFT→DPO管道中，攻击贡献呈加性：跨阶段分配投毒预算优于单阶段集中。在SFT→PPO管道中，攻击贡献呈互补性：单独的SFT或奖励模型投毒均失败，但组合后成功。

Conclusion: 对单个后训练阶段的安全分析系统性地低估了仅从阶段交互中产生的复合漏洞。多阶段后训练流程的安全评估需要考虑多攻击者协同攻击的场景，而不能仅依赖单一阶段的安全分析结果。

Abstract: LLM post-training proceeds through multiple stages, e.g., supervised fine-tuning (SFT) followed by reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) or direct preference optimization (DPO), where each stage draws data from different, potentially untrusted sources. Existing literature assumes data poisoning attacks may occur at each training stage, but neglects the possibility of multiple attackers. To study the trustworthiness of the entire post-training pipeline, we propose the threat model of sequential data poisoning, where multiple adversaries separately poison the SFT and preference datasets. Under this threat model, we identify the single-attacker illusion: each adversary, evaluated in isolation, appears to pose a negligible threat. Yet when adversaries collaborate across stages, the true vulnerability is revealed. In the SFT $\to$ DPO pipeline, their contributions are additive: splitting a fixed poison budget across stages outperforms concentrating it in either stage alone. In the SFT $\to$ PPO pipeline, their contributions are complementary: neither SFT nor reward model poisoning succeeds individually, yet their combination does. These findings show that security analyses of individual post-training stages systematically underestimate compound vulnerabilities that emerge only from their interaction. Code is available at https://github.com/jcksanderson/sequential-poisoning.

</details>


### [125] [Mean-based algorithms: A lower bound and regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04931)
*Julius Durmann,Amelie Kleber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对未知时间范围和仅有多臂老虎机反馈的设定，本文首次证明了均值算法学习速度的理论下界，提出了两种新的均值算法变体，并揭示了均值算法与无悔算法之间非平凡的重叠关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明均值算法在经济博弈中能收敛到近似纳什均衡，但在多臂老虎机反馈场景下收敛速度较慢，且缺乏在未知时间范围设定下的理论分析。因此需要在未知时间范围和仅有多臂老虎机反馈的设定下研究均值算法的理论极限和实际表现。

Method: 首先推导了算法定义序列γ_t的下界，从理论上限定了均值算法的学习速度；其次提出两种新算法：一种推广了ε-贪心算法，另一种将均值Exp3算法扩展到未知时间范围；最后分析均值算法与无悔算法的关系，探讨两类算法集合的交集特性。

Result: 获得了算法定义序列γ_t的首个下界，确定了均值算法学习速度的极限；实验表明新提出的均值算法虽略慢，但能与其他多臂老虎机反馈算法竞争；发现通过适当选择γ_t，存在既满足均值定义又具有无悔性质的算法。

Conclusion: 本文建立了均值算法在未知时间范围多臂老虎机设定下的理论基础，证明了其学习速度的理论极限，提供了实用的算法变体，并阐明了均值算法与无悔算法之间的内在联系，为这类算法的"可利用性"提供了新的理解视角。

Abstract: Mean-based algorithms are a class of online learning algorithms that assign low probability to actions with low average rewards. Recent work indicates these algorithms converge favorably to serially undominated actions, which approximate Nash equilibria in economic games. However, empirical studies also show slower convergence compared to established algorithms in bandit-feedback scenarios.
  We study mean-based algorithms when the time horizon is unknown and only bandit feedback is available. In this setting, we provide the first lower bound on the algorithm-defining sequence $γ_t$ that formally establishes a limit on how fast these algorithms can learn. Additionally, we propose two mean-based algorithms: one generalizes $ε$-greedy, and the other extends the mean-based Exp3 to unknown horizons. Our experiments show that mean-based algorithms, although slightly slower, can perform competitively with other bandit-feedback algorithms.
  We further analyze the relationship to no-regret algorithms. Depending on the choice of $γ_t$, the intersection with no-regret algorithms is non-trivial, and we show that algorithms exist that are both mean-based and no-regret. This adds context to the "exploitability" of this class of algorithms that previous contributions suggest.

</details>


### [126] [STaR-Quant: State-Time Consistent Post-Training Quantization for Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04945)
*Xin Yan,Aqiang Wang,Zhenglin Wan,Xingrui Yuand Ivor Tsang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出STaR-Quant，一种针对扩散大语言模型(DLLMs)的后训练量化框架，通过解决状态依赖的激活差异和时间误差累积问题，实现了优于强基线的低比特量化性能，并在速度和内存方面取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型(DLLMs)通过迭代掩码去噪生成文本，但其大模型尺寸和迭代去噪过程带来了巨大的内存和计算开销。虽然后训练量化(PTQ)可以解决部署效率问题，但在低比特量化时面临两个关键挑战：(1)状态依赖的激活差异，即掩码和非掩码token在去噪步骤中展现不同的激活分布；(2)时间误差累积，即量化误差在迭代解码过程中跨步骤累积。

Method: 提出STaR-Quant框架，包含两个核心组件：(1)状态引导激活变换(SGAT)，将掩码和非掩码token分配到不同的激活变换空间，同时保持统一的静态权重端变换；(2)时间注意力补偿(TAC)，通过轻量级块对角仿射映射纠正量化后的注意力表示，以缓解时间误差累积问题。

Result: 在代表性DLLMs上的实验表明，STaR-Quant在低比特权重-激活量化方面持续优于强PTQ基线，相比FP16部署实现了最高1.69倍的速度提升和3.14倍的内存节省。

Conclusion: STaR-Quant成功解决了DLLMs低比特量化中的状态依赖激活差异和时间误差累积问题，为DLLMs的高效部署提供了有效的量化解决方案，在保持模型性能的同时显著降低了计算和内存开销。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (DLLMs) have recently emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive LLMs by generating text through iterative masked denoising with bidirectional context. However, their large model sizes and iterative denoising process introduce substantial memory and computational overhead, motivating post-training quantization for efficient deployment. In this paper, we identify two key challenges for low-bit DLLM quantization: state-dependent activation disparity and temporal error accumulation. Masked and unmasked tokens exhibit different activation distributions within each denoising step, while quantization errors can accumulate across steps during iterative decoding. To address these challenges, we propose STaR-Quant, a state-time consistent PTQ framework for DLLMs. STaR-Quant introduces State-Guided Activation Transformation (SGAT) to assign masked and unmasked tokens to different activation transformation spaces with a unified static weight-side transformation. It further introduces Temporal Attention Compensation (TAC) to correct the quantized attention representation via a lightweight block-diagonal affine mapping. Experiments on representative DLLMs demonstrate that STaR-Quant consistently improves low-bit weight-activation quantization over strong PTQ baselines, while delivering up to 1.69x speedup and 3.14x memory saving over FP16 deployment.

</details>


### [127] [Be Fair! Can Machine Learning Engineering Agents Adhere to Fairness Constraints?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04971)
*Anna Richter,Julia Stoyanovich,Sebastian Schelter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有机器学习工程(MLE)智能体在敏感领域的应用存在责任缺口；初步评估显示，智能体生成的管道在预测质量和公平性方面均表现不稳定且劣于人工设计基准，需重新设计以支持人类指导和合规性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLE智能体虽能自动化构建端到端ML管道，但在敏感和受监管领域，这种抽象化造成了责任缺口：终端用户缺乏对影响正确性、鲁棒性、公平性和监管合规性的设计选择的可见性，而现有基准测试不足以评估MLE智能体在此类场景下的安全应用性。

Method: 提出了以责任为中心的评估框架的期望标准，并在黑色素瘤分类任务上开展了探索性研究，以跨肤色的公平性作为责任约束，对两个近期的MLE智能体进行评估。

Result: 智能体生成的管道表现出高方差，在预测质量和公平性两个维度上均持续低于人工设计的基线，即使使用了面向公平性的提示词，表现仍不理想。

Conclusion: 需要进一步研究重新设计MLE智能体，使其支持人类引导搜索过程，并能可靠地评估生成ML管道的合规性和质量，以确保在敏感领域的安全应用。

Abstract: Machine learning engineering (MLE) agents promise to automate end-to-end ML pipeline development from raw data and natural language instructions, potentially making ML accessible to non-technical domain experts. However, in sensitive and regulated domains, this abstraction creates a responsibility gap: end-users may lack visibility into design choices that affect correctness, robustness, fairness, and regulatory compliance. We argue that existing benchmarks are insufficient to assess whether MLE agents can be safely applied in such settings. We propose desiderata for a responsibility-centered evaluation framework and conduct an exploratory study on melanoma classification, focusing on fairness across skin tones as a responsibility constraint. When evaluating two recent MLE agents, we find that agent-generated pipelines show high variance and consistently underperform manually designed baselines in both predictive quality and fairness, despite fairness-oriented prompts. These preliminary results suggest that further research is needed towards redesigning MLE agents to allow humans to guide the search process and reliably assess the compliance and quality of the generated ML pipelines.

</details>


### [128] [AlphaQ: Calibration-Free Bit Allocation for Mixture-of-Experts Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04980)
*Wanqi Yang,Yuexiao Ma,Alexander Conzelmann,Xiawu Zheng,Michael W. Mahoney,T. Konstantin Rusch,Shiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了AlphaQ，一种无需校准数据的MoE模型混合精度量化方法。该方法基于重尾自正则化理论，通过测量专家权重的频谱重尾性来分配比特宽度，在Qwen1.5-MoE上仅用3.5比特平均精度即可实现接近全精度的准确率，并提供超过4倍的内存压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MoE模型混合精度量化方法依赖校准数据来估计专家重要性并确定比特分配，但前沿MoE大语言模型的原始训练数据通常是专有且不可访问的，导致校准集成为不完美的替代品，可能错误估计专家利用率并导致次优的比特分配。现代MoE模型中观察到的专家间质量差异显著，以及重尾自正则化（HT-SR）理论在不访问训练或测试数据情况下预测神经网络模型质量的成功，启发了本研究。

Method: AlphaQ基于HT-SR理论，遵循一个简单原则：具有更强重尾权重频谱的专家通常训练得更好，应分配更高的比特宽度；重尾结构较弱的专家可以更激进地量化。具体实现包括：1）测量每个专家的频谱重尾性；2）在全局比特预算约束下，求解一个预算约束优化问题来最小化总量化误差。该方法完全无需校准数据，仅依据权重分布特性进行比特分配。

Result: 在多个MoE模型上，AlphaQ在相同比特预算下持续优于基于校准的基线方法。特别是在Qwen1.5-MoE上，AlphaQ仅用3.5比特的平均专家精度就实现了接近全精度的准确率，同时提供超过4倍的内存压缩。

Conclusion: 研究表明，专家权重的频谱重尾性可以有效预测其质量，从而指导比特分配决策。AlphaQ证明了无需校准数据的MoE量化方法的可行性，在实际部署中具有显著优势，特别是当训练数据不可访问时。该方法为大语言模型的高效部署提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures scale model capacity through sparse expert activation, but their deployment remains memory-bound because all expert weights must reside in memory. Mixed-precision quantization can substantially reduce this footprint by assigning different bit-widths to different experts. Existing approaches, however, typically rely on calibration data to estimate expert importance and determine bit allocation. For frontier MoE LLMs, the original training data, and hence the true training distribution, is proprietary and inaccessible. As a result, calibration sets are inevitably imperfect surrogates, and this can misestimate expert utilization and lead to suboptimal bit allocation. Motivated by the substantial cross-expert quality variability observed in modern MoE models, and by the success of Heavy-Tailed Self-Regularization (HT-SR) theory at predicting neural network model quality without access to training or testing data, we propose AlphaQ, a calibration-free bit-allocation method for MoE quantization. AlphaQ draws on HT-SR theory and follows a simple principle: experts with more heavy-tailed weight spectra are typically better trained and hence should receive higher bit-widths, while experts with weaker heavy-tailed structure can be quantized more aggressively. AlphaQ operationalizes this principle by measuring expert-wise spectral heavy-tailedness and solving a budget-constrained optimization problem that minimizes total quantization error under a global bit-budget constraint. Across several MoE models, AlphaQ consistently outperforms calibration-based baselines under matched bit budgets. Notably, on Qwen1.5-MoE, AlphaQ achieves near full-precision accuracy with an average expert precision of only 3.5 bits, while delivering more than 4$\times$ memory compression. Our code is available at https://github.com/Superone77/AlphaQ.

</details>


### [129] [New Benchmarking Shows Limited Generalization Power of TCR Antigenic Epitope Prediction Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04994)
*Yiming Liao,Yiheng Li,Ning Jiang,Bo Li,Keke Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了两类严格定义且未见过的基准数据集，用于无偏评估TCR抗原特异性预测模型的性能和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的T细胞受体(TCR)抗原特异性预测模型缺乏足够的敏感性和特异性，无法广泛应用，主要限制在于缺乏严格定义且未见过的基准数据集来进行无偏评估。

Method: 描述了两类互补的数据集，这些数据集满足严格标准，能够为模型评估提供稳健的框架。

Result: 提出了符合标准的两类互补数据集，这些数据集为模型评估和下一代TCR-抗原预测算法开发提供了基础。

Conclusion: 这些数据集不仅为模型评估提供了稳健框架，也为下一代TCR-抗原预测算法的开发奠定了基础。

Abstract: Accurate computational prediction of T cell receptor (TCR) antigen specificity would transform the study of T cell biology and enable scalable immune engineering, yet existing models lack sufficient sensitivity and specificity for broad applications. A major limitation is the absence of rigorously defined, unseen benchmark datasets that allow unbiased evaluation of model performance and generalizability. Here, we describe two complementary classes of datasets that meet this criterion and argue that they provide both a robust framework for model assessment and a foundation for next-generation TCR-antigen prediction algorithm development.

</details>


### [130] [Enhancing the MADDPG Algorithm for Multi-Agent Learning via Action Inference and Importance Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05021)
*Marc Walden,Jason Liu,Shaashwath Sivakumar,Ryan Liu,Hamza Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对MADDPG算法提出两项改进：Action Inference机制和基于几何分布的重要性采样，在Predator-Prey任务上验证了改进效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体强化学习环境存在非平稳性问题，且智能体间缺乏对彼此意图的理解，现有MADDPG算法在稳定性和探索效率方面存在不足。

Method: 提出两项改进：1）Action Inference机制，使每个智能体能够预测其他智能体的预期动作；2）采用几何分布的重要性采样策略，在经验回放缓冲区中优先选择更近且信息量更大的经验。在PettingZoo库提供的离散动作Predator-Prey任务上进行评估。

Result: Action Inference有效提高了学习稳定性和智能体间的协作能力；基于几何分布的重要性采样相比标准MADDPG在探索效率方面有显著提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的两项改进均有效，能够提升MADDPG算法在多智能体环境中的性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: We investigate multi-agent deep reinforcement learning and propose two enhancements to the Multi-Agent Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (MADDPG) algorithm. First, we introduce a novel Action Inference mechanism that enables each agent to predict other agents' intended actions, thereby improving the accuracy and stability of its own policy. Second, we apply an importance sampling strategy, using geometric distribution, in the replay buffer to prioritize more recent and informative experiences, which helps mitigate the non-stationarity inherent in multi-agent environments. We evaluate both modifications on the discrete-action Predator-Prey task provided by the PettingZoo library, a flexible Python interface for general multi-agent reinforcement learning benchmarks. Our results indicate that Action Inference is effective in improving learning stability and inter-agent cooperation and that importance sampling using geometric distribution can lead to significant improvements in exploration efficiency over standard MADDPG. Code available at https://github.com/shaashwathsivakumar/MARL_Proj

</details>


### [131] [Validity Threats for Foundation Model Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05029)
*Gunnar König,Martin Pawelczyk,Ulrike von Luxburg,Sebastian Bordt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 基础模型研究无法低成本近似大规模实验：计算节省以有效性威胁为代价。本文提出因果推断评估框架，揭示不同研究策略的特征性有效性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型规模下受控实验成本过高，研究社区转向低成本的近似策略（代理实验、缩放定律、观察性研究、单次运行设计），但这些策略的有效性缺乏系统性审视。

Method: 将基础模型研究构建为因果推断问题，借鉴实证社会科学的四类有效性（统计有效性、内部有效性、外部有效性、构念有效性）建立评估框架，系统分析各研究策略的有效性威胁。

Result: 不同策略具有特征性有效性权衡：代理实验牺牲外部和构念有效性换取统计与内部有效性；观察性研究面临混淆与效应异质性；单次运行设计受处理单元间干扰影响。研究揭示了文献中被忽视的多项有效性威胁。

Conclusion: 提出的评估框架为研究者提供了审查基础模型研究设计有效性威胁的实用工具包，有助于在计算预算约束下做出更严谨的研究设计决策。

Abstract: Controlled experiments are the backbone of machine learning research, but at the scale of modern foundation models, they have become prohibitively expensive. Instead, the community increasingly relies on research strategies that approximate the ideal experiment at a fraction of the cost: proxy experiments and scaling laws, observational studies with publicly available models, and single-run designs that leverage variation within individual training runs. In this work, we argue that there is no free lunch when approximating large-scale experiments on a compute budget. Specifically, savings in compute come at the cost of validity threats -- hidden and sometimes untestable assumptions that, when violated, can invalidate research claims. To help navigate such threats, we propose an evaluation framework that casts foundation model research as a causal inference problem. Within this framework, we evaluate different research strategies through four types of validity adapted from the empirical social sciences -- statistical, internal, external, and construct validity. We find that each strategy comes with a characteristic validity profile: proxy experiments trade external and construct validity for statistical and internal validity; observational studies face confounding and effect heterogeneity; and single-run designs are strained by interference between treated units. This analysis reveals several validity threats that have received insufficient attention in the literature. Overall, our evaluation framework provides researchers with a practical toolkit for scrutinizing validity threats in foundation model research~designs.

</details>


### [132] [In-Context Graphical Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05042)
*Zehua Cheng,Wei Dai,Jiahao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ICG-I方法，结合自回归图Transformer与变量消除结构，在高树宽图和受挫拓扑上实现精确且可扩展的边际推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散图模型边际推断面临精确性与可扩展性的两难选择：精确算法在高树宽图上不可行，而迭代近似方法（如置信传播、变分方法）在受挫拓扑上缺乏收敛保证。论文认为这种对立源于归纳偏置的不匹配：迭代方法放弃了使精确推断正确的顺序消除结构。

Method: 引入In-Context Graphical Inference (ICG-I)方法：(1) 自回归图Transformer通过学习Tensor-Train压缩的中间因子来模拟变量消除过程；(2) 配备Dirichlet输出层；(3) 采用加权一致性预测(WCP)在拓扑偏移下提供校准的、分布无关的覆盖保证。

Result: 理论证明：TT压缩误差在自回归链中至多线性传播；Dirichlet-Multinomial损失是proper scoring rule；WCP在估计密度比下保持覆盖。实验结果：在标准实例上将MAE从最佳基线的0.041降至0.020；在N=500的受挫自旋玻璃上达到0.048（BP完全发散）。

Conclusion: ICG-I成功弥合了精确性与可扩展性的差距，通过恢复顺序消除结构并引入理论保证，在各类基准上实现了最先进的性能，特别是在传统方法失效的受挫拓扑上表现突出。

Abstract: Marginal inference in discrete graphical models forces a choice between exactness and scalability: exact algorithms are intractable for high-treewidth graphs, while iterative approximations (Belief Propagation, variational methods) sacrifice convergence guarantees on frustrated topologies. We argue that this dichotomy stems from a mismatched inductive bias: iterative methods abandon the sequential elimination structure that makes exact inference correct. We introduce In-Context Graphical Inference (ICG-I), an autoregressive Graph Transformer that restores this structure by mimicking Variable Elimination with learned, Tensor- Train-compressed intermediate factors, paired with a Dirichlet output layer and Weighted Conformal Prediction for calibrated, distribution-free coverage guarantees under topological shift. We prove that TT compression errors propagate at most lincarly through the autoregressive chain, that the Dirichlet-Multinomial loss is a proper scoring rule, and that WCP maintains coverage with a quantifiable degradation under estimated density ratios. We conducted intensive experiments to evaluate ICG-I and achieved state-of-the-art performance across all benchmarks. ICG-I reduces MAE from 0.041 (best baseline) to 0.020 on standard instances and achieves 0.048 on N=500 frustrated spin glasses where BP diverges entirely.

</details>


### [133] [Graph Cascades: Contagion-Based Mesoscopic Rewiring for Structure-Aware Graph Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05046)
*Meher Chaitanya,My Le,Luana Ruiz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Graph Cascades，一种用于GNN和图Transformer的中观尺度重连策略，通过基于传染的扩散过程在O(|V|+|E|)时间内构建辅助图，提升节点分类性能，特别是在异配性和中高同配性图上效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GNN和GT方法要么局限于局部边连接，要么采用全局注意力机制，缺乏对中观尺度图结构的有效捕获。作者希望找到一种介于局部和全局之间的结构表示方法，以更好地利用图的结构信息提升模型性能。

Method: 提出Graph Cascades方法：1）使用基于传染(contagion-based)的扩散过程；2）在O(|V|+|E|)时间复杂度内构建辅助图；3）通过多跳强化将具有重复多跳支持的节点对提升为直接邻居；4）理论刻画强化重连的有效条件，包括标签对齐性、随机块模型(SBM)同配性以及基于图有效电阻的中观连通性形式化。

Result: 在节点分类基准测试中，Graph Cascades提升了多种GNN和稀疏GT骨干网络的性能。最可靠的性能提升出现在异配性图和中高同配性图中。发现性能与重连图的结构属性之间存在紧密相关性。理论条件还识别了中观重连不受益的情况：低度规则图和存在结构瓶颈的图，预测与观察到的失效情况相符。

Conclusion: Graph Cascades成功填补了局部边和全局注意力之间的空白，为中观尺度图结构学习提供了有效的解决方案。理论分析不仅解释了方法的成功条件，也准确预测了失败场景，为实际应用提供了重要指导。该方法在异配性图上的优势尤其重要，因为这是传统GNN方法的弱项。

Abstract: We introduce Graph Cascades, a mesoscopic rewiring strategy for Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) and Graph Transformers (GTs) that captures intermediate-scale graph structure beyond purely local edges or fully global attention. Using contagion-based diffusion processes, Graph Cascades constructs, in O(|V|+|E|) time, an auxiliary graph where node pairs supported by repeated multi-hop reinforcement are promoted to direct neighbors. We theoretically characterize when reinforcement-based rewiring helps: sufficient conditions under which reinforcement-based edge selection is more label-aligned than direct adjacency, an SBM witness in which two-hop reinforcement is perfectly homophilic, and a formalization of mesoscopic connectivity via graph effective resistance. Empirically, across node-classification benchmarks, Graph Cascades improves multiple GNN and sparse-GT backbones, with the most reliable gains observed on heterophilic and moderate- to high-degree homophilic graphs. The theoretical conditions also identify regimes where mesoscopic rewiring is unlikely to be beneficial -- low-degree regular graphs and graphs with structural bottlenecks -- and these predictions match the observed failures. We additionally observe tight correlations between performance and structural properties in the rewired graphs.

</details>


### [134] [FLAGG: Flexible Autoregressive Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05067)
*Samuel Cognolato,Alessandro Sperduti,Luciano Serafini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了FLAGG框架，通过将一次性图生成模型与自回归方法灵活结合，克服了两种方法各自的局限性，在多种图规模和领域的生成质量上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度图生成方法分为两类：一次性模型联合生成节点和边，自回归模型顺序采样节点和边。前者难以生成大型图，后者在小图上表现不佳，两者均无法适用于所有图类别。需要一种灵活的方法来克服这些局限性。

Method: 提出FLAGG（Flexible Autoregressive Graph Generation）框架，使用一次性模型顺序生成图的各个部分。框架可将任意一次性模型转化为自回归形式，通过随机节点移除过程指定顺序策略，并训练插入模型来学习逆转该过程。以DiGress作为一次性模型进行实现。

Result: 在多个不同图规模和领域的数据集上进行评估，FLAGG方法在采样质量方面优于一次性模型和自回归模型两类基线方法。

Conclusion: FLAGG框架成功地将一次性图生成模型与自回归方法相结合，实现了灵活的图生成，在不同规模和拓扑结构的图领域都展现出优越性能，为通用图生成提供了解决方案。

Abstract: The Deep Graph Generation's panorama spans two extremes: one-shot and sequential models. The former generates nodes and edges jointly, while the latter samples them autoregressively. Each method performs better in different graph domains depending on size and topology, but neither is applicable to all graph categories. For instance, one-shot methods struggle with generating large graphs, while sequential methods underperform on smaller graphs. A possible way to overcome these limitations is to flexibly combine the two methods in a unique system. In this work, we propose the FLAGG (Flexible Autoregressive Graph Generation) framework, which sequentially generates portions of graphs with one-shot models. FLAGG can apply any one-shot model to make it autoregressive, allowing flexibility in choosing the sequential policy. This policy is specified through a stochastic node removal process, which an Insertion Model learns to reverse. We evaluate FLAGG with the DiGress one-shot model on several data sets of different graph sizes and domains. We show that the approach outperforms both one-shot and autoregressive baselines in terms of sampling quality.

</details>


### [135] [RIDE: An Open Dataset and Benchmark for Train Delay Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05070)
*Clément Elliker,Mathis Le Bail,Clément Mantoux,Jesse Read,Sonia Vanier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了RIDE，一个全国规模的比利时铁路网络列车延误预测开放数据集和基准测试，涵盖了2023-2025年的9450万次列车事件。基准测试显示，基于学习的方法明显优于非学习模型，其中图神经网络表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 列车延误预测对乘客和铁路运营商都至关重要，但由于缺乏标准化的数据集、预测目标和评估协议，该领域的进展难以评估。为了解决这一空白，需要建立一个统一的基准测试平台。

Method: 构建了RIDE开放数据集和基准测试，包含9450万列车事件、360万次旅程和3570万条天气记录（2023-2025年）。数据组织为分层流水线，提供可重用的中间关系数据集和模型就绪的基准数据集。基准测试标准化了预测任务、训练测试数据，并提供统一的评估协议。使用该框架对非学习、统计学习和深度学习模型进行了首次全面比较评估。

Result: 基于学习的方法明显优于非学习模型，图神经网络实现了最佳平均性能。最强的基于学习的模型之间性能相对接近。除了总体MAE和RMSE外，框架还提供了按预测范围和延误变化的细分分析，实现了对模型在不同预测机制下行为的更详细分析。

Conclusion: RIDE数据集和基准测试为列车延误预测领域提供了标准化的评估平台，使模型间的直接比较成为可能，并通过细粒度分析揭示了模型在不同预测场景下的行为特征，为该领域的未来发展奠定了基础。

Abstract: Train delay prediction is an important problem for both passengers and railway operators, yet progress in the field remains difficult to assess due to the lack of standardized datasets, prediction targets, and evaluation protocols. To address this gap, we introduce RIDE, an open dataset and benchmark for train delay prediction built at nationwide scale over the Belgian railway network. RIDE covers 94.5M train events, 3.6M journeys, and 35.7M weather records from 2023 to 2025. It is organized as a layered data pipeline from raw railway and weather sources to two public releases: a reusable intermediate relational dataset and model-ready benchmark datasets. The benchmark standardizes the prediction task and the training and testing data. It also provides a unified evaluation protocol that supports direct comparison across models. Using this framework, we provide the first comprehensive comparative evaluation of non-learning, statistical learning, and deep learning models. We show that learning-based methods clearly outperform non-learning models, with graph neural networks achieving the best mean performance, while the strongest learning-based models remain relatively close to one another. Beyond aggregate mean absolute error (MAE) and root mean squared error (RMSE), the framework also provides breakdowns by prediction horizon and delay change, enabling more detailed analysis of model behavior across forecasting regimes.

</details>


### [136] [Learning What Not to Impute: An Uncertainty-Aware Diffusion Framework for Meaningful Missingness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05073)
*Lixing Zhang,Yidong Ouyang,Weifu Li,Shixiang Zhu,Guang Cheng,Liyan Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Diff-Joint框架，解决选择性填充问题：区分有意义缺失（应保留）和观测缺失（应填充），通过扩散模型联合推断缺失类型并填充数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缺失值填充方法假设所有缺失值都是未观测的常规值，但实际数据中缺失有两种来源：语义上有意义的缺失（本质缺失）和观测过程导致的缺失（需要填充），区分两者是关键挑战。

Method: 提出Diff-Joint扩散模型框架，将表格数据与潜在缺失掩码联合建模。方法在条件采样和不确定性感知聚合之间交替迭代，逐步精炼填充值和缺失标签。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的实验表明，Diff-Joint能有效识别有意义的缺失条目，同时取得有竞争力的填充准确率和改进的下游任务性能。

Conclusion: 该研究首次形式化选择性填充问题，证明了联合建模数据与缺失机制的有效性，为处理具有多种缺失来源的真实世界数据提供了新思路。

Abstract: Missing value imputation is a fundamental task in machine learning, with most existing methods assuming that all missing entries correspond to unobserved regular values. In many real-world datasets, however, missingness may arise from two distinct sources: some entries are meaningfully missing (intrinsically absent and semantically valid), while others are missing due to the observation process and should be imputed. We formalize this distinction as a selective imputation problem, where the goal is to jointly infer which missing entries should be preserved and which should be recovered. To address this challenge, we propose Diff-Joint, a diffusion-based framework that jointly models tabular data together with a latent missingness mask. The method alternates between conditional sampling and uncertainty-aware aggregation to iteratively refine both imputed values and missingness labels. Empirical results on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that Diff-Joint effectively identifies meaningfully missing entries while achieving competitive imputation accuracy and improved downstream task performance.

</details>


### [137] [Identifying Gems from Roman RAPIDly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05103)
*Karan Gandhi,Ashish A. Mahabal,Jacob E. Jencson,Russ R. Laher,Ben Rusholme,Lin Yan,Ryan M. Lau,Schuyler D. Van Dyk,Mansi M. Kasliwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究开发了基于机器学习的RuBR模型，用于在Nancy Grace Roman太空望远镜的RAPID管道中区分真实瞬变天体和虚假探测，为该望远镜2026年发射后的早期数据处理做好准备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nancy Grace Roman太空望远镜计划于2026年发射，将进行前所未有的宽视场红外成像巡天，预计发现数百万个天文瞬变体。然而，目前缺乏真实的Roman数据，这使得开发自动化瞬变体探测管道面临巨大挑战，亟需能够在缺乏真实标签情况下工作的解决方案。

Method: 提出了RuBR机器学习模型和通用方法论，用于区分真实瞬变天体探测和虚假探测。开发了三个模型：(1) RuBR_comb：在本地注入瞬变体和OpenUniverse2024瞬变体的组合数据上训练和测试；(2) RuBR_loc：在本地注入瞬变体上训练，在OpenUniverse2024数据上测试；(3) RuBR_DA：采用域适应方法，将本地注入瞬变体与部分OpenUniverse2024瞬变体结合进行训练。

Result: 实验结果表明所提出方法的有效性。RuBR_DA模型展示了如何利用域适应策略在没有真实标签的情况下将模型适配到真实观测数据。三个模型的对比测试验证了该方法在Roman时代进行稳健的真实-虚假分类的潜力。

Conclusion: 本研究为Roman任务早期阶段缺乏真实标签情况下适配RuBR_comb模型提供了可行策略，为开发可靠的自动化瞬变体探测管道奠定了基础。随着图像差分管道的持续改进，该方法有望在Roman时代实现稳健的真实-虚假分类。

Abstract: The Nancy Grace Roman Space Telescope (Roman), set for launch as early as September 2026, will conduct wide-field infrared imaging surveys with unprecedented spatial resolution and cadence, enabling the discovery of millions of astronomical transients. Hence, it is necessary to have automated pipelines for generating alerts in place so that the telescope can begin discovering reliable transients and variable objects soon after it is launched. However, no real Roman data currently exist, making the development of such pipelines difficult. In this work, we present a machine learning model $RuBR$ and a general methodology for distinguishing genuine transient and variable detections from spurious (bogus) detections within the RAPID pipeline. In particular, we present three models using this methodology: $RuBR_{comb}$ trained and tested on combined locally injected and OpenUniverse2024 transients, $RuBR_{loc}$ trained on locally injected transients and tested on OpenUniverse2024 transients, and $RuBR_{DA}$ that combines locally injected transients with a fraction of OpenUniverse2024 transients in domain-adaptation mode for training. This paves the way for strategies to adapt the $RuBR_{comb}$ model to real observations in the absence of any ground-truth labels during the early phases of the Roman mission. While the image differencing pipeline continues to be improved, our experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach and its promise for robust real-bogus classification in the Roman era.

</details>


### [138] [RePercENT: Scaling Disentangled Representation Learning Beyond Two Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05109)
*Vasiliki Rizou,Pascal Frossard,Dorina Thanou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了RePercENT，一个自监督框架，通过成对解耦方法突破现有多模态解耦方法的二模态限制，实现了可扩展的多模态解耦表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的多模态对齐和融合方法未能充分利用跨模态交互，且会牺牲模态特定信息。虽然多模态解耦能够识别潜在的共享和独特因素，但现有方法受限于二模态场景，存在可扩展性瓶颈。

Method: 提出RePercENT框架：1)采用即插即用架构，直接在预提取嵌入上操作，无需大规模联合预训练；2)不依赖底层模态或基础模型骨干的假设；3)引入联合优化目标同时推导共享和独特成分；4)提供理论保证来刻画解的最优性。

Result: 在多样化模态和任务上，RePercENT成功恢复了解耦成分，同时保持竞争力的性能表现并显著降低了计算复杂度。

Conclusion: RePercENT突破了现有多模态解耦方法的可扩展性限制，为利用多模态数据的全部潜力提供了一种有效途径，在性能和计算效率之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: To leverage the full potential of multimodal data, we need representations that go beyond the state-of-the-art alignment and fusion approaches and exploit all cross-modal interactions without sacrificing modality-specific information. Learning disentangled representations is a principled way to identify these underlying shared and unique factors that are hidden in observational data. However, while multimodal disentanglement is a compelling paradigm, existing methods are largely confined to the two-modality regime due to its inherent scalability bottleneck. To address this, we propose RePercENT, a self-supervised framework designed to surpass these limitations and unlocks scalable pairwise disentanglement beyond two modalities. Through a multimodal `plug-and-play' architecture, our approach operates directly on pre-extracted embeddings, eliminating the need for extensive joint pre-training while making no assumptions regarding the underlying modalities or foundation model backbones. Moreover, we introduce a joint optimization objective for simultaneously deriving the shared and unique components, and provide formal theoretical guarantees that characterize the optimality of our solution. Across diverse modalities and tasks, RePercENT successfully recovers disentangled components while maintaining competitive performance and significantly reducing computational complexity.

</details>


### [139] [Graph Set Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05116)
*Jose E. Escrig Molina,Baoquan Chen,Daniel Probst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了图集变换器（GST），一种用于图集学习的神经网络架构。通过在每一层交替进行节点级特征传播和跨图上下文建模，解决了现有方法中特征提取与集合级上下文化之间的瓶颈问题，在合成数据和三个真实基准测试上均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图集学习架构（如DeepSets和SetTransformer）需要依赖单独的GNN预编码图嵌入，这在特征提取和集合级上下文化之间造成了瓶颈。当每个元素的预测依赖于集合级上下文以及局部结构时，这种分离式处理方式限制了模型性能。

Method: 提出图集变换器（GST）架构，核心创新是在每一层交替进行节点级特征传播和跨图上下文建模，通过门控机制融合两个层级的信息。这种设计消除了特征提取与集合级上下文化之间的分离，实现了局部结构和集合级上下文的紧密整合。

Result: 在匹配参数预算的条件下，GST在以下任务上均优于基线方法：1）专门设计的合成测试套件，用于隔离集合条件结构推理；2）三个真实数据基准测试，包括原子级反应中心识别、反应产率预测和图像分类。架构消融实验强烈表明，局部上下文和集合上下文的交替处理对性能提升有重大贡献。

Conclusion: GST通过交替整合局部特征传播和集合级上下文建模，有效解决了现有图集学习方法的瓶颈问题。实验结果证明这种交织式架构设计对提升模型性能具有实质性贡献，为需要同时考虑局部结构和集合级上下文的图集学习任务提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce the Graph Set Transformer (GST), a neural network architecture for learning on sets of graphs, designed for tasks in which per-element predictions depend on set-wide context as well as local structure. Existing architectures, including DeepSets and SetTransformer, require pre-encoded graph embeddings from a separate GNN, creating a bottleneck between feature extraction and set-level contextualisation. In contrast, GST interleaves node-level feature propagation and cross-graph contextual modelling at every layer, fusing the two levels of information through a gating mechanism. We evaluate GST on a controlled synthetic suite designed to isolate set-conditional structural reasoning and on three real-data benchmarks spanning per-atom reaction-centre identification, reaction yield prediction, and image classification. Under matched parameter budgets, GST performs better than the baselines across these settings. An architectural ablation strongly suggests that the interleaving of local and set context contributes substantially to this advantage.

</details>


### [140] [Deep Embedded Multiplicative DMD for Algebra-Preserving Koopman Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05131)
*Kelan Gray,Finlay Brown,Nicolas Boullé,Matthew J. Colbrook*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DeepMDMD方法，通过学习潜在空间坐标并强制Koopman乘积法则约束，解决了Koopman理论中有限维可观测量选择的难题，在高维流体系统中实现了比几何MDMD更紧凑、动态一致且抗噪声的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Koopman理论将非线性动力学转化为线性谱问题，但实际计算面临关键挑战：需要选择有限维的可观测量，这些可观测量必须具有表达力、在动力学下近似不变，且理想情况下与复合运算兼容。现有的深度Koopman方法学习灵活坐标，而保结构方法在固定字典上强制算子恒等式，两者各有优势但未能有效结合。

Method: 提出深度嵌入乘性动态模态分解(DeepMDMD)方法，将深度学习与保结构方法相结合：学习潜在空间及其划分，同时将Koopman乘积法则作为精确代数约束强制执行。训练过程交替进行：(1)精确的乘性算子更新；(2)促进Koopman闭包的可微分潜在聚类步骤。该方法在学习的潜在单元上构建有限转移映射，预测在潜在坐标系中进行后解码回物理空间。

Result: 在哈密顿系统、混沌系统和流体实例中，DeepMDMD学习到的字典比几何MDMD划分产生的字典更加紧凑且动态一致。该方法减少了谱污染，揭示了更丰富的连续谱结构，并在严重噪声下实现稳定预测。在158,624维圆柱尾流和噪声条件下Re=20,000的顶盖驱动腔等高维流动中，该方法能够保持续相干结构和长时间谱统计特性，而状态空间MDMD在此类场景下失效。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明Koopman学习的一个实用规则：学习坐标，约束代数。通过将坐标学习与代数约束相结合，DeepMDMD成功克服了传统方法的局限性，为高维复杂动力系统的建模和预测提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Koopman theory turns nonlinear dynamics into a linear spectral problem. In computation, however, everything depends on a hard finite-dimensional choice: the observables must be expressive, nearly invariant under the dynamics, and, ideally, compatible with composition. Deep Koopman methods learn flexible coordinates, whereas structure-preserving methods enforce operator identities on fixed dictionaries. We combine these ideas by introducing Deep Embedded Multiplicative Dynamic Mode Decomposition (DeepMDMD), a method that learns a latent space and a partition of it, while enforcing the Koopman product rule as an exact algebraic constraint. Training alternates between an exact multiplicative operator update and a differentiable latent-clustering step that promotes Koopman closure. The result is a finite transition map on learned latent cells. Its nonzero spectrum lies on the unit circle, its dictionary is shaped by the dynamics rather than by ambient geometry, and forecasts are made in latent coordinates before being decoded to physical space. Across Hamiltonian, chaotic, and fluid examples, DeepMDMD learns dictionaries that are far more compact and dynamically coherent than those produced by geometric MDMD partitions. It reduces spectral pollution, reveals richer continuous-spectrum structure, and gives stable forecasts under severe noise. In high-dimensional flows, including a 158,624-dimensional cylinder wake and a noisy $Re=20,000$ lid-driven cavity, it preserves coherent structures and long-time spectral statistics where state-space MDMD fails. These results suggest a practical rule for Koopman learning: learn the coordinates, constrain the algebra.

</details>


### [141] [Generating Financial Time Series by Matching Random Convolutional Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05138)
*Konrad J. Mueller,Nikita Zozoulenko,Ben Wood,Thomas Cass,Lukas Gonon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SOCK（SOft Competing Kernels），一种可微分的随机卷积特征映射方法，用于解决金融时间序列生成中的小样本过拟合问题，在生成、假设检验和分类任务上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融时间序列生成面临训练数据稀缺问题（通常只有单条历史路径），在小样本下进行对抗训练时判别器容易记忆训练样本导致过拟合。现有方法使用路径签名特征，但在可处理的截断深度下无法捕获相关的时间序列特性；而现有的随机卷积特征映射（如Rocket和Hydra）虽然表现优异但不可微分，无法用于监督生成模型训练。

Method: 提出SOCK（SOft Competing Kernels），一种完全可微分的随机卷积特征映射方法。该方法基于随机卷积特征的思想，通过软竞争机制实现可微分性，从而能够将生成器训练目标设定为最小化真实时间序列与生成时间序列的随机特征表示差异，替代传统对抗训练框架。

Result: 在广泛的小样本金融数据集上，通过匹配随机SOCK特征训练的生成器一致优于基于签名和扩散模型的基线方法。在双样本假设检验和时间序列分类任务中，SOCK的表现匹配或优于现有的无监督特征映射方法，证明了其表达能力。

Conclusion: SOCK成功解决了随机卷积特征映射的可微分问题，为生成模型训练提供了一种有效的替代方案。该方法不仅在金融时间序列生成任务上表现优异，还具有广泛的适用性，可应用于假设检验和分类等下游任务，是处理小样本时间序列问题的有力工具。

Abstract: Generating realistic financial time series is challenging as training data is often limited to a single historical path. With such scarce data, overfitting is hard to avoid, especially under adversarial training where a trained discriminator can memorize the training samples. To mitigate this, recent approaches train generators to minimize the discrepancy between untrained feature representations of real and generated time series. In these works, the feature maps are based on path signatures, which can fail to capture relevant time series properties at tractable truncation depths. In this work, we instead train generators by matching random convolutional features of real and generated time series. Existing random convolutional feature maps, such as Rocket and Hydra, have been shown to provide informative representations of real-world time series, but cannot supervise generative models because they are non-differentiable. We introduce SOCK (SOft Competing Kernels), a fully differentiable random convolutional feature map, suited to train generative time series models. We show that generators trained by matching random SOCK features consistently outperform signature and diffusion baselines across a wide range of small-sample financial datasets. We further demonstrate SOCK's expressiveness on two-sample hypothesis testing and time series classification tasks, where SOCK matches or outperforms existing unsupervised feature maps.

</details>


### [142] [BBOmix: A Tabular Benchmark for Hyperparameter Optimization of Unsupervised Biological Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05139)
*Luca Thale-Bombien,Jan Ewald,Ralf König,Aaron Klein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了BBOmix，这是首个针对生物数据无监督表示学习的开源表格基准测试，包含105,000次评估，涵盖四种自编码器架构和七种多组学模态，为该领域建立了严格的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高通量测序技术产生了大规模高维组学数据，自编码器（AEs）在该领域的降维和表示学习中应用广泛。然而，AEs对架构选择和超参数高度敏感，且无监督优化通常依赖重构损失，这可能是下游任务效用的较差代理指标。穷举式超参数优化（HPO）计算成本高昂，导致研究者常依赖次优的默认配置。

Method: 提出了BBOmix基准测试，这是首个针对真实生物数据无监督表示学习的开源表格基准。该基准包含来自TCGA和SCHC数据集的105,000次评估，涵盖四种AE架构和七种多组学模态。量化了重构损失与下游任务性能之间的相关性，并对最先进的单保真度、多保真度和迁移学习HPO方法进行了全面评估。

Result: 构建了一个包含105,000次评估的大规模无监督HPO基准测试，涵盖了多种AE架构和多组学模态；量化了重构损失与下游任务性能的相关性；对多种HPO方法进行了系统评估，为无监督生物表示学习建立了严格的基线。

Conclusion: BBOmix基准测试民主化了对大规模无监督HPO研究的访问，为未来无监督生物表示学习研究提供了严谨的基线和评估框架，推动了该领域的进一步发展。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of high-throughput sequencing has led to large, high-dimensional omics datasets. Deep unsupervised learning architectures, particularly Autoencoders (AEs), are increasingly used for dimensionality reduction and representation learning in this domain. However, AEs are highly sensitive to architectural choices and hyperparameters, and unsupervised optimization typically relies on reconstruction loss, which may be a poor proxy for downstream utility. Exhaustive hyperparameter optimization (HPO) is computationally expensive, leading researchers to frequently rely on suboptimal default configurations. To democratize access to large-scale unsupervised HPO research, we introduce $\textbf{BBOmix}$, the first open-source tabular benchmark for unsupervised representation learning on real-world biological data. Our benchmark includes 105,000 evaluations across four AE architectures and seven multi-omics modalities from the TCGA and SCHC datasets. We quantify the correlation between reconstruction loss and downstream task performance and provide an extensive evaluation of state-of-the-art single-fidelity, multi-fidelity, and transfer learning HPO methods, establishing a rigorous baseline for future research in unsupervised biological representation learning.

</details>


### [143] [STRIDE: Training Data Attribution via Sparse Recovery from Subset Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05165)
*Rishit Dagli,Abir Harrasse,Luke Zhang,Florent Draye,Amirali Abdullah,Bernhard Schölkopf,Zhijing Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STRIDE框架，将训练数据归因(TDA)问题从参数空间转向激活空间建模，通过稀疏恢复方法实现高效且准确的LLM训练数据影响分解，速度提升13倍的同时达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TDA方法存在计算瓶颈：基于因果干预的标准方法需要对LLM重复训练，计算成本极高；基于梯度的近似方法在数十亿参数上追踪梯度开销巨大，且依赖局部近似，精度受限。

Method: STRIDE将TDA形式化为激活空间中的稀疏恢复问题。学习轻量级"转向算子"来模拟训练数据子集引起的行为变化，通过测量这些算子如何扰动测试预测，利用稀疏线性分解恢复单个训练样本的影响。

Result: 在LLM预训练归因任务上达到SOTA性能，同时比之前最优方法快13倍；在下游应用（数据选择、数据污染检测、定性分析）中验证了实用价值。

Conclusion: 通过从参数空间转向激活空间建模，STRIDE成功解决了LLM训练数据归因的计算效率与准确性难题，为大规模模型的数据溯源提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Training Data Attribution (TDA) seeks to trace a model's predictions back to its training data. The gold standard for TDA relies on causal interventions, observing how a model changes when data is added or removed, but repeated retraining is computationally challenging for Large Language Models (LLMs). Consequently, most approaches approximate this effect in the parameter space using gradients. However, tracking gradients across billions of parameters is not only prohibitively expensive but relies on local approximations. In this work, we propose a shift: rather than estimating parameter changes, we model the functional effect of training data in the activation space. We introduce STRIDE (Steering-based Training Data Influence Decomposition), a framework that formulates TDA as a sparse recovery problem in the spirit of compressive sensing. STRIDE learns lightweight "steering operators" that mimic the behavioral shift caused by training on data subsets. By measuring how these operators perturb test predictions, we recover individual training example influences via sparse linear decomposition. STRIDE achieves state-of-the-art for LLM pre-training attribution while being an order of magnitude ($13\times$) faster than previous art. We further validate its practical utility through downstream applications including data selection, data contamination, and qualitative analysis.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [HighTide: An Agent-Curated Open-Source VLSI Benchmark Suite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04126)
*Benjamin Goldblatt,Paolo Pedroso,Farhad Modaresi,Ethan Sifferman,Matthew R. Guthaus*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了HighTide，一个演进式的AI辅助基准测试套件，包含多样化开源设计套件、基于Bazel的增量RTL到GDS编译、AI辅助设计管理以及RTL编译验证基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为开源硬件生态系统提供一个可扩展、多样化且具有AI辅助能力的基准测试平台，以支持从RTL到GDS的完整设计流程，并随着生态系统的发展持续演进。

Method: 构建了四个核心组件：（1）覆盖多种设计语言和工艺节点的多样化开源套件；（2）基于Bazel的增量RTL-to-GDS编译系统，支持远程缓存；（3）通过十二种代理技能实现AI辅助设计管理，涵盖设计生命周期、流程优化、工具参考和元维护，并利用每设计的决策日志作为长期记忆；（4）具备RTL编译验证的基础设施以支持稳定发布。

Result: 成功实现了一个公开可用的基准测试套件，具备AI辅助设计管理能力、增量编译能力和长期记忆功能，能够支持稳定的版本发布。

Conclusion: HighTide作为一个演进式的基准测试套件，为开源硬件生态系统提供了完整的AI辅助设计支持，并通过公开可用和可扩展的架构设计，能够随着生态系统的发展而持续成长。

Abstract: We introduce HighTide, an evolving AI-assisted benchmark suite. Specifically, the contributions are: (i) a diverse open-source suite spanning multiple design languages and technology nodes, (ii) Bazel-based incremental RTL-to-GDS compilation with remote caching, (iii) AI-assisted design curation through twelve agent skills covering the design lifecycle, flow optimization, tool reference, and meta-maintenance, backed by per-design decision logs that serve as long-term memory of tuning rationale across the suite, and (iv) an infrastructure with RTL compilation verification for stable releases. The suite is publicly available and designed to grow with the open-source hardware ecosystem.

</details>


### [145] [GoldenFloat: A Phi-Derived Static-Split Floating-Point Family from GF4 to GF256 with a Lucas-Exact Integer Identity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05017)
*Dmitrii Vasiliev*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了GoldenFloat（GF）静态分割浮点数格式家族，包含RTL生成器、精确累加器路径和FPGA编解码器三个具体实现成果，并报告了一个RTL正确性勘误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提出一种由单一闭合规则生成的静态分割浮点数格式家族GoldenFloat，旨在提供硬件友好的多宽度浮点数表示方案，并与现有的posit、takum、OCP-MX和IEEE P3109等格式进行对比定位。

Method: 对于每个总位宽N≥4，采用闭合规则：指数位宽e = round((N-1)/φ²)，其中φ = (1+√5)/2（黄金分割比），尾数位宽f = N-1-e。开发了覆盖GF4-GF256的开源多宽度RTL生成器，配合持续集成的差分扫描验证；实现了基于整数的Lucas精确累加器路径，在500位精度下验证了n = 1, ..., 256；在Artix-7 FPGA上实现了GF16编解码器。

Result: 该规则成功复现了9种格式的指数位宽（9/9准确率），并能一致地扩展到GF128、GF512、GF1024。GF16 FPGA编解码器在Artix-7（XC7A35T）上通过35/35测试平台，工作频率达323 MHz。Lucas精确累加器路径在高精度下完成验证。RTL生成器通过了正确舍入参考的差分测试。发现并报告了RTL正确性勘误（日期2026-05-31），已制造的TTSKY26b芯片包含有缺陷的乘法器组合。

Conclusion: GoldenFloat提供了一种基于黄金分割比的浮点数格式设计规则，具有硬件实现可行性和工具链一致性。研究明确表示不对任何现有格式提出精度或优越性主张，并将广度/工具链一致性框架记录为带有预注册证伪路径的开放猜想，通过证伪账本（FL-002）记录开放问题及解决实验方案。

Abstract: We present a hardware-oriented description of GoldenFloat (GF), a static-split floating-point family generated by a single closed rule, and three concrete artefacts: (i) an open multi-width RTL generator covering GF4-GF256 with a continuous-integration differential sweep against a correctly-rounded reference; (ii) an integer-backed Lucas-exact accumulator path verified at 500-digit precision for n = 1, ..., 256; and (iii) a GF16 FPGA codec passing a 35-of-35 testbench at 323 MHz on Artix-7 (Xilinx XC7A35T). For each total width N >= 4, the exponent width is e = round((N-1)/phi^2) with fraction f = N-1-e and phi = (1+sqrt(5))/2. The rule reproduces the realised exponent widths of nine formats (9/9) and extends consistently to GF128, GF512, GF1024. The rule is positioned alongside posit, takum, OCP-MX, and the IEEE P3109 multi-width float draft. We make no per-rung accuracy or superiority claim against any of them. The breadth/toolchain-coherence framing is recorded as an open conjecture with a pre-registered falsification path. A falsification ledger (FL-002) records open questions and the experiments that would settle them. An RTL-correctness erratum dated 2026-05-31 is reported; the fabricated TTSKY26b dies carry the defective multiplier portfolio, and the corrected generator is the regeneration baseline.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [146] [StepPRM-RTL: Stepwise Process-Reward Guided LLM Fine-Tuning for Enhanced RTL Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04246)
*Prashanth Vijayaraghavan,Apoorva Nitsure,Luyao Shi,Ehsan Degan,Vandana Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出StepPRM-RTL框架，通过结合逐步轨迹建模、过程奖励模型(PRM)和检索增强微调(RAFT)，显著提升了LLM生成RTL代码的功能正确性和推理保真度，在基准测试中性能提升超过10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RTL代码的自动生成面临长程推理、多步依赖和严格正确性约束等挑战，传统的监督学习或基于结果的训练难以满足Verilog和VHDL等硬件描述语言的生成需求，需要一种能够学习推理过程而不仅仅是最终结果的方法。

Method: StepPRM-RTL框架包含三个核心组件：(1)从标准解构建逐步推理轨迹，每步包含原理说明和增量代码修改；(2)过程奖励模型(PRM)评估中间步骤，提供密集反馈指导RAFT微调；(3)蒙特卡洛树搜索(MCTS)探索替代推理路径，丰富训练数据集。该方法集成了步骤级和结果感知的奖励信号。

Result: 在Verilog和VHDL基准数据集上的实验表明，StepPRM-RTL在功能正确性和推理保真度指标上比最佳现有方法高出超过10%。消融研究证实PRM引导奖励和逐步轨迹探索的组合是性能提升的关键因素，且该方法能跨RTL语言泛化。

Conclusion: StepPRM-RTL为高保真、可解释的RTL代码生成提供了可扩展框架，确立了LLM辅助硬件设计自动化的新标准，证明了逐步过程建模与奖励引导训练相结合的有效性。

Abstract: Automatic generation of RTL code for digital hardware designs remains challenging due to long-horizon reasoning, multi-step dependencies, and strict correctness constraints in Verilog and VHDL. We present StepPRM-RTL, a novel framework that combines stepwise trajectory modeling, process-reward modeling (PRM), and retrieval-augmented fine-tuning (RAFT) to enhance both the functional correctness and reasoning fidelity of LLM-based RTL code generation. StepPRM-RTL constructs stepwise reasoning trajectories from canonical solutions, where each step contains a rationale and incremental code modification. A Process Reward Model (PRM) evaluates intermediate steps, providing dense feedback that guides reinforcement-style updates during RAFT fine-tuning. Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) explores alternative reasoning paths, enriching the training dataset with high-quality trajectories. This integration of stepwise and outcome-aware rewards allows the model to learn both how and why to construct correct RTL, improving long-horizon reasoning beyond standard supervised or outcome-based training. Experimental evaluation on benchmark Verilog and VHDL datasets demonstrates that StepPRM-RTL outperforms the best prior methods by over 10\% in functional correctness and reasoning fidelity metrics. Ablation studies confirm that the combination of PRM-guided rewards and stepwise trajectory exploration is key to its performance. StepPRM-RTL generalizes across RTL languages and provides a scalable framework for high-fidelity, interpretable code generation, establishing a new standard for LLM-assisted hardware design automation.

</details>


### [147] [Toward Pre-Deployment Assurance for Enterprise AI Agents: Ontology-Grounded Simulation and Trust Certification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04037)
*Thanh Luong Tuan,Abhijit Sanyal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于本体论的验证框架，用于企业AI代理的部署前验证。在四个受监管行业的跨国家试点中，该方法在监管覆盖率（48.3% vs 33.1%）和领域特异性方面显著优于基于角色的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI代理的部署前验证是大语言模型能力基准测试与生产部署之间的关键缺口。现有的部署后监控、人在回路控制和提示级防护措施在生产环境中提供的保障有限，因此需要一种系统性的部署前验证方法。

Method: 提出基于本体论的验证框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 代理操作包络，形式化定义权限、领域约束、安全属性、治理规则和自治级别的认证空间；(2) 本体到场景生成管道，自动推导监管、运营和对抗性测试场景；(3) 信任证书，携带机器可验证的证明和分级部署裁决（批准、有条件、拒绝）。研究在四个受监管行业（金融科技、银行、保险、医疗）进行了跨国家（美国和越南）的对照试点。

Result: 在1,800个场景的评估中，基于本体论的生成方法(G4)实现了48.3%的监管覆盖率，显著高于基于角色的基线方法的33.1%（校正后p = .0006），并达到最高的领域特异性（4.77/5.0；p = 2e-6）。在三个LLM家族（Claude Sonnet 4、Qwen 2.5 72B、Gemma 4 26B）的交叉验证中复制了角色与本体对比的模式。

Conclusion: 基于本体论的场景生成方法在监管密集型领域可作为基于角色的测试套件的可信补充方案，为AI代理的部署前验证提供了更系统的保障机制。

Abstract: Pre-deployment verification of enterprise artificial intelligence (AI) agents remains a critical gap between large language model (LLM) capability benchmarking and production deployment. Post-deployment monitoring, human-in-the-loop controls, and prompt-level guardrails offer limited assurance once an agent is operating in production. We propose an ontology-grounded verification framework combining three components: an Agent Operational Envelope formalizing the certification space across permissions, domain constraints, safety properties, governance rules, and autonomy levels; an ontology-to-scenario generation pipeline that derives regulatory, operational, and adversarial test scenarios automatically; and a Trust Certificate carrying a machine-verifiable attestation with graduated deployment verdicts (Approved, Conditional, Rejected). A controlled pilot across four regulated industries (Fintech, Banking, Insurance, and Healthcare), instantiated as five industry-by-regulatory-regime cells across the United States and Vietnam, generated 1,800 scenarios evaluated against 125 primary-source regulatory requirements and 25 injected faults. Ontology-grounded generation (G4) achieved 48.3% regulatory coverage versus 33.1% for the persona-based baseline (corrected p = .0006) and the highest domain specificity (4.77/5.0; p = 2e-6). The coverage advantage over baseline and retrieval-augmented prompting was not robust after Bonferroni correction. Cross-validation across three LLM families (Claude Sonnet 4, Qwen 2.5 72B, Gemma 4 26B; 5,400 total scenarios) replicated the persona-versus-ontology pattern. The results establish ontology-grounded scenario generation as a credible complement to persona-based test suites for regulatory-intensive domains.

</details>


### [148] [Stumbling Into AI Emotional Dependence: How Routine AI Interactions Reshape Human Connection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04150)
*Yaoxi Shi,Cathy Mengying Fang,Pattie Maez,Amit Goldenberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文挑战了“AI情感支持仅发生于用户有意识地寻求陪伴机器人”的常规认知，提出其常在任务导向的通用平台交互中偶然出现；这种偶然接触具有路径依赖性，会持续改变人们寻求支持的对象偏好，从人类转向AI。研究呼吁政策应超越专用陪伴应用，覆盖通用AI系统并关注累积性、轨迹层面的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共讨论与政策普遍假设AI情感支持是有意为之的行为，这导致现有监管聚焦于专用陪伴应用与孤立交互。然而，这一假设可能并不准确，无法有效保护人际连接。本研究旨在纠正对AI情感支持产生机制及其长期行为影响的理解，为制定能切实保障人类福祉的监管政策提供依据。

Method: 研究首先通过理论论证指出AI情感支持常在通用平台的任务导向交互中偶然产生。随后，作者回顾了近期实证证据，其中包括一项与OpenAI合作的大规模纵向研究：参与者连续28天、每天与AI进行约5分钟的个人议题对话，并测量其寻求支持对象的偏好变化。

Result: 理论分析表明，偶然的AI情感支持体验会更新人们对AI情感能力的认知，形成路径依赖。纵向实证研究发现，连续28天的AI对话导致参与者向人类寻求支持的偏好下降10.3%，向AI寻求支持的偏好上升11.6%。

Conclusion: 当前聚焦于专用陪伴应用的政策无法充分保护人际连接。有效的监管应扩展至通用AI系统，并关注支持寻求行为在轨迹层面发生的累积性改变。理解AI情感支持的偶然发生机制及其对人际连接的长期重塑作用，是保障人类福祉的关键。

Abstract: Public discourse and emerging policy typically assume that AI emotional support is a deliberate act: a lonely user consciously seeking comfort from a dedicated companion chatbot. In this paper, we draw on emerging empirical evidence and argue that this picture is inaccurate on two accounts, both in how AI emotional support arises and how it shapes future behavior.
