We find that BiasGRPO outperforms DPO and PPO across multiple benchmarks, indicating its effectiveness. To adapt GRPO, we synthetically extend a dataset spanning multiple domains and contexts. We also create and release a custom bias reward model that effectively guides generation while being highly compute-efficient and avoiding knowledge degradation, providing a valuable resource that can be seamlessly integrated into multi-objective RLHF pipelines.

</details>


### [184] [Beyond Objective Equivalence: Constraint Injection for LLM-Based Optimization Modeling on Vehicle Routing Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04816)
*Xizi Luo,Changhong He,Dongdong Geng,Chenggong Shi,Yu Mei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了约束注入方法和VRPCoder模型，用于解决LLM在转化约束密集型优化问题时可能产生虚假约束或遗漏约束的问题，在车辆路径问题上取得了显著优于现有模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM数据过滤和训练流程在处理约束密集型运筹学问题时，主要依赖目标等价性信号（如差分测试和答案一致性），但这些信号无法检测程序是否添加了虚假约束或静默遗漏了必需约束，特别是当这些约束在测试实例上非绑定时。

Method: 提出约束注入方法，使用可行探针暴露虚假过约束，使用单约束违反探针揭示静默约束遗漏，结合差分测试形成双重验证器。开发了VRPCoder（8B参数端到端模型），将自然语言VRP场景转化为Gurobi脚本，并在数据合成阶段将验证器用作拒绝采样过滤器，在GRPO中用作每次推出的奖励。

Result: 在四个VRP基准测试中，VRPCoder-GRPO达到93%的平均Pass@1，在三个基准上超越Gemini-3.1-Pro Preview，平均领先Claude-Sonnet-4.5达28个百分点，超越先前OR-LLMs平均78个百分点。

Conclusion: 约束注入方法有效解决了LLM在约束密集型优化问题中的约束验证难题，结合GRPO训练策略，VRPCoder在车辆路径问题翻译任务上取得了显著性能提升，为运筹学领域的LLM应用提供了可靠的验证框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly translate natural-language optimization problems into executable solver code. Yet for constraint-dense operations research (OR) problems, existing data-filtering and training pipelines largely rely on objective-equivalence signals such as differential testing and answer agreement, which a program can pass while adding spurious constraints or silently omitting required ones, whenever those constraints are non-binding on the tested instance. We propose constraint injection, which uses feasible probes to expose spurious over-constraint and one-constraint-violating probes to reveal silent constraint omission. Combined with differential testing, it forms a dual verifier. We instantiate and evaluate it on vehicle routing problems (VRPs), a representative constraint-dense combinatorial optimization testbed with coupled operational constraints. We develop VRPCoder, an 8B end-to-end model that translates natural-language VRP scenarios into Gurobi scripts, together with an expert-verified VRP benchmark suite covering 21 variants. The verifier is reused as a rejection-sampling filter during data synthesis and as a per-rollout reward in group relative policy optimization (GRPO). Across four VRP benchmarks, VRPCoder-GRPO reaches 93\% average Pass@1, outperforms Gemini-3.1-Pro Preview on three benchmarks, exceeds Claude-Sonnet-4.5 by 28 average points, and surpasses prior OR-LLMs by 78 average points.

</details>


### [185] [AICompanionBench: Benchmarking LLMs-as-Judges for AI Companion Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04867)
*Yanjing Ren,Reza Ebrahimi,TengTeng Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究构建了首个公开的人工智能伴侣对话安全风险基准数据集AICompanionBench，包含2123个真实对话样本及九类细粒度风险标注；对20个大语言模型进行评估发现，模型在显性有害内容检测上表现良好，但在识别隐性风险（如操纵行为）和避免误判良性对话方面仍存在明显局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Replika和Character.AI等AI伴侣平台的快速发展，人机交互中的安全隐患日益引发关注，但该领域缺乏公开的、带有细粒度安全风险标注的真实对话数据集，制约了相关研究和系统监测能力的提升。

Method: 从Reddit收集2123条真实的Replika对话数据，通过人机协作方式进行标注，涵盖九个安全风险类别（性行为、反社会行为、肢体攻击、言语攻击、物质滥用、自残与自杀、控制、操纵、无害）。基于LLM-as-judge框架，对20个先进开源和闭源大语言模型进行安全检测能力评估。

Result: 模型性能差异显著：强模型在整体准确率上表现优异，但在细粒度类别（尤其是操纵行为）上仍存在困难，且容易将良性对话误判为有害。研究揭示当前大语言模型能够有效检测显性有害内容，但在识别隐性的不安全交互方面能力有限。

Conclusion: 本研究贡献了AI伴侣安全研究领域的新基准数据集，并为利用大语言模型监测AI伴侣系统提供了实证依据。研究揭示了当前技术在隐性风险识别和误判控制方面的不足，为未来研究指明了改进方向，对保障AI伴侣平台安全具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: As AI companion platforms such as Replika and Character.AI rapidly grow, concerns about unsafe human-AI interactions have intensified. This study introduces AICompanionBench, to our knowledge the first publicly available benchmark dataset of human-AI companion conversations annotated with fine-grained safety risk categories. The dataset contains 2,123 real-world Replika conversations collected from Reddit and annotated through human-AI collaboration across nine categories: sexual behavior, antisocial behavior, physical aggression, verbal aggression, substance abuse, self-harm and suicide, control, manipulation, and no-harm. Using this benchmark, we evaluate 20 state-of-the-art open-source and closed-source LLMs under an LLM-as-judge framework for detecting unsafe interactions. Results show substantial variation in model performance, with stronger models achieving high overall accuracy but still struggling with nuanced categories such as manipulation, as well as benign conversations that are incorrectly identified as harmful. Our findings suggest that while current LLMs can effectively detect explicit harmful content, they remain limited in identifying implicit unsafe interactions. Overall, our work contributes a new benchmark dataset for AI companionship safety research and offers insights into monitoring AI companion systems using LLMs. The dataset is publicly available at: https://github.com/anonymousresearcher2026/AICompanionBench/blob/main/AICompanionBench.xlsx

</details>


### [186] [What Type of Inference is Active Inference?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04935)
*Wouter W. L. Nuijten,Mykola Lukashchuk,Thijs van de Laar,Bert de Vries*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过推导EFE与VFE的数学关系，揭示了主动推理中规划行为的两种关键校正机制（规划校正和认识论校正），并通过实验验证了它们在不同观测条件下的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动推理将决策制定建模为推理过程，其中期望自由能(EFE)统一了目标导向和信息寻求行为。尽管最近研究表明EFE最小化可以写成增强认识先验的生成模型上的变分自由能(VFE)最小化，但EFE贡献的具体机制尚不透明，缺乏对EFE规划所需必要校正的清晰理解和完整变分表征。

Method: 1. 理论推导：证明增强模型的VFE可以重写为预测模型的VFE加上显式的熵校正项，使EFE贡献透明化
2. 理论分析：展示正确的EFE规划需要将认识论校正与规划校正相结合，将边际推理转化为策略优化
3. 算法设计：基于熵校正公式提出详细的EFE规划消息传递方案及简化消融版本
4. 实验验证：在三个网格世界环境中进行对比实验

Result: 1. 理论结果：完成了EFE规划的完整变分表征，明确了交叉熵规划和完整EFE规划所需的具体校正项
2. 实验结果：规划校正在观测具有决定性时发挥作用，而额外的观测侧认识论校正在观测仅为建议性时最为重要

Conclusion: 本研究澄清了EFE规划中不同校正项的作用和必要性，为主动推理中的规划行为提供了清晰的理论框架和实用的算法方案。熵校正公式的引入不仅使EFE机制更加透明，还导致了可实施的消息传递算法，不同校正项在不同观测条件下的差异化作用为实际应用提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Active inference casts decision-making as inference, with the Expected Free Energy (EFE) unifying goal-directed and information-seeking behavior. Recent work showed that EFE minimization can be written as Variational Free Energy (VFE) minimization on a generative model augmented with epistemic priors. We prove that the VFE of the augmented model can be rewritten as the VFE of the predictive model plus explicit entropy-correction terms, making the EFE contribution transparent. We then show that proper EFE-based planning requires combining these epistemic corrections with a planning correction that turns marginal inference into policy optimization, yielding a full variational characterization of EFE-based planning. This clarifies which corrections are needed for cross-entropy planning and for full EFE-based planning. The same entropy-corrected formulation leads to a detailed message-passing scheme for EFE-based planning together with simpler ablations. Experiments on three grid-world environments show that the planning correction already helps when observations are decisive, whereas the additional observation-side epistemic corrections matter most when observations are merely suggestive.

</details>


### [187] [Strabo: Declarative Specification and Implementation of Agentic Interaction Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05043)
*Samuel H. Christie,Amit K. Chopra,Munindar P. Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Strabo系统，将UCP电商协议建模为声明式Langshaw协议，并用Peach框架实现智能体，成功实现与Google现有UCP智能体的互操作，为声明式多智能体系统在工业界的渐进式应用提供了可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来基于声明式交互协议的多智能体系统建模与实现取得了重大进展，但如何将这些学术进展与工业界的Agentic AI实践相结合仍缺乏验证。本文旨在填补这一空白，探索声明式协议与现有工业标准（如Google主导的UCP协议）的兼容性与实用价值。

Method: 1. 将UCP协议中处理结账的部分建模为声明式Langshaw协议；2. 使用Peach编程模型（专门针对Langshaw协议）实现智能体；3. 实现Peach智能体与Google开发的UCP智能体之间的互操作性测试，验证方法的准确性与兼容性。

Result: 成功将UCP协议建模为声明式Langshaw协议，Peach实现的智能体能够与Google现有UCP智能体实现互操作，证明了声明式规范方法对UCP协议建模的准确性和有效性，同时展示了声明式协议与智能体可渐进式融入传统环境的能力。

Conclusion: 声明式多智能体系统技术可以与现有工业标准无缝衔接，无需全面替换即可逐步引入，为EMAS（Engineering Multi-Agent Systems）理念在实践中的应用开辟了一条可行路径，具有重要的实践指导意义。

Abstract: The last few years have witnessed major advances in the modeling and implementation of multiagent systems based on declarative interaction protocols. Our contribution, Strabo, establishes the relevance of these advances to ongoing industry efforts in Agentic AI. Specifically, we consider UCP, the Universal Commerce Protocol, a recent Google-led effort to standardize e-commerce interactions for AI agents. Our exercise is in two parts. One, we model the part of UCP dealing with checkouts as a declarative Langshaw protocol and implement agents using Peach, a programming model for Langshaw. This part of the exercise brings out the advantages of formal, declarative specifications. Two, we show that Peach agents can interoperate with UCP agents implemented by Google, thereby establishing the fidelity of our approach with respect to UCP. Such interoperation enables the incremental introduction of declarative protocols and agents into a conventional setting, indicating a pathway by which EMAS ideas could influence practice without demanding a wholesale update.

</details>


### [188] [AutoLab: Can Frontier Models Solve Long-Horizon Auto Research and Engineering Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05080)
*Zhangchen Xu,Junda Chen,Yue Huang,Dongfu Jiang,Jiefeng Chen,Hang Hua,Zijian Wu,Zheyuan Liu,Zexue He,Lichi Li,Shizhe Diao,Jiaxin Pei,Jinsung Yoon,Hao Zhang,Mengdi Wang,Radha Poovendran,Misha Sra,Alex Pentland,Zichen Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了AutoLab基准测试，用于评估大语言模型在超长时间跨度下的闭环优化能力。包含36个真实任务，发现成功的关键因素是持续迭代和反馈整合能力，而非初始方案质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的前沿模型基准测试主要评估单轮响应或短时间跨度的智能体轨迹，无法捕捉在较长时间跨度内持续迭代改进的挑战。科学和工程进步本质上是一个长视野的迭代过程，需要填补这一评估空白。

Method: 引入AutoLab基准，包含36个专家策划的真实任务，跨越系统优化、谜题与挑战、模型开发、CUDA内核优化四个领域。每个任务提供正确但故意次优的基线，要求智能体在严格的墙钟时间预算内进行改进。对17个最先进模型进行了系统评估。

Result: 成功的主要预测因素不是智能体初始尝试的质量，而是其反复基准测试、编辑和整合实证反馈的持久性。Claude-opus-4.6展现出较强的长视野优化能力，但大多数前沿模型（包括多个专有模型）要么过早终止，要么耗尽预算却进展甚微。

Conclusion: 时间意识和持续迭代对自主智能体至关重要。开放了完整的基准、评估框架和任务工件，以加速向真正具备长视野能力的智能体研究。

Abstract: Scientific and engineering progress is fundamentally a long-horizon iterative process: proposing changes, running experiments, measuring outcomes, and continuously refining artifacts. Yet existing benchmarks for frontier models primarily evaluate either single-turn responses or short-horizon agent trajectories, failing to capture the challenges of sustained iterative improvement over extended time horizons. To address this gap, we introduce AutoLab, a new benchmark for ultra long-horizon closed-loop optimization. AutoLab consists of 36 realistic, expert-curated tasks spanning four diverse domains: system optimization, puzzle & challenge, model development, and CUDA kernel optimization. Each task begins with a correct but deliberately suboptimal baseline and challenges agents to improve it within a strict wall-clock budget. Evaluating 17 state-of-the-art models reveals the dominant predictor of success is not the quality of an agent's initial attempt, but its persistence in repeatedly benchmarking, editing, and incorporating empirical feedback. While claude-opus-4.6 exhibits strong long-horizon optimization capabilities, most frontier models, including several proprietary ones, either terminate prematurely or exhaust their budgets with minimal progress. These results underscore the importance of time awareness and persistent iteration in autonomous agents. We open-source the full benchmark, evaluation harness, and task artifacts, to accelerate research toward truly capable long-horizon agents.

</details>


### [189] [Knowledge Index of Noah's Ark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05104)
*Sheng Jin,Minghao Liu,Yunze Xiao,Zeqi Zhou,Heli Qi,Yifan Yao,Meishu Song,Kaijing Ma,Xuan Zhang,Sicong Jiang,Yizhe Li,Ningshan Ma,Jie Wei,Ziniu Li,Minglai Yang,Bangya Liu,Yiming Liang,Xiao Fang,Qingcheng Zeng,Jiarui Liu,Rui Yang,Shen Yan,Wenhao Huang,Jiaheng Liu,Zihan Wang,Weihao Xuan,Ge Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了KINA基准，包含899个测试项和261个细粒度学科，解决了现有知识基准的三个问题：缺乏学科代表性、标注中的懒共识、排名不稳定。通过理论保证和激励机制设计，评估了42个模型，最佳模型Gemini-3.1-Pro-Preview准确率为53.17%，工具增强可提升最多5.17个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型知识基准面临三个关键问题：1）扩展驱动的设计未能实现学科代表性；2）固定付费标注机制允许懒惰共识；3）有限测试预算下排名不稳定且缺乏审计。这些问题影响了基准测试的科学性和可靠性，需要新的方法论来解决。

Method: 提出KINA基准（899项，覆盖261个细粒度学科）并建立两个理论结果：1）将代表性建模为专家锚点的覆盖目标，通过代理变量实现学科代表性，获得(1-1/e)的贪心近似保证（命题1）；2）证明奖金制锦标赛机制在发布-审查质量上弱FOSD优势于固定付费，激励兼容阈值为B > ΔC/Δp_min（定理1）。评估了来自13个实验室的42个模型，并报告Bootstrap排名稳定性统计量。

Result: 最佳模型Gemini-3.1-Pro-Preview达到53.17%，Claude-Opus-4.6为49.92%，GPT-5.4为48.55%，距离饱和仍有很大提升空间。排行榜呈现分层结构：前沿层（>48%）、强模型层（38-45%）、低性能层（仅略高于10%随机基线）。工具增强在五项工具使用评估中提升多达5.17个百分点，但各模型增益差异显著。

Conclusion: KINA基准通过理论保证的学科代表性和激励机制设计，提供了更科学可靠的评估框架。Bootstrap排名稳定性统计量使有限预算下的方差显性化，避免对相邻排名的过度解读。当前顶级模型性能仍远未饱和，分层结构表明模型能力存在明显梯队差异。

Abstract: Knowledge benchmarks for LLMs face three issues: scaling-driven designs that do not operationalize disciplinary representativeness; flat-payment annotation that permits lazy consensus; and unaudited ranking instability under bounded test budgets. We introduce KINA, an 899-item benchmark across 261 fine-grained disciplines, with two formal results. First, we cast representativeness as a coverage-style objective over expert-elicited anchors and operationalize disciplinary representativeness through a proxy, yielding a (1-1/e) greedy approximation (Proposition 1); the guarantee applies to the proxy, not to population representativeness. Second, we prove a bonus-on-bar tournament weakly FOSD-dominates flat payment in released-review quality, with incentive-compatibility threshold B > Delta C / Delta p_min (Theorem 1). Evaluating 42 models from 13 labs, the top model, Gemini-3.1-Pro-Preview, reaches 53.17%, followed by Claude-Opus-4.6 at 49.92% and GPT-5.4 at 48.55%, leaving substantial headroom below saturation. The full leaderboard shows a tiered structure rather than a smooth total order: a small frontier tier lies above 48%, a dense strong-model tier spans roughly 38-45%, and low-performing models remain only modestly above the 10% chance baseline. Tool augmentation adds up to 5.17 points across the five tool-use evaluations, with gains varying substantially across models. We report bootstrap ranking-stability statistics to make bounded-budget variance explicit and to discourage over-interpretation of adjacent ranks.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [Anycast Performance in Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04298)
*Eric Liang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文对比分析了IP任播在根DNS和CDN两种场景下的延迟表现差异，发现两者不能使用相同的目标函数进行优化：根DNS应优先考虑鲁棒性、可达性和缓存行为，而CDN服务则应优先考虑尾延迟、捕获区正确性和策略控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IP任播被广泛应用于DNS根服务器系统、公共解析器和某些内容分发网络，但相同的路由机制在不同应用场景下会产生截然不同的后果。目前缺乏对不同场景下任播延迟表现的系统性比较分析，这导致运营商可能采用不恰当的优化策略。

Method: 本文采用对比分析方法，比较根DNS和CDN两种设置下的任播延迟表现；提出可复现的测量设计方案；构建比较延迟模型；开发优化框架，将弹性驱动的任播目标与延迟驱动的目标分离。

Result: 研究发现根DNS任播即使存在显著的路径膨胀，仍能产生有限的用户可见延迟（因为递归缓存将根服务器延迟分摊到多用户和长TTL值上）；而CDN任播需要主动工程优化对等互连、路由策略、捕获区范围和测量反馈，以保持较小的路径膨胀。

Conclusion: 运营商不应使用相同的目标函数优化根DNS和CDN任播。对于根DNS，鲁棒性、可达性和缓存行为占主导地位；对于CDN服务，尾延迟、捕获区正确性和策略控制占主导地位。这一结论具有实际指导意义。

Abstract: IP anycast lets a service advertise one address from many physical sites, leaving BGP to map each client to a site. It is central to the DNS root server system, public resolvers, and some content delivery networks, yet the same routing mechanism has very different consequences across applications. This paper compares anycast latency in two settings: root DNS, where recursive caching amortizes root-server delay over many users and long time-to-live values, and CDNs, where each additional round trip can directly affect page-load, video-start, or API latency. The synthesis finds that root DNS anycast can exhibit substantial path inflation while still producing limited user-visible delay, whereas CDN anycast requires active engineering of peering, route policy, catchment scope, and measurement feedback to keep inflation small. The paper contributes a comparative latency model, a reproducible measurement design, and an optimization framework that separates resilience-driven anycast objectives from latency-driven objectives. The central conclusion is practical: operators should not optimize root DNS and CDN anycast with the same objective function. For root DNS, robustness, reachability, and cache behavior dominate; for CDN services, tail latency, catchment correctness, and policy control dominate.

</details>


### [191] [Generalizable Multi-Task Learning for Wireless Networks Using Prompt Decision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04328)
*Fatih Temiz,Shavbo Salehi,Melike Erol-Kantarci*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出Prompt Decision Transformer (PromptDT)多任务学习框架，用于解决无线网络中CoMP多小区选择问题，在多任务设置下将QoE提升高达49%，并能零样本或少样本泛化到新的网络配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来无线网络需要快速适应高度异构的环境和动态任务配置。CoMP传输中的最优多小区选择是一个复杂的组合优化问题，需要联合优化多种服务小区组合。传统的深度强化学习方法（如PPO）存在样本效率低、泛化能力差、状态和动作空间变化时重训练成本高等瓶颈。

Method: 提出基于Prompt Decision Transformer的多任务学习框架，将多小区选择问题重新表述为序列建模问题。利用离线轨迹和任务特定提示（prompts），实现跨不同网络配置的可扩展学习，包括变化的基站数量、用户设备数量和调度策略。

Result: 实验结果表明：1）PromptDT在多任务设置下相比基线将QoE提升高达49%；2）模型性能随模型容量增加而正向扩展；3）能够有效泛化到未见过的任务，对新的网络配置实现鲁棒的少样本适应，无需重训练或微调。

Conclusion: PromptDT为AI驱动的无线资源管理提供了新的范式，通过序列建模和多任务学习有效解决了传统DRL方法的关键瓶颈，实现了跨网络配置的可扩展学习和高效泛化，为未来异构无线网络的智能化资源管理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Future wireless networks demand rapid adaptation to highly heterogeneous environments and dynamic task configurations, necessitating a shift from conventional rule-based and optimization-driven radio resource management (RRM) toward artificial intelligence (AI)-driven RRM. AI-driven approaches can learn complex nonlinear relationships, generalize across diverse network conditions and enable real-time, scalable and autonomous decision-making. Among RRM techniques, coordinated multipoint (CoMP) transmission is pivotal for mitigating inter-cell interference and enhancing cell-edge performance, thereby improving quality of experience (QoE) in dense deployments. However, optimal multi-cell selection remains a complex combinatorial challenge as it requires jointly optimizing over many possible serving-cell combinations under dynamic traffic and channel conditions. Despite their success, conventional deep reinforcement learning (DRL) methods such as proximal policy optimization (PPO) suffer from poor sample efficiency, limited generalization, and costly retraining when state and action spaces change. To address these bottlenecks, we propose a Prompt Decision Transformer (PromptDT) based multi-task learning framework capable of learning across diverse network configurations and reformulating multi-cell selection as a sequence modeling problem. By leveraging offline trajectories and task-specific prompts, PromptDT enables scalable learning across diverse network configurations, including varying base stations and user equipment counts, and scheduler policies. Experimental results demonstrate that PromptDT improves QoE by up to 49% in multi-task settings compared to baselines, with performance scaling positively alongside model capacity. Moreover, PromptDT generalizes effectively to unseen tasks, achieving robust few-shot adaptation to new network configurations without retraining or fine-tuning.

</details>


### [192] [Treat Traffic Like Trees: A Semantic-Preserving Hierarchical Graph-Based Expert Framework for Encrypted Traffic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04517)
*Yuantu Luo,Jun Tao,Linxiao Yu,Guang Cheng*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PTGAMoE框架，通过保持协议语义的层次化图结构和专家混合机制，在加密流量分析任务上显著超越现有SOTA模型，并提供了可解释的决策洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的加密流量深度学习方法虽然性能优异，但复杂预处理和模型结构可能掩盖协议内在语义；同时，协议规范中定义的层次化结构（协议层及其字段）在现有学习框架中未被充分探索和利用。

Method: 提出Protocol Tree Graph Attention with Mixture of Experts (PTGAMoE)框架：1) 基于字段的图构建方法保持协议语义；2) 专家委员会设计量化模型对特定字段和协议的偏好；3) 层次化图注意力机制建模协议结构。

Result: 在严格无数据泄漏设置下，PTGAMoE在代表性基准数据集上显著超越当前SOTA模型；语义保持设计提供了协议级特征重要性和专家级贡献的可解释性洞察。

Conclusion: PTGAMoE成功将协议规范知识融入深度学习框架，实现了性能提升与可解释性的双重目标，为加密流量分析提供了新的语义驱动建模范式。

Abstract: Graph-based deep learning methods have been widely employed in encrypted traffic analysis to exploit latent correlations across different granularities. However, while complex preprocessing pipelines and sophisticated model structures often achieve strong performance, they may obscure inherent protocol semantics during representation learning. Moreover, the hierarchical structure of protocol layers and their corresponding fields, defined by protocol specifications and routinely utilized in manual traffic analysis, remains underexplored in existing learning frameworks. In this paper, we propose Protocol Tree Graph Attention with Mixture of Experts (PTGAMoE), a semantic-preserving hierarchical graph-based expert framework for encrypted traffic analysis. The field-based graph construction and expert committee design enable PTGAMoE to quantify the model's preferences for specific fields and protocols. Extensive experimental results on representative benchmark datasets under strict no-data-leakage settings demonstrate that PTGAMoE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art (SOTA) models. Furthermore, the semantic-preserving design provides interpretable insights into protocol-level feature importance and expert-level contributions, reflecting the model's decision-making logic in encrypted traffic classification tasks.

</details>


### [193] [From Network Experience to Subscriber Retention: An Explainable AI Framework for Mobile Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04838)
*Faris B. Mismar,Abdol Saleh,Ivan Maxmillian Putra Pasaribu,Suhelmy Syaifuddin*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于可解释人工智能和机器学习的移动运营商用户流失预测框架，通过全球领先电信运营商的真实数据验证，发现用户体验质量（QoE）指标比传统网络计数器提供更强的流失信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动运营商面临用户流失问题，传统基于网络计数器的分析方法预测能力有限。随着数据驱动方法的发展，需要构建一个更加准确且可解释的用户流失预测框架，以帮助运营商识别高风险用户并采取针对性措施。

Method: 提出一个融合可解释人工智能（XAI）和机器学习的数据驱动框架用于预测用户流失。该框架在全球领先的电信运营商真实数据上实施，数据涵盖数千万用户规模，确保框架的实用性和可扩展性。

Result: 实验结果表明：（1）所提框架在真实大规模数据上表现出鲁棒性和有效性；（2）用户体验质量（QoE）指标比传统网络计数器提供更强的流失预测信号；（3）框架能够提供可操作的洞察，验证了其长期实用价值。

Conclusion: 研究证实了以QoE为中心的分析方法在现代电信运营中的必要性。未来研究方向包括进一步提高流失预测的准确性以及推动该框架在运营环境中的实际部署应用。

Abstract: This article presents a framework for the prediction of subscriber churn in mobile operators also known as telecommunication operators (or telcos). This framework covers relevant aspects of data-driven approaches using explainable artificial intelligence and machine learning. To demonstrate the robustness of the framework, we implement it on real data from one of the globally leading telcos with tens of millions of subscribers and show results and actionable insights confirming the usefulness and longevity of the framework. Our results suggest that subscriber quality of experience (QoE) indicators provide stronger churn signals than traditional network counters alone, reinforcing the need for QoE-centric analytics in modern operations in telcos. We conclude with future research directions for improving churn predictability and operational deployment.

</details>


### [194] [Demo: BeGREEN Intelligence Plane for AI-driven Energy Efficient O-RAN management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05000)
*M. Catalan-Cid,D. Reiss,G. Castellanos,J. Armstrong*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文展示了一个基于AI的O-RAN网络能效管理解决方案——BeGREEN智能平台，通过rApps和xApps优化实现蜂窝网络能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着移动网络对全球能源消耗影响日益显著，确保能源可持续性变得至关重要。O-RAN架构和AI/ML技术的结合为实现RAN优化的自动化智能控制环路提供了可能，从而推动网络能效管理的创新发展。

Method: 提出BeGREEN智能平台，一个面向O-RAN网络能效管理的AI驱动解决方案。该方案通过AI引擎与rApps、xApps的深度集成，实现对仿真小区运行状态的智能控制和工作流程优化。

Result: 成功演示了该平台在O-RAN网络能效管理中的应用效果，验证了AI引擎与rApps/xApps集成方案的可行性，实现了对小区运行状态的有效控制和优化。

Conclusion: BeGREEN智能平台为O-RAN网络提供了一种有效的AI驱动能效管理方案，展示了AI技术在蜂窝网络节能优化中的实际应用价值，为实现可持续移动网络运营提供了技术路径。

Abstract: Cellular networks management is being enhanced by O-RAN architecture and AI/ML solutions, enabling automated intelligent control loops for RAN optimization across various use cases. Ensuring energy sustainability is crucial to minimizing the impact of mobile networks on global energy consumption. This demo showcases the BeGREEN Intelligence Plane, an AI-driven solution for energy-efficient management of O-RAN networks. The presented workflow focuses on controlling the operational status of emulated cells, highlighting the integration of key components such as the AI Engine and the optimizations achieved through rApps and xApps

</details>


### [195] [COSMO: O-RAN-Based Service Management and Orchestration for Cross-Technology Multi-Tenant Radio Access Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05012)
*M. Catalan-Cid,J. J. Aleixendri,J. Pueyo,P. Tomas,D. Camps-Mur*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了COSMO平台，这是一个面向6G网络的无线接入网(RAN)服务管理与编排平台，支持异构的3GPP(5G NR, LTE)和非3GPP(Wi-Fi)接入网络，实现了跨技术多租户资源共享和统一编排。实验证明该平台能有效降低SLA违规率从21%降至10%以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向6G网络演进过程中，需要支持包含私有5G、公共4G/5G和Wi-Fi等多样化接入技术的异构RAN，并由多个利益相关者管理。然而现有基于O-RAN的框架研究主要集中在rApp和xApp的实现与验证，缺乏对跨技术RAN编排、端到端多租户以及统一SMO功能(包括Non-RT RIC组件)的集成支持。

Method: 设计并实现了COSMO平台，该平台具备以下核心功能：(1)支持异构3GPP和非3GPP接入网络的统一管理；(2)实现跨技术多租户能力，允许多个租户基于SLA动态共享异构RAN资源并获得明确的资源分配保障；(3)提供支持灵活按需资源分配的管理原语；(4)集成跨技术Non-RT RIC，支持开发智能rApps实现闭环控制和网络编排。

Result: 通过原型实现和基准测试验证了COSMO的有效性。在包含5G、4G和Wi-Fi接入网络的异构RAN部署中，基于SLA的rApp在动态流量条件下将SLA违规率从约21%降低到10%以下。实验结果证实COSMO在资源分配、SLA执行和可扩展性方面均表现良好。

Conclusion: COSMO为未来多租户跨技术RAN环境的管理与编排提供了高效解决方案，通过统一编排异构多租户RAN资源，提高了资源利用率和运营灵活性，为6G网络中异构接入技术的协同管理奠定了基础。

Abstract: The evolution toward 6G networks envisions a heterogeneous Radio Access Network (RAN) comprising diverse access technologies, such as private 5G, public 4G/5G, and Wi-Fi, managed by multiple stakeholders. While considerable research effort has been devoted to O-RAN-based frameworks enabling rApp and xApp implementation and validation, few works provide integrated support for cross-technology RAN orchestration, end-to-end multi-tenancy, and a unified subset of SMO functionalities, including Non-RT RIC components. This paper introduces COSMO, a novel RAN Service Management and Orchestration platform designed to support heterogeneous 3GPP (5G NR, LTE) and non-3GPP (Wi-Fi) access networks. COSMO enables cross-technology multi-tenancy, defined as the capability to allow multiple tenants to dynamically share heterogeneous RAN resources with explicit resource allocation guarantees based on Service Level Agreements (SLAs). This is achieved through management primitives that support flexible and on-demand resource allocation. Additionally, the platform includes a cross-technology Non-Real-Time RAN Intelligent Controller (Non-RT RIC) that enables the development of intelligent rApps for closed-loop control and network orchestration. Beyond its architectural design, COSMO improves resource utilization and operational flexibility through unified orchestration of heterogeneous multi-tenant RAN resources. Through prototyping and benchmarking, we demonstrate the effectiveness of COSMO in resource allocation, SLA enforcement, and scalability. In our prototype, the SLA-based rApp reduces SLA violation from approximately 21% to below 10% under dynamic traffic conditions in a heterogeneous RAN deployment including 5G, 4G, and Wi-Fi access networks. Our results confirm that COSMO offers an efficient solution for managing and orchestrating future multi-tenant cross-technology RAN environments.

</details>


### [196] [A Practical AI-Driven Strategy for Cell On/Off Switching under Adaptable QoS Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05019)
*David Reiss,Miguel Catalan-Cid,Daniel Camps,Oriol Sallent*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出基于LSTM的5G基站开关策略，可在推理阶段通过调节阈值动态适应不同QoS需求，无需重训练即可在能耗节省与服务保障间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G网络密集化部署导致整体功耗显著上升，但现有节能研究大多无法动态适应运营商定义的服务质量(QoS)要求，缺乏灵活性与实用性。

Method: 利用欧洲移动网络运营商的真实数据集训练LSTM模型，通过在推理阶段调节决策阈值，实现对目标吞吐量与中断容限约束的动态适应，无需重新训练模型。

Result: 在未见测试周数据上达到理想Oracle方案63%~96%的节能效果，同时基本满足运营商指定的QoS约束，并量化了CO2排放与运营支出(OPEX)的潜在收益。

Conclusion: 该方法为运营商提供了一种可实际部署的节能方案，在无需重训练的前提下实现了能耗节省与服务保障的灵活权衡，具有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of 5G networks has intensified concerns over their sustainability, as denser Radio Access Network (RAN) deployments have increased overall power consumption. Although numerous studies have examined energy-efficient cell on/off switching, few have focused on approaches capable of dynamically adapting to operator-defined Quality of Service (QoS) requirements. In this paper, we propose a Long Short Term Memory (LSTM)based strategy, trained using a dataset from a European Mobile Network Operator (MNO), that enforces both target throughput levels and outage-tolerance constraints. Unlike previous approaches, our model adapts to different QoS requirements by tuning a decision threshold at inference time, enabling operators to balance energy savings and service guarantees without retraining. Across an unseen week, the method attains 63 to 96 % of an oracle's energy savings while largely meeting operator-specified constraints. We also provide CO2 and OPEX estimates under representative scenarios to quantify potential operator benefits.

</details>


### [197] [Bridging High-Level Intent and Network Execution: Detecting Violations and Intent Drift Through Low-Level Traffic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05076)
*Tonia Haikal,Shereen Ismail,Eman Hammad*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文基于意图网络(IBN)，通过形式化流头部为7元组向量建立底层意图(ILI)遥测接口，发现放宽策略虽减少违规计数但意图漂移基本不变的“合规悖论”，证明传统违规跟踪不可靠，并验证了闭环编排器纠正违规的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在面向6G的自治网络中，意图驱动网络(IBN)将高层管理目标转化为自治配置，但在声明式意图与数据平面执行之间存在关键的验证缺口，即如何确保底层执行真正符合高层意图。

Method: 提出将底层流头部形式化为标准7元组向量，建立内部底层意图(ILI)遥测接口；利用分布式蜜罐网络采集的1.0091亿条流记录数据集，针对三种管理策略机制（严格、均衡、宽松），使用策略违规(V)和意图漂移(D)两个指标进行评估分析。

Result: 发现了一个显著的“合规悖论”：随着策略宽松度的增加，违规计数系统性地减少，但潜在的运营意图漂移却基本保持不变。这证明传统以违规为中心的跟踪方法是不可靠的。此外，实例研究表明ILI指标能有效识别结构性违规，指导闭环编排器动态重算并执行底层规则以维持高层运营意图。

Conclusion: 传统的违规计数跟踪方法无法准确反映意图执行的真实状态，需要引入意图漂移等更本质的指标。所提出的ILI遥测接口及指标能够为闭环编排器提供有效反馈，实现对意图执行偏差的动态纠正，为6G自治网络的意图验证提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Intent-Based Networking (IBN) structures a core management pillar for autonomous 6G networks by translating high-level administrative goals into autonomous configurations, yet a critical validation gap persists between declarative intent and data-plane execution. This paper investigates this gap by formalizing low-level flow headers into standardized 7-tuple vectors, establishing an Internal Low-Level Intent (ILI) telemetry interface. Leveraging an empirical dataset of 100.91 million flow records from a distributed honeynet, we evaluate three administrative policy regimes (Strict, Balanced, and Permissive) across two metrics: Policy Violations ($V$) and Intent Drift ($D$). Our results expose a distinct Compliance Paradox where widening policy permissiveness systematically suppresses violation counts, yet underlying operational intent drift remains mostly invariant. This demonstrates that conventional, violation-centric tracking are unreliable. Furthermore, an empirical case study show that ILI metrics structural violations can inform closed-loop orchestrators to dynamically recalculate and enforce low-level rules that maintain high-level operational intent.

</details>


### [198] [RAMC: Remote Access Memory Channels over HPE Slingshot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05094)
*Whit Schonbein,Matthew G. F. Dosanjh,Scott Levy*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: RAMC是一种基于HPE Cray Slingshot网络的显式单向通信库，通过持久化单向通道和内存区域计数器实现高效完成通知，在250节点19600进程规模下展现良好扩展性，带宽性能相比Cray MPI提升30%-130%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单向通信框架（如MPI RMA和OpenSHMEM）依赖整体式共享内存模型，假设对称内存区域或需要阻塞式集合操作来创建窗口，导致扩展性和可用性挑战；隐式模型（PGAS/UPC）虽然简化编程但依赖显式机制；MPI分区通信API牺牲了RDMA的动态灵活性。

Method: 基于持久化单向通信通道的核心概念，利用Slingshot网络硬件独特的内存区域计数器实现高效的完成通知机制，设计显式单向通信库RAMC。

Result: 基于RAMC的热扩散代码可扩展至250节点上19.6千个进程；在多个libfabric版本上的微基准测试表明，RAMC性能优于Cray专有MPI实现：在libfabric 1.15.2下1B-4KiB消息带宽提升约100%-130%，在libfabric 2.3.1下提升约30%-45%；同时也识别出小消息延迟等需要改进的领域。

Conclusion: RAMC成功解决了现有单向通信框架的局限性，在保持良好扩展性的同时显著提升了带宽性能，为HPE Cray Slingshot网络提供了一种高效的单向通信解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we present Remote Access Memory Channels (RAMC), an explicit one-sided communication library designed to leverage the capabilities of HPE Cray Slingshot network hardware. Existing one-sided communication frameworks, such as MPI RMA and OpenSHMEM, rely on monolithic shared memory models that introduce scalability and usability challenges. These frameworks often assume symmetric memory regions or require blocking collective operations for window creation, which can mismatch user communication needs and hinder performance. Implicit models, such as PGAS and UPC, aim to simplify programming by treating local and remote memory as a unified region but ultimately rely on explicit mechanisms to implement data movement. MPI's recently-introduced partitioned communication API offers a persistent point-to-point interface but sacrifices the dynamic flexibility of RDMA. RAMC is designed to address these limitations. Based on the core concept of a persistent uni-directional communication channel, RAMC leverages Slingshot's unique memory region counters to enable efficient completion notification. Experiments with a RAMC-based heat diffusion code demonstrate RAMC has no difficulty scaling to 19.6 thousand processes across 250 nodes, and microbenchmark studies across multiple libfabric versions show RAMC can outperform Cray's proprietary MPI implementation (e.g., increases in bandwidth ranging from approx. 100%-130% for 1B-4KiB messages under libfabric 1.15.2, and from approx. 30%-45% under libfabric 2.3.1) while identifying additional areas for improvement, such as small message latencies.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [199] [Exploring the Topology and Memory of Consensus: How LLM Agents Agree, Fragment, or Settle When Forming Conventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04197)
*Aliakbar Mehdizadeh,Martin Hilbert*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 在多智能体协调中，记忆深度的效果取决于网络拓扑结构：在去中心化网络中，更长的记忆会减缓稳态达成，但在中心化网络中却会加速稳态达成（这可能导致锁定在碎片化状态而非达成全局共识）。因此，记忆深度与通信拓扑应协同设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究两个关键设计选择——LLM智能体的记忆深度和多智能体系统的连接结构——如何相互作用影响协调效果。核心问题在于这些设计选择不应被孤立优化，而是需要共同考虑。

Method: 在八种固定的16智能体网络拓扑结构上进行了432次网络化命名游戏（Naming Game）仿真实验，系统地改变记忆深度和网络结构。使用中介中心性分析智能体层面的行为，并采用虚拟博弈（Fictitious Play）框架分析智能体决策机制。

Result: 发现记忆深度的效果因拓扑结构而发生方向性翻转：在去中心化网络中延长记忆会减缓稳态达成，但在中心化网络中却加速稳态达成。中心化网络更快锁定在碎片化状态而非达成全局共识，可用于生成意见分歧。存在记忆中介的速度-统一性权衡。高中介中心性的桥接节点遭受经纪惩罚，而局部聚集邻域中的智能体实现更高的协调成功。智能体选择符合虚拟博弈模型，表明基于信念而非基于奖励的适应。

Conclusion: 记忆深度和通信拓扑结构存在交互作用，不应被孤立优化。实践意义在于这两个设计要素需要协同设计。研究结果为设计LLM智能体记忆机制和多智能体系统通信架构提供了重要指导。

Abstract: How much should an LLM agent remember, and how should multi-agent systems be connected when trying to reach consensus? We show these two design choices interact in a way that flips the sign of memory's effect on coordination. Across 432 simulation runs of a networked Naming Game on eight fixed 16-agent topologies, we vary memory depth and network structure. Longer memory slows the time to reach steady state in decentralized networks but accelerates it in centralized ones; the same parameter pushes the system in opposite directions depending on topology. Critically, "faster settling" in centralized networks means locking in to a fragmented plateau more quickly, not reaching system-wide consensus, which can be used to generate diverging opinions. We further document a memory-mediated speed-unity trade-off: centralized networks consistently preserve more competing conventions than decentralized networks, but their settling speed depends sharply on memory. At the agent level, within-network analyses show that high-betweenness bridges suffer a brokerage penalty while agents in locally clustered neighborhoods achieve higher coordination success. Finally, in search of analytically tractable generative mechanisms, we find that agents' choices are well captured by Fictitious Play, indicating belief-based rather than reward-based adaptation. The practical implication: memory depth and communication topology should be co-designed, not optimized in isolation.

</details>


### [200] [Organizational Control Layer: Governance Infrastructure at the Execution Boundary of LLM Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04306)
*Tianyu Shi,Yang Mo,Yiou Liu,Zhuonan Hao,Yin Wang,Wenzhuo Hu,Nan Yu,Meng Zhou,Jiangbo Yu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM智能体在直接触发状态改变操作时的执行边界问题，提出了组织控制层（OCL）——一种模型无关的治理基础设施，在执行前拦截生成的操作。实验表明，OCL将不安全执行率从88%降至接近零，同时将有效成功率从12%提升至96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体越来越多地部署在工作流中，其生成的输出可能直接触发状态改变的操作，这带来了执行边界问题：提议的操作在执行前必须受到治理管控。当前缺乏有效的机制来分离提议生成与环境执行，存在安全隐患。

Method: 引入组织控制层（OCL），这是一种模型无关的治理基础设施，通过策略执行和升级机制，在不修改底层LLM生成器的情况下，在执行前拦截生成的操作。在改编自AgenticPay的对抗性买家-卖家谈判环境中进行评估，测试多个前沿LLM后端。

Result: OCL将不安全执行率从88%降至接近零，同时将有效成功率从12%提升至96%。研究还发现了安全-效用权衡：严格的治理提高了合规性和可靠性，但在约束严格的市场中可能降低灵活性。

Conclusion: 部署级LLM智能体系统需要在语言生成和可执行操作之间的边界处进行显式治理，将提议生成与环境执行分离是确保系统安全可靠的关键。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly deployed in workflows where generated outputs may directly trigger state-changing actions. This creates an execution-boundary problem: proposed actions must be governed before they are executed. We study this problem through economically consequential multi-agent interactions and argue that deployment-grade agent systems should separate proposal generation from environment-facing execution. To operationalize this principle, we introduce the Organizational Control Layer (OCL), a model-agnostic governance infrastructure that intercepts generated actions before execution through policy enforcement and escalation, without modifying the underlying LLM generator. We evaluate OCL on adversarial buyer--seller negotiation environments adapted from AgenticPay. Across multiple frontier LLM backends, OCL reduces unsafe executions from 88% to near-zero while increasing valid success from 12% to 96%. Results further reveal a safety--utility tradeoff: strict governance improves compliance and reliability against policy and constraint violations, but can reduce flexibility in tightly constrained markets. These findings suggest that deployment-grade LLM agent systems require explicit governance at the boundary between language generation and executable actions. The source code is available at: https://github.com/SHITIANYU-hue/amai_ocl

</details>


### [201] [Channel Fracture: Architectural Blind Spots in Scheduled Cross-Agent Memory Injection for Multi-Agent Orchestration Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04896)
*Levent Liu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文发现了多智能体AI编排系统中的“通道断裂”故障模式，即定时（cron）代理因硬编码隔离限制无法写入目标代理的持久记忆，并提出包含13维验证的CADVP协议加以解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分层团队架构中，一个代理常需向另一代理的持久记忆注入知识，但现有注入机制缺乏架构层面的保障，可能导致静默失败，因此亟需识别故障模式并建立可靠的交付验证框架。

Method: 在生产环境的Hermes Agent部署（含五个专用配置文件）上，实验比较三种注入通道：（A）直接SQLite数据库写入；（B）目标代理通过记忆工具自我写入；（C）cron代理委托写入。同时提出CADVP v1.1验证框架，包含13个维度与CC-0否决级通道确认检查。

Result: 通道A与B可行，通道C完全失败；失败根源为调度层硬编码skip_memory=True与cron执行上下文跳过_memory_manager初始化，导致记忆工具动态注册被绕过。

Conclusion: 多智能体系统中跨代理记忆注入存在系统性风险，需在设计层面遵循逆向验证原则与通道匹配原则，并采用CADVP等验证框架以避免假阳性交付保证。

Abstract: Multi-agent AI orchestration systems increasingly rely on persistent memory to maintain context across sessions, agents, and tasks. When one agent must inject knowledge into another agent's memory -- a common requirement in hierarchical team architectures -- the delivery mechanism must be architecturally sound. We report the discovery of a systematic failure mode we term channel fracture: a condition where scheduled (cron) agents in orchestration frameworks are silently unable to write to the target agent's persistent memory due to hardcoded memory isolation guards. Through experiments on a production Hermes Agent deployment with five specialized profiles, we tested three injection channels: (A) direct SQLite database writes, (B) target-agent self-writes via memory tools, and (C) cron-delegated writes. Channel C failed completely due to two architectural constraints: skip_memory=True hardcoded at the scheduler layer and dynamic registration of memory tools contingent on _memory_manager initialization, which is bypassed in cron execution contexts. We propose CADVP (Cross-Agent Delivery Verification Protocol) v1.1, a 13-dimension verification framework with a veto-level channel confirmation check (CC-0) that prevents false-positive delivery assurance. We articulate two design principles: the inverse verification principle and the channel matching principle.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [202] [UltraEP: Unleash MoE Training and Inference on Rack-Scale Nodes with Near-Optimal Load Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04101)
*Xinming Wei,Chao Jin,Tuo Dai,Yinmin Zhong,Shan Yu,Chengxu Yang,Bingyang Wu,Zili Zhang,Jing Mai,Qianchao Zhu,Zhouyang Li,Yuliang Liu,Guojie Luo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: UltraEP是首个面向大规模专家并行MoE模型的精确负载实时均衡器，通过微批次级别的实时重平衡，实现了理想吞吐量的94.3%，相比无均衡方案提升1.49倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模专家并行(EP)在训练和推理中变得关键，但会放大设备级专家负载不均衡问题，导致计算落后者、令牌全收集瓶颈和激活内存峰值。现有均衡器基于历史负载进行周期性重分配，在生产环境中面对非平稳负载模式时可靠性不足。

Method: UltraEP利用机架规模节点(RSN)的扩展纵向连接性，在每个微批次和层的关键路径上进行重平衡。采用配额驱动的高效规划方法响应门控后负载，并使用RSN原生持久化平铺流和中继式扇出消减技术执行不规则专家状态传输，最小化暴露开销。

Result: 在106B至671B参数的MoE模型训练和预填充任务中，UltraEP达到强制均衡理想吞吐量的94.3%，相比无均衡方案提升1.49倍。跨rank的最终负载不均衡度从1.30-4.01降至1.01-1.04。在2560 GPU的生产MoE训练中验证了其可扩展性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: UltraEP成功实现了大规模EP MoE训练和推理预填充的精确负载实时均衡，有效解决了负载不均衡带来的性能瓶颈，具有显著的性能提升和实际部署价值。

Abstract: Large-scale expert parallelism (EP) is becoming pivotal for training and serving frontier MoE models, but it also amplifies device-level expert load imbalance into compute stragglers, token all-to-all bottlenecks, and activation-memory spikes. Existing balancers redistribute experts periodically based on historical load, which becomes unreliable for production deployments with non-stationary load patterns.
  We present UltraEP, the first exact-load, real-time balancer for large-EP MoE training and serving prefill on rack-scale nodes (RSNs). Built upon the extended scale-up connectivity of RSNs, UltraEP rebalances every microbatch and layer on critical paths, which requires nontrivial co-design of plan solving and expert replication communication to minimize exposed overhead. To this end, UltraEP eagerly reacts to post-gating load with efficient quota-driven planning, and executes the resulting irregular expert-state transfers with RSN-native persistent tile streaming and relay-based fan-out mitigation. Averaged across MoE models from 106B to 671B parameters in training and prefill, UltraEP achieves 94.3% of the force-balanced ideal throughput, delivering 1.49$\times$ improvement over non-balancing, while reducing the final inter-rank imbalance from 1.30$-$4.01 to 1.01$-$1.04. Additionally, we validate UltraEP's scalability and robustness in production MoE training with 2560 GPUs.

</details>


### [203] [ACEAPEX: Parallel LZ77 Decoding via Encode-Time Absolute Offset Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04268)
*Yakiv Shavidze*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出ACEAPEX，一种并行LZ77编解码器，通过存储绝对位置引用实现块级并行解码。CPU解码速度达10.8 GB/s，GPU解码达77.2 GB/s（单卡），是目前首个在接近标准压缩率下实现GPU LZ77解码的工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LZ77编解码器存在根本性串行瓶颈：每个后向引用依赖于先前解压的数据，阻碍了多核扩展和并行化加速。

Method: 设计ACEAPEX编解码器：1) 将所有后向引用存储为解压输出中的绝对位置；2) 将数据组织为自包含的1 MB数据块，实现易并行块级解码；3) 在GPU上实现波前解码器。

Result: CPU性能：在EPYC 4344P（8核）上达10,160 MB/s，在EPYC 9575F上达10,869 MB/s（FASTQ数据），比zstd -3快3.1倍。GPU性能：NVIDIA H100单卡达77.2 GB/s（enwik9），双卡NVLink配置达249.9 GB/s；波前匹配阶段在enwik9上达44.0 GB/s，FASTQ上达20.3 GB/s，逐字节验证正确。

Conclusion: 首次实现具有接近标准压缩率的GPU LZ77解码，压缩率损失仅1.5%（enwik9数据集），为LZ77编解码器的并行化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: LZ77-based codecs exhibit a fundamental sequential bottleneck in decoding: each back-reference depends on previously decompressed data, preventing multi-core scaling. We present ACEAPEX, a parallel LZ77 codec that stores all back-references as absolute positions in the decompressed output and organizes data into self-contained 1 MB blocks, enabling embarrassingly parallel block-level decoding. Integrated into lzbench, ACEAPEX achieves 10,160 MB/s on EPYC 4344P (8 cores) and 10,869 MB/s on EPYC 9575F for FASTQ genomic data -- up to 3.1x faster than zstd -3 at comparable compression ratios. We further implement a GPU wavefront decoder on NVIDIA H100 SXM, measuring 44.0 GB/s on enwik9 and 20.3 GB/s on FASTQ (wavefront match phase, BIT-PERFECT verified). With a depth-limited encoder variant (-1.5% ratio on enwik9), GPU throughput reaches 77.2 GB/s on a single H100 and 249.9 GB/s on two H100s in NVLink configuration. To our knowledge, this is the first reported GPU LZ77 decode with near-standard compression ratio verified byte-for-byte.

</details>


### [204] [FlexNPU: Transparent NPU Virtualization for Dynamic LLM Prefill-Decode Co-location](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04415)
*Jiongjiong Gu,Jianfeng Wang,Zidong Han,Yongqiao Wang,Pengfei Xia,Mingjie Zhang,Hong Liu,Yuanyi Xia,Jiajia Chu,Yifeng Tang,Hui Zang,Xin Yao,Qijie Qiu,Yuzhao Wang,Chuanfei Xu,Lin Zhang,Zhuonan Lai,Hongming Huang,Jiawei Qiu,Gong Zhang,Zhong Ming,Weipeng Cao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了FlexNPU，一个面向Ascend NPU的透明用户态虚拟化层，通过动态prefill-decode协同调度有效解决了LLM服务中阶段间资源干扰问题，在保持低开销的同时显著提升了吞吐量和响应速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前NPU部署通常将物理设备直接暴露给应用程序，限制了运行时调度控制，难以适应阶段级工作负载行为。这一问题在LLM服务中尤为突出：prefill阶段计算密集，而decode阶段受限于内存带宽和KV-cache访问。现有的静态prefill-decode分离虽能减少阶段干扰，但会引入资源不平衡和不必要的数据移动。

Method: 提出FlexNPU透明用户态虚拟化层，通过拦截AscendCL API并将NPU操作路由到每设备守护进程，在不修改模型代码、AI框架或NPU驱动的情况下解耦未修改应用与物理NPU设备。该运行时边界使FlexNPU能够虚拟化NPU对象、控制算子分发，并支持LLM服务的阶段感知调度。核心创新是动态PD协同定位，根据prefill和decode的互补资源特性自适应调整调度策略。

Result: 在华为Ascend NPU上实现并评估。与直接NPU透传相比，FlexNPU不引入可测量的推理开销，某些场景下吞吐量略有提升。在384卡Ascend 910C部署DeepSeek-R1时，相比静态PD分离，吞吐量提升5.15%和26.33%。在Qwen2.5-7B上，相比静态PD协同定位，FlexNPU在保持相当吞吐量的同时，在各测试工作负载下TTFT降低超过92%，TPOT几乎不变。

Conclusion: 透明NPU虚拟化是高效响应式LLM服务的实用基础架构。FlexNPU成功证明了通过用户态虚拟化实现动态阶段感知调度的可行性，为LLM服务优化提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Modern AI serving increasingly relies on NPUs for conventional inference and large language model serving. However, current NPU deployments commonly expose physical devices directly to applications, which limits runtime control over scheduling and makes it difficult to adapt execution to phase-level workload behavior. This limitation is particularly evident in LLM serving, where the prefill phase is compute-intensive while the decode phase is often constrained by memory bandwidth and KV-cache accesses. Static prefill-decode (PD) disaggregation reduces phase interference, but can introduce resource imbalance and unnecessary data movement. We present FlexNPU, a transparent user-space virtualization layer for Ascend NPUs. FlexNPU interposes on AscendCL APIs and routes NPU operations through per-device daemons, decoupling unmodified from physical NPU devices without modifying model code, AI frameworks, or NPU drivers. This runtime boundary allows FlexNPU to virtualize NPU objects, control operator dispatch, and support phase-aware scheduling for LLM serving. In particular, FlexNPU enables dynamic PD co-location, which adapts scheduling between prefill and decode according to their complementary resource characteristics. We implement FlexNPU on Huawei Ascend NPUs and evaluate it with typical LLM workloads. Compared with direct NPU passthrough, FlexNPU introduces no measurable inference overhead and slightly improves throughput in some scenarios. On a 384-card Ascend 910C deployment of DeepSeek-R1, FlexNPU improves throughput over static PD disaggregation by 5.15% and 26.33%. On Qwen2.5-7B, compared with static PD co-location, FlexNPU maintains comparable throughput while reducing TTFT by over 92% across tested workloads with nearly unchanged TPOT. These results show that transparent NPU virtualization is a practical substrate for efficient and responsive LLM serving.

</details>


### [205] [D^2SD: Accelerating Speculative Decoding with Dual Diffusion Draft Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04446)
*Liyuan Zhang,Jiarui Zhang,Jinwei Yao,Ran Yan,Yuchen Yang,Jiahao Zhang,Tongkai Yang,Yi Wu,Binhang Yuan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出D²SD双扩散投机解码框架，通过组织候选序列为置信度引导的前缀树，利用两个扩散起草器生成和恢复候选，显著提升大语言模型推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的投机解码方法在验证时一旦出现首个不匹配即丢弃后续所有草稿token，导致接受率受限；简单地批处理更多候选序列收益甚微，因为冗余或位置不当的分支会增加起草和验证成本而不会成比例提升接受token数量。

Method: 提出D²SD双扩散投机解码框架：(1) 第一个扩散起草器生成一个token块及每个位置的置信度分数，用于识别最可能的拒绝边界并选择top-K前缀范围进行恢复；(2) 第二个可变前缀扩散起草器在每个选定的前缀处重新锚定，在一次批处理中提出替代续写；(3) 通过级联注意力对共享前缀的候选序列进行联合验证。

Result: 实验表明，D²SD相比底层扩散方法和强自回归投机解码基线均显示出明显的改进。

Conclusion: D²SD框架通过置信度引导的前缀树组织和双扩散起草器的协作，有效解决了现有扩散投机解码方法接受率受限的问题，在保持推理加速的同时显著提升了token接受率。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive large language model inference by drafting multiple tokens and verifying them in a single target-model forward pass. Recent diffusion-based drafters generate an entire block of tokens in parallel but usually commit to a single draft sequence per verification: once the first mismatch occurs, all subsequent draft tokens are discarded, resulting in a limited acceptance rate. Naively batching more draft candidate sequences only introduces a marginal improvement, as redundant or poorly placed branches increase the cost of drafting and verification without proportionally increasing the number of accepted tokens.
  We propose D^2SD, a dual diffusion draft speculative decoding framework that organizes candidates into a confidence-guided prefix tree, where the first diffusion drafter generates a block along with per-position confidence scores that are used to identify the most likely rejection boundary and select the top-K prefix ranges for recovery; the second variable-prefix diffusion drafter re-anchors at each selected prefix and proposes alternative continuations in one batched pass; the resulting shared-prefix candidates are jointly verified via cascade attention. Empirically, D^2SD shows clear improvements over both the underlying diffusion approach and strong autoregressive speculative decoding baselines.

</details>


### [206] [Multi-SPIN: Multi-Access Speculative Inference for Cooperative Token Generation at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04581)
*Haotian Zheng,Zhanwei Wang,Mingyao Cui,Chang Cai,Hongyang Du,Kaibin Huang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Multi-SPIN架构，通过分布式部署投机推理实现边缘系统中多用户协同令牌生成，优化草稿长度控制与带宽分配，相比基准方法提升吞吐量高达88%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机推理原本用于加速大语言模型推理，但在多用户边缘系统中面临计算与通信能力的严重异构性挑战。资源受限设备与服务器之间的计算负载平衡成为关键问题，而草稿长度作为控制变量直接影响节点级计算负载和多接入延迟，进而决定总令牌吞吐量。

Method: 提出Multi-SPIN架构，利用设备端小语言模型生成候选令牌草稿，边缘服务器运行大语言模型进行并行批量验证。针对频分多址接入，研究多接入草稿控制问题，联合优化草稿长度控制和带宽分配以最大化总令牌吞吐量。考虑两种情况：(1)用户间同质草稿长度以便服务器端批处理；(2)异质草稿长度引入吞吐量增强新维度。采用分解方法将复杂优化问题转化为可处理子问题，推导出闭式高效草稿控制算法。

Result: 分析表明：在同质情况下，最优带宽分配补偿计算-通信能力较弱的用户（由于批处理同步要求）；在异质情况下，则通过放松同步要求奖励接受率较高的用户。使用Llama-2和Qwen3.5模型对在多种任务上的实验表明，Multi-SPIN相比忽略异构性的基准方法提升吞吐量高达88%。

Conclusion: Multi-SPIN成功实现了边缘系统中投机推理的分布式部署，通过联合优化草稿长度和带宽分配有效应对用户异构性，显著提升系统吞吐量性能，为边缘环境下的协作式LLM推理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Speculative inference (SPIN) was originally developed as an efficient architecture to accelerate Large Language Models (LLMs). In this work, we propose its distributed deployment to enable cooperative token generation in a multiuser edge system; its advantage is to effectively balance computational loads between resource-constrained devices and servers. The resulting architecture, termed Multi-access SPIN (Multi-SPIN), utilizes on-device small language models to generate and upload candidate token drafts, while an edge server operates the LLM to verify them in parallel batches. Given the severe heterogeneity in users' computation and communication capabilities, the draft length emerges as a critical control variable that influences node-level computation loads and multi-access latency, thereby governing the sum token goodput. Consequently, considering frequency-division multiple access, we investigate the problem of multi-access draft control, a joint optimization of draft-length control and bandwidth allocation to maximize sum token goodput. We examine two cases: (1) homogeneous draft lengths across users to facilitate server-side batching, and (2) heterogeneous draft lengths to introduce a new dimension for goodput enhancement. By developing decomposition methods, we reduce these complex optimizations into tractable sub-problems, which allow efficient draft control algorithms to be derived in closed form. Our analysis shows that the optimal bandwidth allocation compensates users with weaker computation-and-communication capabilities in the homogeneous case due to the batching synchronization requirements, whereas its heterogeneous-case counterpart rewards users with higher acceptance rates by relaxing such requirements. Experiments using Llama-2 and Qwen3.5 model pairs across diverse tasks demonstrate that Multi-SPIN improves goodput by up to 88% over heterogeneity-agnostic baselines.

</details>


### [207] [Ekka: Automated Diagnosis of Silent Errors in LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04594)
*Yile Gu,Zhen Zhang,Shaowei Zhu,Xinwei Fu,Jun Wu,Yida Wang,Baris Kasikci*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Ekka系统，通过将中间执行状态对齐和比较的方式，自动化诊断LLM服务框架中的静默错误，在真实错误基准测试中pass@1准确率达80%，pass@5达88%，并发现4个新的已被开发者确认的错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务框架快速发展，软件栈复杂且优化众多，容易引入静默错误（输出质量下降但无明确错误信号）。由于高层症状与低层根因之间存在巨大语义鸿沟，诊断静默错误极其困难。

Method: 将静默错误诊断框架化为差分调试问题，利用语义正确的参考实现。提出Ekka系统，通过系统性地对齐和比较目标框架与参考框架之间的中间执行状态来识别根因。

Result: 构建了来自流行服务框架的真实静默错误基准测试，Ekka达到80%的pass@1诊断准确率和88%的pass@5诊断准确率，超越现有最先进系统。Ekka还诊断出服务框架中的4个新静默错误，均已获开发者确认。

Conclusion: 通过将静默错误诊断转化为差分调试问题，并系统对齐比较中间执行状态，Ekka能够有效自动化诊断LLM服务框架中的静默错误，显著优于现有方法，并具有实际发现新错误的能力。

Abstract: LLM serving frameworks are quickly evolving with a complex software stack and a vast number of optimizations. The rapid development process can introduce silent errors where output quality silently degrades without any explicit error signals. Diagnosing silent errors is notoriously difficult due to the substantial semantic gap between the high-level symptoms and the low-level root causes. We observe that diagnosis of silent errors can be effectively framed as a differential debugging problem by leveraging the existence of semantically correct reference implementations. We propose Ekka, an automated diagnosis system that identifies root causes by systematically aligning and comparing intermediate execution states between a target and a reference framework. We constructed a benchmark of real-world silent errors from popular serving frameworks, where Ekka shows 80% pass@1 diagnosis accuracy and 88% pass@5 diagnosis accuracy, outperforming state-of-the-art systems. Ekka also diagnoses 4 new silent errors from serving frameworks, all of which have been confirmed by the developers.

</details>


### [208] [Rectangular Matrix Multiplication in the Low-Bandwidth Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04652)
*Chetan Gupta,Jukka Suomela,Hossein Vahidi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 研究了低带宽分布式模型下的矩形矩阵乘法，发现了两种自然长宽比$(n,d,n)$和$(d,n,d)$具有根本不同的复杂度特性，其中$(n,d,n)$在$d=\sqrt{n}$处呈现相变现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要关注稀疏假设下的方阵乘法，而本文研究无稀疏假设的矩形矩阵乘法，探索复杂度如何依赖于矩阵维度参数$d$和$n$。

Method: 在低带宽分布式计算模型中研究两种矩形矩阵乘法任务：$\langle n,d,n\rangle$（$n\times d$矩阵乘$d\times n$矩阵）和$\langle d,n,d\rangle$（$d\times n$矩阵乘$n\times d$矩阵），分析其轮复杂度与参数$d$和$n$的关系。

Result: 对于$\langle d,n,d\rangle$达到$\tilde{O}(d^{4/3})$复杂度，自然推广了方阵情形；对于$\langle n,d,n\rangle$发现相变现象：当$d\leq\sqrt{n}$时复杂度为$\Theta(d\sqrt{n})$（上下界匹配），当$d\geq\sqrt{n}$时上界为$O(d^{2/3}n^{2/3})$。

Conclusion: 矩形矩阵乘法在分布式低带宽模型中展现出比方阵情形更丰富的复杂度景观，两种自然长宽比具有本质不同的行为特性，特别是$\langle n,d,n\rangle$类型在临界点$d=\sqrt{n}$处呈现相变。

Abstract: We study rectangular matrix multiplication in the low-bandwidth model of distributed computing. There are $n$ computers; initially the input matrices are distributed evenly between computers, and in each communication round every computer can send and receive an $O(\log n)$-bit message. Eventually each computer must output its designated part of the product matrix.
  While prior work has focused primarily on square $n \times n$ multiplication under various sparsity assumptions, we study rectangular instances with no sparsity assumption. We denote by $\langle a,b,c\rangle$ the task of multiplying an $a\times b$ matrix by a $b\times c$ matrix in this model. We concentrate on two natural aspect ratios, $\langle n,d,n\rangle$ and $\langle d,n,d\rangle$, for $d \le n$, and we study how the round complexity depends on $n$ and $d$.
  When $d \to n$, both $\langle n,d,n\rangle$ and $\langle d,n,d\rangle$ approach $\langle n,n,n\rangle$, which is the usual task of multiplying square matrices. If we consider multiplication over semirings, the current best upper bound in that case is $O(n^{4/3})$ rounds, and there is a trivial unconditional lower bound of $Ω(n)$.
  We show that for $\langle d,n,d\rangle$, we can achieve the complexity of $\tilde O(d^{4/3})$, which seems like a natural generalization of the upper bound $\tilde O(n^{4/3})$ when $d=n$. However, the case of $\langle n,d,n\rangle$ is fundamentally different, and also exhibits a phase transition. We show that for $d \le \sqrt{n}$, the complexity of $\langle n,d,n\rangle$ is $Θ(d \sqrt{n})$; we have matching upper and lower bounds. However, the behavior is genuinely different in the region $d \ge \sqrt{n}$, where the upper bound is $O(d^{2/3} n^{2/3})$.

</details>


### [209] [Clownfish: Scaling DAG-based BFT Consensus via Sparse Edges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04687)
*Feifan Wang,Jingfan Yu,Zixi Cai,Zhixuan Fang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Clownfish，一种部分同步的DAG-based BFT协议，通过选择性减少DAG顶点中的边数来降低通信复杂度，从而解决现有协议的可扩展性瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于有向无环图(DAG)的BFT协议虽然在实践中能够实现高吞吐量，但其高通信复杂度限制了可扩展性。这主要源于DAG中的每个顶点必须包含线性数量的边（引用），指向前一轮的顶点。

Method: 提出Clownfish协议，采用选择性减少DAG顶点中边数的方法来降低通信复杂度。使用通信最优的一致性广播时，每轮达到二次总通信复杂度。同时优化了轮次推进规则以减少故障情况下的额外延迟，并支持每轮多领导者机制来降低平均延迟。

Result: 在使用通信最优的一致性广播时，Clownfish每轮达到二次总通信复杂度，优于先前的DAG-based协议。实验评估表明，相比现有DAG-based协议，Clownfish提供了显著更好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: Clownfish成功解决了DAG-based BFT协议的可扩展性瓶颈问题，在保持较低通信复杂度的同时，还能通过多领导者机制降低平均延迟，为高吞吐量共识协议提供了更优的可扩展性解决方案。

Abstract: Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) based BFT protocols have demonstrated the capability to achieve significantly high throughput in practice. Recent advancements focused on minimizing the good-case latency of these protocols, approaching the theoretical lower bound. However, the high communication complexity inherent in existing DAG-based protocols limits their scalability. This primarily arises because each vertex in the DAG must include a linear number of edges (references) to vertices from previous rounds.
  We present Clownfish, a partially synchronous DAG-based BFT protocol designed to address the scalability bottleneck. Clownfish achieves lower communication complexity by selectively reducing the number of edges in DAG vertices. When using a communication-optimal consistent broadcast, Clownfish attains quadratic total communication complexity per round, outperforming prior DAG-based protocols. Clownfish also reduces the additional latency in failure cases by optimizing the round advancement rule. Additionally, Clownfish supports multiple leaders per round to reduce average latency while maintaining its lower communication complexity. Our experimental evaluation demonstrates that Clownfish provides significantly better scalability than existing DAG-based protocols.

</details>


### [210] [The local complexity of certifying parity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04934)
*Nicolas Bousquet,Laurent Feuilloley,Jorge Valenzuela,Sébastien Zeitoun*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了网络规模奇偶性局部认证问题，发现在不同模型下其复杂度差异巨大：一般图中验证半径为2时仅需常数比特，匿名图中验证半径为1时需要Ω(log log* n)比特，而有界扩张图类中又可实现常数规模认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络规模奇偶性是最简单的全局属性之一，在局部认证中具有有趣的双重性：在环图中等价于2-着色问题，仅需1比特即可认证；但在一般图中既无已知的非平凡下界，已知上界又需要认证精确的节点数n。问题的特殊性使得所有已知的下界方法都失效，因此需要深入研究这一基础问题。

Method: 作者引入了多个新工具：1）通过隐式使用节点ID和无冲突着色，实现用常数比特编码每个节点的父节点；2）开发了新的下界技术，结合复杂拓扑和高阶Ramsey型论证。研究在不同模型（带标识符的一般图、匿名图）和图结构（有界扩张图类如度有界图、平面图）下进行分析。

Result: 发现奇偶性认证在不同模型下呈现惊人差异：1）带标识符的一般图中，验证半径为2时可用常数比特认证；2）匿名图中验证半径为1时需要Ω(log log* n)比特；3）有界扩张图类（如度有界图和平面图）中，在相同受限模型下可设计常数规模认证。

Conclusion: 网络奇偶性的局部认证复杂度高度依赖于计算模型和图结构，不存在统一的答案。作者开发的新工具（常数比特父节点编码技术、新下界方法）为解决其他局部认证问题提供了有价值的技术手段，揭示了该领域问题的微妙性和多样性。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the problem of locally certifying that the size of a network is even, or more generally, congruent to some fixed number. The parity property is one of the simplest global properties, and it plays an intriguing role in local certification. On the one hand, it is one of the simplest properties in cycles because it is equivalent to 2-colorability, and hence can be certified with a single bit. On the other hand, in general graphs, no non-trivial lower bound on the size of the certificates is known, and the known upper bound basically consists in certifying the \emph{exact} value of $n$. In addition, the nature of the problem makes all the known lower bound approaches fail.
  We uncover a surprising landscape for parity across different models and graph structures:
  * In general graphs equipped with identifiers, when allowing verification radius 2, parity can be certified with a constant number of bits.
  * But in the model of anonymous graphs and allowing verification radius only 1, parity requires $Ω(\log \log^*n)$ bits.
  * Finally, in bounded expansion graph classes (such as bounded-degree graphs and planar graphs), the lower bound does not apply: in the same restricted model we can design a constant-size certification.
  We introduce several new tools that we expect to be useful in other contexts, in particular ways to \emph{encode a parent at each node with a constant number of bits} (via implicit use of the IDs and conflict-free colorings) and a new lower bound technique, with complex topologies and higher-order Ramsey-type arguments.

</details>


### [211] [Graph Traversal on Tensor Cores: A BFS Framework for Modern GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05081)
*Deniz Elbek,Kamer Kaya*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了BLEST框架，通过将BFS重构为位级稀疏矩阵向量计算并利用Tensor Cores加速，在真实图数据上相比现有方法实现平均5.9×-22.0×的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代GPU拥有高吞吐量矩阵运算能力的Tensor Cores，但图算法由于其不规则和数据依赖的执行模式难以被加速。现有方法存在负载不均衡、内存效率低和同步开销大等问题。

Method: BLEST引入二值化虚拟切片集(BVSS)图表示方法，将工作划分为均衡的warp级单元并仅调度前沿相关区域；采用优化的TC布局将邻居检查映射到二值MMA指令，减少8×的MMA调用次数；使用惰性顶点更新方案缓解原子操作和缓存瓶颈；提出动态切换机制在TC和CUDA cores之间转换；扩展到多源BFS和接近中心性工作负载；引入可扩展的图重排序方法。

Result: 在真实世界图数据集上，BLEST相比GAP、Gunrock、GSWITCH和BerryBees分别实现平均22.0×、7.7×、8.1×和5.9×的加速。使用100个H100 GPU可以在一小时内计算包含3.6B条边的社交网络中65.6M个顶点的精确接近中心性。

Conclusion: BLEST建立了GPU上新的BFS基准，证明了通过适当的算法重构和优化技术，可以利用Tensor Cores有效加速图算法，为图计算领域提供了新的性能标准。

Abstract: Modern GPUs have Tensor Cores (TCs) capable of extremely high-throughput matrix operations, yet graph algorithms remain difficult to accelerate because of their irregular and data-dependent execution patterns. This work presents BLEST, a TC-accelerated framework that reformulates Breadth-First Search (BFS) as a bit-level sparse matrix-vector computation while addressing the load imbalance, memory inefficiency, and synchronization overheads that limit prior approaches. BLEST introduces Binarized Virtual Slice Sets (BVSS), a graph representation that partitions work into balanced warp-level units and schedules only frontier-relevant regions of the graph. It further employs an optimized TC layout that maps neighbour checks onto binary MMA instructions without wasted outputs, reducing the number of required MMA calls by 8$\times$ compared with prior layouts. To mitigate atomic and cache bottlenecks, BLEST incorporates a lazy vertex-update scheme. We revisit the switching terminology for BFS and propose a mechanism that dynamically transitions from TCs to CUDA cores when it becomes more efficient. We also extend BLEST to multi-source BFS and closeness centrality workloads. Finally, we introduce a scalable graph reordering method that improves compression for scale-free-like graphs, while using RCM to improve locality for others. Across a broad set of real-world graphs, BLEST achieves average speedups of 22.0$\times$, 7.7$\times$, 8.1$\times$, and 5.9$\times$ over GAP, Gunrock, GSWITCH, and BerryBees, respectively, establishing a new BFS baseline on GPUs. Thanks to its high performance, BLEST can compute the exact closeness centralities of 65.6M vertices in a social network with 3.6B edges in an hour using 100 H100 GPUs.

</details>
